### [1] [A Composite Rest-Frame UV Spectrum of Galaxies at $5.7 < z < 6.9$: Ionization, Outflows, and Implications for Intergalactic Metal Enrichment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33154)
*David DePalma,Robert A. Simcoe,Anna-Christina Eilers,Rongmon Bordoloi,Daichi Kashino,Gauri Kotiwale,Jorryt Matthee*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过堆叠104个z≈6.5的[O III]发射星系的JWST/NIRSpec光谱，研究其ISM与恒星风的发射/吸收特征，发现存在~200 km/s的外流，但该外流尺度不足以解释类星体光谱中观测到的重元素吸收体频率，暗示低质量星系（M⋆∼10⁵–10⁶ M⊙）可能是早期星系际介质金属增丰的主要贡献者。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解释高红移类星体光谱中频繁出现的重元素吸收体的起源，检验z~6.5星系外流能否将金属输送到足够远的距离以产生这些吸收体。

Method: 对104个z=6.50、M_UV=−20.7、M_★=10^8.93 M⊙的[O III]发射星系进行JWST/NIRSpec光谱堆叠，分析Lyα、高电离发射线（O III]、C III]、He II）及多种吸收线的强度、宽度与速度偏移，并构建简单外流传播模型。

Result: 观测到Lyα和多条吸收线蓝移~200 km/s，证实存在外流；高电离发射线较强但线宽窄，不支持宽线AGN电离；外流传播模型表明该样本星系外流仅能到达3–9 kpc，不足以解释观测到的重元素吸收体丰度。

Conclusion: z~6.5已探测到的[O III]发射星系无法单独解释类星体光谱中的重元素吸收体频率；早期星系际介质的金属增丰更可能源自尚未直接探测到的低质量星系（M⋆∼10⁵–10⁶ M⊙），它们可能成团分布在JWST已探测的大质量星系周围。

Abstract: We present a stacked JWST/NIRSpec spectrum of 104 galaxies at $λ_{\rm rest}=1200\unicode{x2013}2000\;\mathrm{\mathring{A}}$ to study nebular emission and absorption from the interstellar medium (ISM) and stellar winds. The sample is drawn from known [O III] emitters with mean redshift $z=6.50$, absolute UV magnitude $M_\mathrm{UV}=-20.7$, and stellar mass $M_{\star}=10^{8.93}\;\mathrm{M_\odot}$. The stacked continuum closely resembles lower-redshift Lyman Break Galaxy composites, with a few notable differences. Ly$\;α$ has comparable strength and is redshifted relative to the [O III] systemic frame, consistent with production in gaseous outflows. High-ionization emission lines including O III], C III], and He II are slightly stronger than in lower-redshift stacks but exhibit narrow widths, disfavoring ionization from a broad-line AGN. Emission-line ratios slightly favor ionization by a hard stellar continuum, though a contribution from a narrow-line nuclear source cannot be strictly excluded. Numerous strong absorption lines at both high and low ionization are blueshifted by $\sim200\;\mathrm{km\,s^{-1}}$, again indicating outflows driven by stellar winds or entrained metal-enriched ISM. A simple model suggests that at $z\sim6.5$ most star-forming galaxies are too young for winds at these velocities to propagate beyond $\sim3\unicode{x2013}9$ proper kpc, implying that the observed [O III] emitter population alone cannot explain the frequency of heavy-element absorbers seen in quasar spectra. In this picture, early enrichment of the intergalactic medium likely arises from undetected low-mass galaxies ($M_\star\sim10^5\unicode{x2013}10^6\;\mathrm{M_\odot}$) clustered around the more massive systems detected in JWST/NIRCam WFSS surveys.

</details>


### [2] [$El\ Ascua$: An ultra-luminous, radio-loud, Compton-thick AGN at $z\sim 3.8$ hiding in plain sight revealed by MINERVA multi-band MIRI observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33155)
*Aidan P. Cloonan,Edgar P. Vidal,Alexandra Pope,Anna Sajina,Nicholas S. Martis,Yoshihisa Asada,Olivia R. Cooper,Adam Muzzin,Stacey Alberts,Jacqueline Antwi-Danso,Gabriel Brammer,Sam E. Cutler,Kartheik G. Iyer,Ivo Labbé,Danilo Marchesini,Tim B. Miller,Ikki Mitsuhashi,Luke Robbins,Ghassan T. E. Sarrouh,Monu Sharma,Mauro Stefanon,Katherine A. Suess,Katherine E. Whitaker,Chris J. Willott,Pratika Dayal,Anna de Graaff,Ben Forrest,Karl Glazebrook,Jenny E. Greene,Kelsey Hargraves,William W. H. Jarvis,Daniel J. Lyon,Ian McConachie,Gaël Noirot,Veronica Pratt,Bingjie Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用MINERVA巡天的多波段MIRI测光数据，在UDS场发现了一个红移z~3.8、极度中红外明亮的康普顿厚AGN（el Ascua），结合ALMA和VLA观测及紫外-射电能谱建模，确认其为一个罕见的射电噪康普顿厚AGN，并揭示其处于黑洞剧烈吸积但尚未被反馈清除尘埃遮蔽的关键阶段。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解超大质量黑洞在尘埃严重遮蔽下的增长过程，弥补当前黑洞普查中缺失的此类源。

Method: 基于MINERVA巡天的多波段MIRI中红外测光，结合ALMA（870 μm）和VLA（1.4 GHz）探测，开展紫外至射电波段的能谱建模，并通过X射线与中红外光度比值判断康普顿厚性质，以及IR-射电比分析射电过量。

Result: 发现z~3.8的极亮CT AGN el Ascua；测得宿主星系恒星质量log(M*/M⊙)=10.82±0.57、SFR=157±8 M⊙/yr；AGN本征吸积光度log(L_acc/erg s⁻¹)=47.12±0.58；确认CT遮蔽并发现射电过量，暗示其为罕见射电噪CT AGN；AGN吸积率与SFR比极高，表明处于深度遮蔽的黑洞增长峰值阶段。

Conclusion: MIRI具备高效发现以往遗漏的严重尘埃遮蔽AGN的能力，el Ascua代表了超大质量黑洞在反馈启动前的关键演化相位。

Abstract: Heavily dust-obscured active galactic nuclei (AGN) are crucial for understanding supermassive black hole growth. Using multi-band MIRI photometry from the MINERVA survey, we report the discovery in the UDS field of an extremely mid-infrared bright Compton-thick AGN (CT AGN) at $z \sim 3.8$, \textit{el Ascua} (the Ember). The AGN and/or its host galaxy is detected at 870\,$μ$m with ALMA and at 1.4\,GHz with the VLA. With UV-to-radio spectral energy distribution modeling, we estimate that the dusty host galaxy has a stellar mass of $\log\left( M_\star / M_\odot \right)=10.82 \pm 0.57$ and a star formation rate of ${\rm{SFR}} =157 \pm 8 \,M_\odot\,{\rm yr^{-1}}$, and we confirm the presence of a powerful dusty AGN with an intrinsic accretion luminosity of $\log\left( L_{\rm{acc}} /{\rm erg\,s^{-1}} \right)=47.12 \pm 0.58$. CT obscuration is revealed by comparing the upper limit on the X-ray luminosity over $2-10 \,{\rm{keV}}$ with the $6 \,{\rmμ{m}}$ mid-IR AGN luminosity. We additionally find evidence for a radio excess by comparing its total IR-to-radio ratio to those of typical star-forming galaxies and radio-quiet AGN, implying that \textit{el Ascua} could be a rare radio-loud CT AGN. With an extremely high ratio between black hole accretion rate and SFR, the AGN is likely nearing the peak of a deeply obscured phase of black hole growth before AGN feedback blows away the obscuring material. Our work demonstrates the power of MIRI to reveal heavily obscured AGN missing from our supermassive black hole census.

</details>


### [3] [Dark Matter and MOND successes and degeneracies in reproducing SPARC rotation curves and weak lensing data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33157)
*Amy Smith,Francesco Shankar,Daeun Jeong,Nicola Menci,Andrea Lapi,Hao Fu,Xinyu Dai,Mariangela Bernardi,Arianna Di Cintio,Annagrazia Puglisi,Alexander Belyaev,Hendrik Ulbricht,Ivette Fuentes-Guridi*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文通过拟合147个晚型星系的旋转曲线，并结合弱引力透镜数据，比较了冷暗物质（含/不含重子反馈）、温暗物质、模糊暗物质及MOND等模型对星系动力学的解释能力；结果表明MOND在百千秒差距至兆秒差距尺度上更优地再现了平均旋转曲线的平坦性，而多数暗物质模型需引入外部质量才能匹配观测，未来巡天数据将提供更严格检验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 星系外围观测到的旋转速度高于仅由重子物质预测的速度，从而引出暗物质或MOND等替代理论的需求。

Method: 使用SPARC数据库中147个晚型星系的旋转曲线，拟合多种暗物质模型（CDM含/不含重子反馈、WDM、Fuzzy DM）和MOND；并结合弱引力透镜数据将平均旋转曲线拓展至约1 Mpc。

Result: 在星系个体层面，含重子反馈的CDM模型略优；但考虑距离与倾角不确定性后，各模型间退化加剧；所有DM模型预测的晕质量高于丰度匹配预期，仅含反馈的CDM可减小光度-晕质量关系的弥散；MOND能在≤1 Mpc尺度上自然再现旋转曲线平坦性且恒星质光比在观测误差内，而纯DM模型（无外部质量）难以做到；弱透镜数据倾向支持MOND。

Conclusion: MOND在大尺度（至1 Mpc）上对平均旋转曲线的解释优于当前主流暗物质模型，尤其在结合弱透镜数据后；未来BIG-SPARC、SKA、EUCLID和Rubin等观测将提供更独立、严格的理论甄别。

Abstract: The observed rotation velocities of spiral galaxies at large scales exceed those predicted by their baryonic components, motivating the need for dark matter (DM) or Modified Newtonian Dynamics (MOND). We fit the rotation curves of 147 late-type galaxies from the SPARC database, using a suite of models including Cold Dark Matter (CDM) models with and without baryonic feedback, Warm DM, Fuzzy DM, and MOND. We also combine SPARC kinematics with weak-lensing data to extend the mean rotation curves to ~1 Mpc. On a galaxy-by-galaxy basis, the inner SPARC rotation curves marginally favour CDM models with baryonic feedback, although accounting for the uncertainties in distances and inclinations increases the degeneracy among DM-based models and MOND. All DM models predict host halo masses larger than abundance-matching expectations, with only the CDM model with baryonic feedback reducing the scatter around the mean luminosity-halo mass relation. MOND is able to reproduce the flatness of the mean galactic rotation curves on scales up to 1 Mpc, providing stellar mass-to-light ratios within observational uncertainties. In contrast, all DM models, without the contribution of mass external to the bound halo, struggle to explain the flat rotation curves. The inclusion of the current weak-lensing data tends to favour MOND over DM models, at least at the largest scales. Future observations from BIG-SPARC, the Square Kilometre Array, and most importantly, upcoming weak-lensing surveys such as EUCLID and Rubin will provide more stringent and independent tests of these competing frameworks

</details>


### [4] [Assessing the detectability of a realistic massive black hole binary population in LSST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33159)
*Lorenzo Bertassi,Beatrice Sala,Edyn Davies,Fabio Rigamonti,Maria Charisi,Riccardo Buscicchio,Paola Severgnini,Massimo Dotti*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文评估了一种用于检测具有任意形状周期性的新算法在模拟LSST数据中的性能，重点研究了大质量黑洞双星（MBHB）在类星体光变曲线中的电磁信号，并分析了不同检测阈值和波段对周期分布及探测率的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着LSST巡天的开展，有望在未来十年内探测到大质量黑洞双星（MBHB）在类星体光变曲线中引起的周期性调制；但需发展能处理任意波形周期信号并适应类星体红噪声的高效算法。

Method: 采用一种新提出的、适用于任意形状周期信号的灵活检测算法，在模拟的10年LSST-like数据上进行测试；考虑物理驱动的双星种群模型，并研究不同周期先验假设及两种检测阈值设定方法（基于后验几率比和控制误报率）的影响。

Result: 检测到的周期分布峰值集中在5–10年之间（依赖LSST波段）；探测到的双星比例在~20%至~3%之间变化，取决于检测阈值和观测波段；观测周期分布峰值受真实双星周期分布影响。

Conclusion: LSST中MBHB群体的探测可有效约束其种群性质，进而揭示其演化历史；所用算法在真实巡天数据中具备实用潜力。

Abstract: Electromagnetic signatures of massive black hole binaries (MBHB) are expected to be found in quasar light curves. Specifically, MBHB light curves are expected to show periodic modulations which may originate from different physical processes. The detection of such periodicities requires algorithms suitable to model modulations with arbitrary shapes embedded in quasar red noise, in massive datasets. With the advent of the Vera C. Rubin Observatory Legacy Survey of Space and Time (LSST), such sources may finally be within reach in the next decade. In this work, we assess the performance of a recently proposed flexible detection algorithm for periodicities of arbitrary shapes with simulated LSST-like data that span its 10-year baseline. For the first time, we examine a physically motivated population of binaries and assess the effect of assumed priors on the period on the periodicity search. We study two methods to set the detection threshold: the first based on the posterior odds ratio, which assumes knowledge of the expected fraction of binaries in quasar light curves, and the second, which limits the detection of false positives. We find that the expected distribution of detected periods shows a clear peak between 5 and 10 years, depending on the specific LSST band considered. We recover a detected binary fraction ranging from ~ 20% to ~ 3% depending on the detection threshold assumed and the band analysed, with the peak of the observed period distribution depending on the underlying binary period distribution. Therefore, the detection of a population of binaries in LSST can constrain the binary population and shed light on their evolution.

</details>


### [5] [The Hosts and Mass Distribution Function of the Most Massive Black Holes: Implications for the nHz Gravitational Wave Background](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33162)
*Marc Postman,Tod R. Lauer,Daniel J. D'Orazio,Bellamy Bailey*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用星系中心核心半径r_γ和最亮团星系（BCG）样本，建立了z=0时超大质量黑洞（SMBH）的质量分布函数（MDF），发现M_BH与r_γ呈线性关系，并推导出高质黑洞（M_BH ≥ 7×10⁹ M⊙）主要存在于BCG中，其MDF可解释纳赫兹引力波背景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解z=0时最重超大质量黑洞（SMBH）的质量分布，及其与宿主星系（尤其是BCG）演化的关系，并检验其对纳赫兹引力波背景的贡献。

Method: 基于哈勃太空望远镜（HST）和深度透镜巡天（DLS）图像构建BCG核心半径分布函数（CDF）；建立M_BH–r_γ经验关系；结合CDF与该关系导出黑洞质量分布函数（MDF）；并与Liepold & Ma (2024)结果拼接以覆盖全质量范围。

Result: 获得M_BH ≈ 1.3×10¹⁰ (r_γ/kpc) M⊙关系（散射σ = 0.25±0.03 dex）；CDF在r_γ > 0.5 kpc时呈N(r_γ) ∝ r_γ⁻³；M_BH ≥ 7×10⁹ M⊙的黑洞MDF为log(dN/d(M_BH/10¹⁰M⊙)) = (−3.05±0.42)log(M_BH/10¹⁰M⊙) − 5.11；高质黑洞几乎全属BCG；r_γ与描述BCG并合增长的参数α相关。

Conclusion: z=0时存在显著数量的M_BH > 7×10⁹ M⊙ SMBH，主要集中于BCG；其MDF与较低质量段拼接后，足以解释当前观测到的纳赫兹引力波背景，支持大质量黑洞通过并合主导增长的图景。

Abstract: We use the core-radius, $r_γ,$ of a galaxy's central core along with a volume-limited sample of brightest cluster galaxies (BCG) to determine the $z=0$ mass distribution function of the most massive super-massive black holes (SMBH). We recover a $M_{BH}-r_γ$ relationship between the SMBH mass, $M_{BH},$ and $r_γ$ of form $M_{BH} \approx1.3\times10^{10}~(r_γ/{\mathrm{kpc}})~M_\odot,$ with $σ= 0.25\pm0.03$ dex scatter in $M_{BH}.$ We derive a core distribution function (CDF), based on HST and DLS images of the BCGs. The CDF falls steeply as $N(r_γ)\sim r_γ^{-3}$ for $r_γ>0.50$ kpc. Only $\sim5\%$ of BCGs have $r_γ>1$ kpc. Combining the CDF and $M_{BH} - r_γ$ relationship yields the BH mass distribution function (MDF). For $M_{BH} \geq 7\times10^{9}~\mathrm{M_\odot}$ (the mean $M_{BH}$ at $r_γ=0.50$ kpc) we derive: $\log\left(\frac{dN(M_{BH})}{d{M_{BH}/10^{10} M_\odot}}~[\rm Mpc^{-3}]\right)=(-3.05\pm0.42)~\log\left(\frac{M_{BH}}{10^{10}M_\odot}\right)~-~5.11\ (+0.41, -0.36)$. Cores with $r_γ\geq 0.50$ kpc are found almost exclusively in BCGs, implying that SMBHs with $M_{BH} \geq7\times10^{9}~\mathrm{M_\odot}$ may also be exclusive to BCGs. Core-radius correlates with $α,$ a parameter that characterizes the growth of BCG envelopes by mergers, suggesting that the most massive SMBHs grew in tandem with their BCG hosts. The BH MDF embodies a significant population of SMBH with $M_{BH} >7\times10^{9} M_\odot.$ When combined with the Liepold & Ma (2024) BH MDF to account for SMBH with $M_{BH} < 7\times10^{9} M_\odot,$ it can comfortably explain the nHz gravitational wave background.

</details>


### [6] [How Steep is your Dust? Direct Measurements of Nebular Attenuation Curves at Cosmic Noon with JWST](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33166)
*Gabriel Maheson,Sirio Belli,Sandro Tacchella,Letizia Bugiani,Joel Leja,Rebecca Davies,Minjung Park,Amir H. Khoram,Matteo Sapori,Caterina Liboni,Benjamin P. Collins*

Main category: astro-ph.GA

TL;DR: 本文利用JWST/NIRSpec光谱直接测量了24个宇宙正午（z=1.8–3.3）星系的电离气体消光曲线，发现其形状高度一致、接近Calzetti曲线；同时揭示了星云与恒星消光差异随质量增大而增大，且与倾角无关，挑战了传统两成分尘埃模型；进一步指出若未实测消光曲线而依赖文献曲线，将导致强线金属丰度诊断产生高达5σ的系统偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准确测定星系内在发射线流量需精确刻画电离气体（nebular）消光曲线，但该曲线在本地与高红移宇宙中均难以约束，存在显著不确定性。

Method: 基于Blue Jay巡天中24个z=1.8–3.3、质量范围9.3<log(M⋆/M⊙)<11.1的恒星形成星系的JWST/NIRSpec深度光谱，测量Hδ至Paβ多达8条氢复合线；结合Prospector SED拟合获得恒星消光；比较不同消光曲线对强线金属丰度诊断（如S32、S3、S23、S3O3）的影响。

Result: （1）电离气体消光曲线形状高度一致，与Calzetti曲线吻合；（2）>90%样本中Balmer与Paschen线给出的消光一致；（3）星云与恒星消光差随恒星质量增大而增大，且二者均与星系倾角无关；（4）使用文献消光曲线而非实测曲线会导致部分金属丰度诊断偏差达5σ，甚至超过流量测量本身的统计误差。

Conclusion: 电离气体消光曲线的形状仍被严重低估，直接测量至关重要；当前广泛采用的文献曲线可能引入不可忽略的系统误差，尤其在金属丰度等关键物理量推断中；需重新评估并约束该曲线以降低星系物理参数测量的系统不确定性。

Abstract: Tracing the shape of the nebular attenuation curve, which is crucial for accurately measuring intrinsic emission-line fluxes, has proven challenging both in the local universe and at high redshift. Here we present nebular dust attenuation curves measured directly for 24 star-forming galaxies ($9.3<\log(\rm M{\star}/M_\odot)<11.1$) at cosmic noon ($1.8<z<3.3$) from the Blue Jay survey. Using deep JWST/NIRSpec spectra, we measure up to eight hydrogen recombination lines from H$δ$ to Pa$β$ per galaxy and find little diversity in the curve shape, with most galaxies agreeing with the \citet{calzetti_dust_2000} attenuation curve. For over $90\%$ of the sample, the nebular reddening from the Balmer lines is consistent with that from the Paschen lines. Using \texttt{Prospector} spectral energy distribution (SED) fits, we obtain the stellar reddening and find that the difference between stellar and nebular reddening increases with stellar mass, and that each reddening measurement is independent of inclination, which disagrees with the two-component dust model. We then measure the impact of using the derived nebular attenuation curve or a literature curve when determining metallicity using the strong-line method, and find that for some diagnostics (e.g., S32, S3, S23, and S3O3), the difference can be as large as $5σ$. Caution is needed when using these diagnostics if the nebular attenuation curve cannot be directly measured, since the resulting uncertainty can exceed the measurement uncertainty on the emission-line fluxes even with high signal-to-noise Balmer decrement measurements. These results show that the shape of the nebular attenuation curve is poorly understood and that constraining it is important for reducing systematic uncertainty in galaxy measurements.

</details>


### [7] [Recently launched AGN-driven outflow in a lensed quiescent galaxy at Cosmic Noon](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33176)
*Letizia Bugiani,Andrew B. Newman,Sirio Belli,Cosimo Marconcini,Pietro Bergamini,Sai Gangula,Claudio Grillo,Andrea Bolamperti,Katherine E. Whitaker,Aliza Beverage,Richard S. Ellis,Amir H. Khoram,Allison Matthews,Marco Lombardi*

Thanks to the combination of lensing magnification with JWST's angular resolution, we are able to build spatially resolved maps of the ionized gas spatial distribution, kinematics, and ionization properties in this system. We detect ionized gas emission throughout the galaxy: the lack of residual star formation inferred from line-ratio diagnostic diagrams, together with the decoupling between the gas and stellar kinematics, suggest that the ionized gas is in a wind. We use the 3D gas kinematics modeling code \texttt{MOKA3D} to constrain the geometry and physical properties of the ionized gas wind. We find that the observations are consistent with a biconical wind driven by the low-luminosity AGN at the center of the galaxy, which is detected in X-rays. We show that typical unresolved observations would not detect the wind, suggesting that the incidence of such outflows may be higher than previously recognized. Simple geometric arguments show that the observed outflow must have been launched $\approx 0.45$~Myr ago from the time of observation. This evidence suggests that low-level AGN activity is an important mechanism for maintaining quiescence in massive galaxies at cosmic noon, potentially over cosmological timescales.

Abstract: Accretion onto supermassive black holes (BHs) from the ISM can occur via capture of cold gas 'clouds' (GMCs, clumps, or overdense structures). Unlike material in stellar tidal disruptions or X-ray binaries, or classical Bondi accretion, ISM clouds are strongly magnetized (plasma $β\equiv P_{\rm thermal}/P_{\rm magnetic} \ll 1$), with supersonic turbulence, and cool rapidly. We derive analytic scalings for the initial conditions of accretion disks formed by capture/disruption of such clouds, in three regimes: (1) low-mass BHs ($M_{\rm BH} \ll M_{\rm cl}$), where turbulent Bondi-Hoyle or hierarchical 'leapfrog' growth via sub-cloud encounters dominates; (2) comparable-mass encounters ($M_{\rm BH} \sim M_{\rm cl}$); and (3) massive BHs tidally disrupting small clouds ($M_{\rm BH} \gg M_{\rm cl}$). In each, the resulting disk is predicted to be initially toroidally-magnetically-dominated ($|B_φ| \gg |B_{z}|$, $β\ll 1$), geometrically thick ($H/R \sim 0.1-1$), and gravitationally stable ($Q \gtrsim 1$), with super-sonic, trans-Alfvenic turbulence. These conditions differ qualitatively from those assumed in classical accretion/$α$ disk theory, 'chaotic cold accretion', stellar tidal disruption, or Bondi-Hoyle accretion. At least initially, the toroidal field should be sustained by flux-freezing on timescales shorter than Parker or turbulent resistive losses. We examine how cloud mass spectra drive accretion rate distributions, duty cycles, and fueling rates, with integrated BH growth dominated by clouds of mass comparable to the BH. Given known cosmological evolution, we suggest a scenario linking (a) inefficient growth and wandering BHs in dwarf galaxies, (b) hyper-Eddington growth in little red dots, (c) peak quasar activity in massive gas-rich systems at cosmic noon, and (d) lower-luminosity AGN in gas-poor galaxies at late times.

Here we report simulations of supernova-driven winds using the GPU-accelerated code QUOKKA that reach a resolution of $Δx = 1$ pc, significantly higher resolution and simulation volume than previous work. At this resolution the winds are dominated by neutral mass, with cloud sizes, masses and column densities broadly similar to those of the HI clouds in the Milky Way's nuclear wind. Abstract: Robust chemodynamic mapping of the Milky Way requires accounting for the selection function of the survey used, the probability that a given star is included in the final catalog as a function of its observable properties. We derive the empirical selection function of the SDSS-V Galactic Genesis survey, the largest Milky Way Mapper program targeting red giant stars for Galactic structure studies. Treating the Galactic Genesis sample as a subsample of its 2MASS parent catalog, we model the selection function as a function of sky position, apparent H magnitude, and G-H color using a Bayesian binomial estimator, computing both a mean and maximum a posteriori (MAP) version. The resulting selection function reflects the survey's targeting strategy and observing history, including the transition from the pencil-beam SDSS-IV footprint to the fuller sky coverage of SDSS-V and the prioritization of the Magellanic Clouds. Using this selection function with the FIRE-2 Milky Way-mass simulations, we construct mock Galactic Genesis catalogs built on existing mock Gaia DR3 catalogs, injecting realistic photometric noise and applying the selection function via a per-star Bernoulli accept-reject procedure. We demonstrate the mock catalogs' utility with an example application to azimuthal metallicity variations in the disk. Furthermore, we release a Python package allowing users to recompute the selection function for their own samples and science cases. The package, selection function and mock catalogs will be updated and made available as the survey continues, extending the tools needed to reach the full scientific potential of SDSS-V Galactic Genesis.

Abstract: The Gaia astrometric mission has provided the first evidence for the bulk rotation of open clusters. While this rotation may arise from residual spin inherited from a cluster's parent molecular cloud, it is unclear whether this explanation always suffices, as angular momentum is carried away by escaping stars on the timescale of two-body relaxation. In particular, the recent observance of significant rotation ($v_{\rm rot} = 0.81 \pm 0.06$ km s$^{-1}$) in the dynamically old ($\approx 4$ Gyr) open cluster M67 motivates a study of another potential source of spin for Solar neighborhood clusters: tidal torquing from gravitational encounters with passing giant molecular clouds (GMCs). We derive an analytic estimate for the change in angular momentum of a cluster due to an impulsive interaction with a passing GMC, and verify this expectation by performing $N$-body simulations over a range of encounter parameters using REBOUND. For highly energetic encounters, significant angular momentum can be transferred, but the stars that receive the most angular momentum are ejected, leaving the cluster remnant with negligible net rotation. Even when considering a random walk of many GMC-cluster encounters, the integrated effects of these interactions cannot explain observations of dynamically old rotating open clusters. However, we find that within $\sim 5$ Myr post-encounter, some disrupted clusters could be confused as rotating clusters while their stars are still dispersing. Using the local density of GMCs, we estimate that up to $\approx 1\%$ of open clusters in the Milky Way may carry this faux-rotation signal.

Abstract: We investigate the two-particle correlation function in a finite volume. Then, we show that the imaginary-time correlation function in a finite volume approaches that of the infinite volume as $τ/L^2 \rightarrow 0$ while it is dominated by the lowest energy mode as $τ/L^2 \rightarrow \infty$, where $τ$ is the imaginary time, $L$ is the volume size. The main result is that the $a\to0$ limits differ between the Fisher metric of the long-distance modes, whose momenta are fixed in physical units, and the Fisher metric of the full $P(σ)$. In the former, the lengths of the infinitely many irrelevant directions that change the kinetic term shrink, relative to the mass-term direction, to order $aΛ\log(1/(aΛ))$ or smaller, and when several modes are combined, only the two directions $(Λ,m)$ of the continuum theory remain. In the latter, the kinetic-term directions shrink at the same rate as the mass-term direction, and the difference in the regularization remains in correlations at distances of the order of the lattice spacing. An exception is the deformation that only adds a contact term to the propagator: as $a\to0$ its length goes to zero in both metrics, and it becomes indistinguishable from a change of the coupling $1/g^2$ (a lattice version of Kutasov's result). The curvature of the coupling plane at fixed kinetic term diverges as $(1/2)\log^2(1/(aΛ))$, not as the correlation volume often assumed in thermodynamic geometry, and the critical point is infinitely far in Fisher distance from any point at finite $a$.

Here we start investigating to what extent can this behavior be mimicked by chiral Anderson models. In particular, we study 3-D chiral-orthogonal systems with logarithmic off-diagonal disorder where negative-power ``spikes'' have been observed [2]. Rather, this power $p_1$ vanishes and the spectrum features a very accurate power behavior with $p<0$ on an interval around $E = 1$ spreading increasingly closer to $E=0$ and $E = \infty$ with increasing $W$. We connect this to the fact that random hoppings $t$ in these quantum systems are drawn from a scale-invariant $1/t$ distribution regularized via $t \in [e^{-W/2}, e^{W/2}]$, and DOS with negative power can naturally arise inside that range. We show that $\lim_{W \to \infty} p(W, E)=-1$ for all energies accessible by our data. Our results imply that, to model the IR sector of the IR phase, the IR cutoff in chiral Anderson systems needs to be viewed as descending from the randomness strength $W$ rather than the lattice size $L$.

Abstract: Machine learning methods have recently gained increasing interest as possible means of solving critical slowing down in lattice field theory, with particular emphasis on sampling methods using field transformations such as normalizing flows, annealing/out-of-equilibrium methods, and diffusion models. The choice of sampling method can be made after training the diffusion model; the theoretical properties of SNFs and T-REX and their relative trade-offs are discussed. Numerical results are presented for scalar field theory and the O(3) non-linear sigma model, using a class of local-footprint energy models with efficient gradient and divergence calculations. Sampling from the trained models follows predicted exactness and scaling properties, and significantly improves over sampling methods without a learned component.

Abstract: Beyond math and code, Large language models (LLMs) are beginning to tackle problems that have traditionally required substantial training for theoretical physicists, but it remains unclear how far this capability extends and where it might break. As a first step toward pushing LLMs in high-energy theoretical physics, we introduce QFT-Bench, a benchmark of 75 graduate- to research-level problems requiring extensive reasoning and derivation across core Quantum Field Theory (QFT) subareas. Each question has a well-defined target and closed-form reference answer. Evaluation combines symbolic verification with LLM-based judge. Across 39 proprietary and open-source models, frontier models already demonstrate substantial capabilities on nontrivial QFT calculations, with the strongest models exceeding 95% accuracy. At the same time, clear gaps remain. Even strong models still make errors in calculations, conventions, and instruction following, while weaker models often fail at the level of physical concepts or derivations. We further monitor models' CoTs and show that current models exhibit nontrivial reasoning unfaithfulness, i.e., they could reach correct answers but the reasoning itself does not justify and logically support them. These results provide a quantitative assessment of current LLM capabilities in QFT and a first step toward broader evaluation in high-energy theoretical physics.

Abstract: We develop a fully relativistic extension of Eddington inversion to reconstruct the phase-space distribution function of dark matter from its density profile. These results demonstrate the importance of relativistic phase-space reconstruction for dark-matter phenomenology in strong gravitational fields.

We identify two simple theories that accommodate all three relations and in which the measured masses fix the flavor structure. Within this model, equilibria appear and disappear through Arnold $A_2$, $A_3$, and $A_4$ catastrophes as the particle number $N$ and interaction strengths vary. Abstract: The large hierarchy of fermion masses in the Standard Model has long posed a challenge to be explained. One approach is to try to invoke a symmetry principle to dictate the structure ("texture") of the matrices of fermion Yukawa couplings to the Higgs field. Here we invoke noninvertible symmetries, specifically, fusion algebras, to provide such textures. In particular, the structure of the fusion-algebra can be used to assign labels to each field, which determines how suppressed any given composite operator is as a term in the Lagrangian. In UV completions, this can be interpreted as specifying a hierarchy of operators that in the IR effective theory become the Yukawa couplings to the Higgs field. Remaining agnostic of a specific UV model, we perform a Monte Carlo numerical scan over unknown Wilson coefficients, and we identify the rank-8 fusion algebra $\rm{FR}^{8,6}_8$ as the one providing the best fit for the textures of the up-quark, down-quark and charged-lepton Yukawa matrices. We also study the predictions for the CKM and PMNS mixing matrices, with the conclusion that none of the fusion algebras we study provide an accurate fit for the masses and mixing angles simultaneously. The fusion algebra structure ensures that the textures that we find are stable under RG running and persist down to low energies.

The largest binding energies occur for $IJ=02$ and $IJ=00$ with values over a hundred MeV, while the $IJ=20$ state is the least bound and has a binding energy of only a few MeV. Moreover, the results of intercluster distances favor a molecular interpretation of these states. The interaction analysis reveals that the $ρρ$ and $ΔΔ$ interactions share certain similarities. Abstract: We developed a new theoretical approach in the description of partonic structure of hadrons based on spectral function representation of valence quarks. In this approach massless valence quarks are considered in the mean field of residual hadron. This approach initially was applied to the calculation of parton distribution function~(PDF) of nucleons that resulted in an observation of pion-cloud structure of nucleon. In this report we present our calculation of pion PDF in the valence region within the same approach. In this approach we describe the soft partonic structure of pion through the valence $q\bar q$ cluster, embedded in the residual field of the pion. The pion PDF is calculated using phenomenological Light-Front wave functions for the $q\bar q$ cluster and the residual field. Our result indicates that the peak position of the x weighted valence PDF~(xPDF) depends on parameters characterizing the virtuality of the $q\bar q$ cluster and the mass of the residual system. These parameters are evaluated by fitting the calculated PDF to the height and the peak position of empirical pion xPDF at starting $Q_0$. They indicate that unlike the nucleon case, very little residual mass is needed in the pion to describe existing pion PDFs in the valence region. They also require the $q\bar q$-cluster to be highly virtual with the interacting quark carrying almost all of the momentum of the $q\bar q$ cluster. This picture is consistent with the dominance of the Feynman mechanism leaving little room for the hard component in the PDF. The calculation practically describes the $\sim (1-x)$ behavior observed recently at $x\to 1$ limit. Our non-trivial observation is that the height and the peak position of xPDF define the analytic behavior of valence PDF at $x\to 1$ limit.

Abstract: We present mass modeling of three strong lensing galaxy clusters found in the Dark Energy Survey utilizing Hubble Space Telescope imaging for lens modeling, and complementary constraints from Chandra X-ray observations. All three clusters are relaxed and have giant arc or ring lens structures ideal for constraints on the dark matter distribution. The strong lens modeling and X-ray temperatures yield consistent total cluster mass estimates. We find these clusters have best-fit X-ray temperatures of $2.7 - 6.2$ keV and total masses of M$_{200} = (1-6) \times 10^{14} M_{\odot}$. Our lens models parameterize dark matter distribution with a generalized Navarro-Frenk-White (GNFW) density profile. While the GNFW profile allows the inner dark matter slope to vary, we find in all cases that the inner slopes are consistent with the standard NFW density profile $γ^{DM} = 1$. The X-ray surface brightness distributions for all three clusters are generally symmetric with no significant substructure, supporting the conclusion that these are relaxed clusters. Abstract: Emission line stacking measurement for cosmological line intensity mapping (LIM) contains not only the average emission line profile of the stacked sources, but usually also the clustering signal, due to the limited angular resolution of the observations. We provide an explicit derivation of the equivalence between the emission line stacking, the two-point cross-correlation function in configuration space and the power spectrum in Fourier space, similar to the case between the angular stacking signal for the kinematic Sunyaev-Zel'dovich effect and the angular power spectrum in harmonic space (arXiv:2512.14625, arXiv:2605.15947). Using the quadratic estimator formalism, we establish that the emission line stacking signal measurement is equivalent to a quadratic estimator for the cross-correlation function. The formalism allows for the explicit modelling of the stacked signal, including the clustering statistics as well as observational effects such as the galaxy selection function and the LIM survey footprint. In particular, using mock HI intensity mapping data, we show that we can accurately model the effects of foreground removal on the stacked signal. The correlation function estimator provides significant speed-up for the stacking measurement and enables modelling of the signal and its covariance for large LIM datasets with MeerKLASS and future SKAO.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现代宇宙学依赖大量高保真度、计算昂贵的模拟（如N体或流体动力学模拟），但其高昂计算成本限制了大规模应用；亟需降低SBI对高精度模拟的依赖。

Result: 相比仅用CAMELS训练，预训练模型达到相同后验精度所需高保真模拟数量减少至5–15%；总CPU计算成本降低达一个数量级；即使FastPM不含重子物理，对全流体模拟功率谱的推断仍获得约5倍计算节省；更精确的FastPM预训练可超越纯CAMELS训练的最优性能。

Conclusion: 迁移学习是实现高保真SBI的高效可行路径，凸显了‘基于模拟的基础模型’在宇宙学中的巨大潜力。

Abstract: Modern cosmology increasingly relies on large ensembles of expensive simulations to model the non-linear Universe and extract information from upcoming surveys. However, generating high-fidelity simulations---whether gravity-only N-body or full hydrodynamical---requires substantial computational resources. In this work, we demonstrate that transfer learning from fast low-fidelity simulations can dramatically reduce the cost of training machine learning models, in particular for simulation-based inference (SBI). We train a Fast Pretrained Model (FastPM) on a set of Fast Particle-Mesh (FastPM) simulations to infer $Ω_m$ and $σ_8$ from the power spectrum, and finetune on a smaller set of high-fidelity CAMELS N-body and hydrodynamical simulations. To reach a fixed posterior accuracy, the pretrained model requires only 5--15\% of the high-fidelity simulations compared to the number of simulations needed when training on CAMELS alone, reducing the total training simulation compute cost, including that of the pretraining simulations, by up to an order of magnitude in CPU hours. Remarkably, the gains persist for the total matter power spectrum of full hydrodynamical simulations: although FastPM contains no baryonic physics, pretraining on FastPM runs that together cost less than a single hydrodynamical simulation still reduces the total training simulation compute by a factor of $\sim$5. Moreover, pretraining on more accurate FastPM runs yields more accurate posteriors than training on CAMELS alone achieves with any number of simulations we consider. These results demonstrate that transfer learning from fast simulations is a simple and powerful route to high-fidelity SBI, and highlight the promise of simulation-based foundation models in cosmology.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解局部环境（包括密度和宇宙网类型）如何影响暗物质晕的形成与空间分布，以及晕的组装偏差是否具有方向性。

Method: 基于ELUCID数值模拟，定义局部密度与宇宙网类型来刻画环境；引入三维宇宙网交叉相关函数（3D-WCCF）并在潮汐本征坐标系中分析晕的空间分布与各向异性。

Result: 1) 晕质量函数在固定宇宙网类型下强依赖于局部密度，在固定密度下弱但显著依赖于宇宙网类型；2) 晕形成红移主要由局部密度决定；3) 3D-WCCF显示晕在e3方向（最弱压缩轴）相关性最强，在e1方向（最强压缩轴）最弱；4) 大质量晕多位于节点且偏好垂直于e1的平面；5) 早期形成晕比晚期的更强烈关联宇宙网，且该组装偏差在丝状、片状和空洞周围呈e3方向主导的各向异性，而在节点周围近似各向同性。

Conclusion: 局部密度是主导暗物质晕统计性质和形成历史的关键环境变量，宇宙网类型则调制其各向异性分布；3D-WCCF为定量刻画晕-宇宙网关系及其组装偏差提供了有效工具。

Abstract: We use the ELUCID simulation to study how the local environment affects the formation of dark matter halos and their spatial distribution around the cosmic web. We describe the environment by local density and cosmic-web type. At fixed web type, the halo mass function (HMF) depends strongly on the local density, whereas at fixed density it depends only weakly, though significantly, on the web type; the character of both dependencies changes around the mean density. The halo formation redshift is primarily driven by local density rather than cosmic web. We then introduce the three-dimensional cosmic-web cross-correlation function (3D-WCCF), which expresses the distribution of halos around cosmic web in the local tidal eigenframe. The 3D-WCCF provides numerous anisotropic characteristics. Around all web types, halos are most strongly correlated along the axis of weakest compression, $\mathbf{e}_3$, and least along the axis of strongest compression, $\mathbf{e}_1$. Nearly all cluster-sized halos reside in knots and lie preferentially in the plane perpendicular to ${\bf e}_1$. The 3D-WCCF also provides important information on the geometry and size of filaments, sheets and voids. Moreover, early-forming halos are more strongly correlated with the web than late-forming ones, and this assembly bias is anisotropic around filaments, sheets, and voids, being strongest along $\mathbf{e}_3$, but nearly isotropic around knots.

</details>

Method: 通过分析Gallardo等人模板构建中的数学错误（混淆点态力律的平均与平均后的幂次变换），利用高斯线性场解析推导、高斯模拟及峰选择示踪体数值验证，重构正确的成对SZ信号模板，并重新拟合原始数据。

Conclusion: Gallardo等人的n=1排除结果源于数学处理错误，而非观测证据；该工作未触及自洽MOND宇宙学对此观测量的预测，相关问题仍待研究。

Abstract: Gallardo et al. [Phys. Rev. Lett. \textbf{136}, 151002 (2026)] constrain the index of the force law $g\propto r^{-n}$ with pairwise kinematic Sunyaev--Zeldovich measurements, $n=2.1\pm0.3$, and exclude the deep-MOND value $n=1$ at $3.3σ$. The exclusion does not follow from their calculation. Their templates for $n\neq2$ [their Eq.~(8)] raise the pair-averaged Newtonian acceleration to the power $n/2$, whereas the pair average of the pointwise law [their Eq.~(30) for deep MOND] is the average of the transformed field; the step that identifies the two attributes to the three-point function a correction that is of second order and not small. In a Gaussian linear field with linearly biased tracers, Gaussian integration by parts shows that the correctly averaged template is exactly proportional to the Newtonian one, with a constant that the analysis absorbs into the mean optical depth. Gaussian realizations with peak-selected tracers find the same proportionality to within 2\% (0.5\% for the fiducial selection), and refitting the data of the Letter with the resulting template gives $χ^2=20.1$--$20.2$, against $20.1$ for the Newtonian template and $33.8$ for Eq.~(8): in every controlled case examined the data do not discriminate between $n=1$ and $n=2$, and the $3.3σ$ exclusion, with the $10σ$ forecast that rescales it, rests on the order in which the average and the power were taken. What a self-consistent MOND cosmology predicts for this observable is a separate question, which the Letter does not address.

</details>

Motivation: 近期DESI的BAO数据联合CMB和SNe数据倾向于动态暗能量（DDE）模型而非标准ΛCDM模型；本文旨在检验遗迹中微子的质量与简并度是否与DDE参数存在简并，从而也能解释该统计偏离。

Abstract: Recent Baryon Acoustic Oscillation (BAO) data from the Dark Energy Spectroscopic Instrument (DESI), fitted jointly with Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB) and Type Ia Supernovae (SNe) data, favor dynamical dark energy (DDE) over the standard $Λ$CDM model. In this work, we investigate whether the mass and degeneracy of relic neutrinos are degenerate with DDE parameters and can also account for this statistical deviation from $Λ$CDM. Using a Markov Chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) analysis of a joint Planck CMB temperature, DESI BAO, and Pantheon+ dataset, we show a robust parameter degeneracy between the DDE equation of state and neutrino physics. We supplement this with a semi-analytical framework that recovers the numerical correlation, showing that the tight constraint on the CMB acoustic scale drives this degeneracy. Our results highlight the clear correlation between DDE and neutrino parameters, underscoring the critical need to jointly constrain the neutrino sector and the dark sector in next-generation cosmological surveys.

</details>

Abstract: The Peanut galaxy cluster (SRGe CL0238.3+2005) offers a rare opportunity to investigate thermal and nonthermal processes only $\sim0.1$\,Gyr after pericentric passage. We present a multi-frequency radio study of this system at $z=0.42$ using new MeerKAT and uGMRT observations, complemented by Chandra and MUSTANG-2 data. We identify two morphologically and spectrally distinct radio components: a double-lobed source in the northern subcluster and extended diffuse emission across the cluster center. The double-lobed source has a strongly curved, ultra-steep spectrum and is offset by $\sim22$\,kpc from the nearest brightest cluster galaxy. Its spectral curvature and positional offset are consistent with remnant AGN plasma that may have been displaced during the merger or reshaped by a shock. The diffuse emission extends over $\sim1$~Mpc and is classified as an ultra-steep spectrum ($α=-1.77\pm0.11$ between 400\,MHz and 1.28\,GHz) radio halo. Its volume-averaged radio emissivity, $(1.1\pm0.1)\times10^{-41}$ erg s$^{-1}$ cm$^{-3}$ Hz$^{-1}$ at 1.4\,GHz, is an order of magnitude higher than the characteristic value for other halos. The radial profile of the halo is well described by a single exponential fit and its radio emission correlates tightly with the X-ray, with a best-fit slope of $1.11\pm0.05$. Chandra and MUSTANG-2 observations reveal candidate surface brightness discontinuities, with the most prominent inner feature coinciding with a region of flatter spectral index, potentially tracing localized particle (re)-acceleration. The coexistence of an extended ultra-steep spectrum radio halo, high radio emissivity, remnant AGN plasma, and candidate X-ray/SZ discontinuities makes the Peanut cluster an important test case for understanding the evolution of diffuse radio emission and its connection to the dynamical state of the intracluster medium in early-stage mergers.

### [85] [Calibration requirements for Epoch of Reionization 21-cm signal observations - V. Bias and variance in the cylindrical power spectrum from uGMRT Band 3 observations of the ELAIS-N1 field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35046)

Abstract: Detection of the redshifted 21-cm signal from neutral hydrogen requires precise control of systematic effects arising from instrumental calibration. In this work, we investigate the impact of both time- and frequency-dependent residual gain and bandpass errors on the estimation of the multifrequency angular power spectrum (MAPS) using radio interferometric observations. We discuss our previously developed analytical framework for estimating bias and excess variance in 21-cm signal MAPS in the presence of these residual errors and apply it to uGMRT Band-3 observations. The statistical properties of the residual gains are derived from the data themselves, including their temporal and spectral correlations. We introduce a robust technique for additionally flagging poor-quality data. Our results show that the excess variance induced by residual gain and bandpass errors dominates over the bias across the range of angular multipoles considered. The amplitude of this variance is comparable to the expected power spectrum upper limit at redshift $z \sim 2.2$, indicating that residual calibration errors can significantly affect the detectability of the 21-cm signal. We further transform the estimated MAPS into ($k_{\perp}, k_{ll}$) space and find the expected foreground wedge structure. Importantly, we observe a significant power leakage into the nominal EoR window, which we attribute to residual calibration systematics. This highlights that even after standard foreground mitigation, time- and frequency-correlated gain errors can introduce mode-mixing and contaminate the EoR window. Our results emphasise the necessity of improved calibration accuracy and robust characterisation of residual gain statistics for current and upcoming interferometric experiments such as the uGMRT and SKA, to enable reliable detection of the cosmological 21-cm signal.

</details>

Abstract: Cosmic voids provide a setting for examining the interplay between mass and environment in shaping galaxy and halo properties. Using the SDSS DR7 volume-limited galaxy sample and the ELUCID constrained simulation of the local large-scale structure, we investigate the radial profiles of galaxy and subhalo properties across different mass ranges, identifying voids independently in the observed galaxy and simulated subhalo distributions. Both stellar mass and subhalo mass increase from void interiors toward the void boundary, indicating that lower-mass galaxies and subhaloes preferentially reside in void interiors. The satellite fraction of subhaloes also increases systematically toward the void boundary. Galaxy colour and specific star formation rate exhibit radial variations that are weaker than their dependence on stellar mass, while the spiral fraction shows little radial dependence at fixed stellar mass. For dark-matter haloes, the apparent radial increase in size within a broad high-mass bin largely disappears when narrower mass ranges are considered. Halo concentration and spin show radial variations at fixed mass, with higher concentrations and lower spins toward void interiors, although the concentration trend is less pronounced in the extended low-mass sample. The formation redshift of low-mass subhaloes also increases toward the void boundary, whereas this dependence weakens and eventually disappears at higher masses. Overall, the radial trends in galaxy properties are generally weaker than their mass dependence, while several subhalo properties retain measurable radial variations at fixed mass.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决当前观测到的宇宙射电偶极与CMB运动学偶极之间不一致的问题（即‘宇宙偶极张力’），并探究潜在的系统误差来源。

Method: 采用Simulation-based Inference（SBI）框架，构建包含ASKAP天线温度响应的前向模型，用神经网络学习似然函数，从而精确估计RACS-low3和RACS-mid1数据中的偶极参数。

Result: RACS-low3偶极方向与CMB一致但振幅大4倍；RACS-mid1偶极方向偏移且振幅大5倍；同时确认通量密度受观测时天线温度显著抑制。

Conclusion: 观测到的强偶极信号难以仅由已知系统误差解释，支持‘宇宙偶极张力’真实存在；SBI方法在建模复杂仪器效应方面具有优势。

Abstract: We measure the dipole in the count of radio galaxies over the sky as seen by the Australian SKA Pathfinder (ASKAP) in the Rapid ASKAP Continuum Survey (RACS). Our analysis focuses on the third observational epoch in the 'low' passband (943.5 MHz; RACS-low3) and the first epoch in the 'mid' passband (1367.5 MHz; RACS-mid1). We find strong evidence for a systematic relation between flux density and the temperature at the time of observation, whereby reported flux densities are suppressed to a greater degree when the ASKAP antennas are warmer. We include this temperature response in our forward model as part of our Simulation-based Inference (SBI) pipeline, generating data realisations that are then used by a neural network to learn the likelihood function. Despite this model, RACS-low3 and RACS-mid1 both exhibit a dipole inconsistent with the kinematic dipole of the Cosmic Microwave Background (CMB), adding further evidence for the 'cosmic dipole tension'. For RACS-low3, the dipole agrees in direction but has an amplitude four times in excess, while the dipole for RACS-mid1 is offset from the CMB and has an amplitude roughly five times in excess. Our findings underscore the importance of interrogating the data for systematic effects and showcase the power of SBI in handling them.

</details>


### [88] [Model-independent calibration of gamma-ray bursts with Bayesian neural networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35503)

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文通过贝叶斯神经网络和多种统计诊断方法，对伽马暴（GRB）的Amati关系进行无模型宇宙学校准，构建了可达z≈9.4的GRB哈勃图，并与超新星、BAO等数据联合约束暗能量性质，结果支持GRB作为高红移距离探针的潜力，但未确证暗能量状态方程演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 伽马暴可拓展宇宙学测距至Ia型超新星覆盖范围之外，但其标准化需可靠校准与不确定性估计；本文旨在无先验宇宙学模型假设下校准Amati关系，评估其稳定性及宇宙学探测能力。

Method: 使用Pantheon+超新星训练贝叶斯神经网络并重标定预测不确定性；采用D'Agostini和Reichart似然在校准z≤1.5的GRB Amati关系；联合GRB哈勃图、超新星、H(z)和DESI BAO数据；通过局部回归、协方差、留三法、红移演化及Efron-Petrosian等多种诊断检验标准化假设；并模拟未来1500个标准化GRB的理想样本。

Result: GRB哈勃图可达z≈9.4；协方差与留三法诊断对宇宙学约束影响小；红移演化参数与零一致，但截断校正显示各向同性能量可能存在沿Amati关系的微弱演化；暗能量扩展模型比ΛCDM拟合更优：AIC支持CPL参数化，BIC支持常数w模型；未来样本在给定不确定性模型下具备强模型区分能力。

Conclusion: GRB可作为超新星距离阶梯在高红移端的互补延伸；当前结果尚不能确立暗能量状态方程的演化；更大且均匀的样本前景可观，但需重视真实选择函数与校准系统误差。

Abstract: Context. Gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) extend cosmological distance measurements beyond the range densely sampled by Type Ia supernovae, but their standardization requires reliable calibration and uncertainty estimates.
  Aims. We calibrate the Amati correlation without assuming a background cosmological model, assess its stability, and examine the cosmological reach of current and future GRB Hubble diagrams.
  Methods. We train a Bayesian neural network on Pantheon+ supernovae, assess and recalibrate its predictive uncertainties, and calibrate the correlation for GRBs at $z \leq 1.5$ using the D'Agostini and Reichart likelihoods. We combine the GRB Hubble diagram with supernovae, Hubble-parameter measurements, and DESI baryon acoustic oscillations. Local-regression, covariance, leave-three-out, redshift-evolution, and Efron-Petrosian diagnostics test the standardization assumptions. We also analyse idealized future catalogues of 1500 standardized GRBs.
  Results. The diagram reaches $z \simeq 9.4$. Covariance and leave-three-out diagnostics produce small changes in the cosmological constraints. A parametric evolution coefficient is consistent with zero, whereas the truncation-corrected diagnostic indicates marginal isotropic-energy evolution that can occur along the Amati relation. Dark-energy extensions improve the phenomenological fit relative to flat $Λ$CDM: AIC favours the Chevallier-Polarski-Linder parametrization, while BIC favours constant-$w$ dark energy. Forecasts show strong conditional model-discrimination power under the adopted uncertainty model.
  Conclusions. GRBs provide a complementary high-redshift extension of the supernova distance ladder. Present results do not establish an evolving dark-energy equation of state. Larger homogeneous samples are promising, but realistic selection functions and calibration systematics remain essential.

</details>


### [89] [pbhalo: computing primordial black hole and minihalo populations from a single primordial perturbation spectrum](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35703)
*B. A. Rudenko*

Main category: astro-ph.CO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了pbhalo，一个开源Python包，用于从给定的原初曲率功率谱计算原初黑洞（PBH）质量函数、晕质量函数和超紧凑晕（UCMH）丰度，并联合应用观测约束。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 小尺度上原初曲率功率谱的增强可产生两类致密天体：辐射主导时期形成的原初黑洞（PBHs）和物质主导时期形成的暗物质微晕（包括超紧凑微晕UCMHs）。需要统一工具来计算并联合施加观测约束。

Method: 开发了开源Python包pbhalo，整合PBH质量函数、晕质量函数和UCMH丰度的计算，并验证其与colossus、pbhstat及已有结果的一致性。

Result: pbhalo成功实现了对PBH、晕及UCMH的统一计算，并展示了对对数正态峰功率谱的联合观测约束。

Conclusion: pbhalo是一个可靠、开源且易用的工具，为研究原初扰动谱及其致密天体产物提供了标准化框架。

Abstract: An enhancement of the primordial curvature power spectrum on small scales can give rise to two populations of compact objects: primordial black holes (PBHs), which form during radiation domination, and dark matter minihalos, including ultracompact minihalos (UCMHs), which form from the same modes during matter domination. We present pbhalo, an open-source Python package that computes the PBH mass function, the halo mass function and the UCMH abundance from a single spectrum in a given cosmology and applies observational constraints on the PBH abundance and on the spectrum jointly. The package components are validated against colossus (a code for halo mass functions and cosmological quantities), pbhstat (a code for the PBH mass function from a given spectrum) and published results; joint constraints are illustrated for lognormal peaks. The code is distributed under the MIT license.

</details>

Abstract: We investigate a non-minimally coupled scalar field in non-metricity gravity, adopting a non-trivial connection class in spatially flat FLRW spacetimes. In contrast to the coincident-gauge choice, this connection class activates an additional dynamical degree of freedom (DoF) that modifies the background evolution. We study two representative models, defined by power-law and exponential coupling functions with the exponential potential for scalar field. Confronting both models with recent combined datasets - DESI BAO, two independent supernova compilations, and cosmic chronometers - we find that each reproduces an extended matter-dominated era followed by a stable late-time de-Sitter attractor, with inferred parameters consistent with the standard $Λ$CDM model. A complementary autonomous dynamical-system analysis identifies a sequence of critical points describing successive stiff-fluid, transient matter-dominated, and stable de-Sitter phases, ensuring a viable thermal history of the universe. The observational and dynamical results are mutually consistent and support the cosmological viability of the proposed models.

</details>

Result: 发现κ≫1时存在绝热高斯波包解：对应dS真空、经典振荡凝聚态（能量密度∝a⁻³）及中心处等效零点能密度（呈尘埃行为）；数值验证零点能定律精度达10⁻³，Hubble率量子修正按单元作用倒数收敛；背景演化逼近ΛCDM，DV偏差为1.3/κ；暗能量严格满足w=−1。

Conclusion: 该cuscuton启发的双曲势模型在强质量分离极限（κ≫1）下，无需拟合数据即可自洽实现真空+尘埃主导的背景宇宙学，为量子起源的暗能量与物质共存提供新机制。

Abstract: We quantize a spatially flat FLRW minisuperspace containing one canonical scalar field with the hyperbolic potential generated by a cuscuton-type superpotential, and extract cosmological histories from the Wheeler-DeWitt wave function in the de Broglie-Bohm interpretation. For the canonical potential parameter, related to the original cuscuton couplings, below an explicit critical value the potential has a unique nondegenerate minimum with positive height, and the ratio $κ$ of the scalar mass to the de Sitter Hubble rate at the minimum diverges as $κ^2\simeq 3b/δ$, where $δ$ is the relative distance to criticality. For the original hyperbolic potential, $κ\leq 3/\sqrt{2}$, whereas its cuscuton-inspired generalization allows a large hierarchy between scalar oscillation and expansion rates. For $κ\gg1$, small excitation and sufficiently large cell action, the reduced wave equation has an adiabatic Gaussian solution describing a de Sitter vacuum, a classical oscillating condensate whose averaged excitation density redshifts as $a^{-3}$, and, at the packet centre, a leading quantum density equal to the zero-point energy of the effective homogeneous oscillator divided by the physical cell volume, which is also dust-like. Full numerical solutions of the Wheeler-DeWitt equation support the averaged zero-point law at the reported $10^{-3}$ level, reproduce its dependence on the position of the Bohmian particle inside the packet, show that quantum corrections to the present Hubble rate converge as the inverse of the cell action, and support a single-field dust-plus-vacuum description at the homogeneous background level when $κ\gg1$. The classical histories approach the $Λ$CDM distance relations with a deviation of $1.3/κ$ in $D_V$. The dark energy component has $w=-1$ by construction, and the model is not fitted to data.

### [92] [Black holes from a nonlocal equation of state: horizon geometry, singularity structure and thermodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33222)

Abstract: We show that a nonlocal equation of state for an anisotropic fluid reduces to a linear equation for the enclosed matter and uniquely selects a Yukawa-type density profile in a static, spherically symmetric Kerr--Schild geometry. The resulting neutral solution has finite-range hair, exponentially screened at large distances, with the asymptotic mass as its only conserved charge, and admits an exact horizon classification: horizonless configurations, Schwarzschild-like black holes, two-horizon geometries with a Cauchy horizon, and an extremal configuration whose mass and radius are obtained in closed form. We prove that the magnitude of the inner surface gravity exceeds that of the outer horizon throughout the two-horizon regime. When the central mass vanishes, the geometry approaches a global monopole in the static branch and a Kantowski--Sachs-type region in the nonstatic branch. The singularity satisfies Królak's strong-curvature criterion and is Tipler-strong along non-radial geodesics in the spacelike branch. For vanishing central mass, the Ricci scalar and the square root of the Kretschmann scalar are locally integrable with respect to the invariant spacetime volume, despite diverging at the origin. A power-law generalization of the nonlocal closure yields solutions expressed through incomplete gamma functions, interpolating between the singular core and a regular black hole with a de~Sitter core, without invoking nonlinear electrodynamics. We obtain closed-form surface gravities, an extended first-law identity and a Smarr relation, and locate the heat-capacity divergence using the lower branch of the Lambert function. Finally, thermodynamic topology distinguishes a Schwarzschild-like regime with one defect from an extremal regime with two opposite-winding defects that merge at the maximum Hawking temperature.

</details>

TL;DR: 本文研究了由具有负指数s的幂律Maxwell场源驱动的旋转反德西特（AdS）黑弦的质量为零的标量扰动，分析了电荷和旋转对准正规模谱的影响，并发现电荷导致无振荡衰减、超几何谱及模式远离光锥的新标度律，而旋转引入纯阻尼轴对称模且不引发超辐射不稳定性。

Abstract: We study massless scalar perturbations of rotating anti-de Sitter black strings sourced by a power-Maxwell field with negative exponent $s$. The charge enters the metric function as $-Qr^{p}$ with $p=2/(1-2s)$, and the case $s=-1/2$ gives a linear term with an algebraic horizon. Since rotation is a local boost of the static string, the rotating spectrum follows exactly from the static one evaluated at a frequency-dependent momentum. We compute the spectrum with the Horowitz-Hubeny series, Chebyshev collocation and time-domain evolution, each implemented in two independent codes and checked against the planar Schwarzschild-AdS limit. The charge acts in two ways. At the reduced charge $\hat q_c=0.72626$ the least damped pair of modes meets on the imaginary axis, so more strongly charged strings ring down without oscillation, and at the endpoint of the branch the spectrum is exactly hypergeometric. At large transverse momentum the distance of the modes from the light cone grows as $k^{1/(1-4s)}$, replacing the $k^{-1/5}$ law of the neutral brane. Rotation continues the exceptional point into a line in the plane of charge and rotation, adds a purely damped axisymmetric tower, and never produces superradiant growth. A near-horizon estimate used earlier for this geometry misses all of these effects.

</details>

Abstract: The orbital evolution of a neutron star and a much lower-mass primordial black hole can differ strikingly from that of typical compact binaries. We show how gravitational-wave emission mediates this evolution when neutron star modes remain locked in resonance with the orbit. These effects may leave observable signatures in pulsar timing or gravitational waves.

Abstract: We investigate the cosmological implications of scalar-field dark matter (SFDM) in a dynamical dark energy framework, extending previous analyses that considered SFDM within the standard $Λ$CDM cosmological model. We consistently implement the scenario at both the background and linear perturbation levels. We perform Bayesian parameter inference using Planck CMB measurements, DESI DR2 baryon acoustic oscillation (BAO) data, and Pantheon+ Type Ia supernovae to constrain the model parameter space. Our theoretical predictions show that lighter scalar-field masses produce increasingly strong scale-dependent suppression of matter fluctuations and more pronounced modifications to the CMB temperature power spectrum, whereas heavier masses progressively approach the cold dark matter limit. For the SFDM mass, we obtain a one-sided 95\% lower limit of $\log_{10}(m_φ/{\rm eV})>-24.04$ from our main joint analysis. We find that replacing the standard cold dark matter fluid with SFDM does not qualitatively alter the constraints on dynamical dark energy or the evidence for its evolution, including a phantom crossing at low redshifts.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统傅里叶基建模在频域间隔约0.1 yr⁻¹下使用对角协方差，虽计算高效，但无法捕捉更精细的谱特征，导致参数推断偏差。

Result: 在两个模拟数据集上验证了该方法具有更高的似然精度和更保真的谱特征恢复能力，且计算代价与传统方法相当。

Conclusion: 所提方法在保持计算效率的同时显著提升了低频随机过程建模的准确性与物理保真度，适用于引力波背景分析等精密计时应用。

Abstract: In pulsar timing array analyses, the stochastic process describing the gravitational wave background is typically modeled using a Fourier basis at discrete frequencies with an interval $\sim 0.1~\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$, with a diagonal Fourier-domain covariance determined by the power spectral density at the corresponding frequencies. Although this rank-reduced modeling significantly accelerates likelihood evaluation and provides a good approximation in many scenarios, it can lead to biased parameter inference because it is insensitive to spectral features on scales smaller than $\sim 0.1~\mathrm{yr}^{-1}$. We introduce a better construction of the low-rank covariance for low-frequency processes by maximizing the log-likelihood averaged over an ensemble of realizations drawn from the true model likelihood. In this approach, the basis-domain covariance matrix is sensitive to the entire model spectrum and is generally non-diagonal. Using two mock datasets, we demonstrate improved likelihood accuracy and more faithful recovery of spectral features compared to the conventional Fourier-diagonal model. Benchmark tests show that this new model requires a computational cost comparable to that of the conventional approach.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索Lorentz对称性破缺背景下类比黑洞的霍金辐射特性，寻找可被天文观测检验的新物理信号。

Method: 基于Einstein-bumblebee理论与Gross-Pitaevskii方程，在类Schwarzschild时空背景中推导声学度规，分析事件视界结构、计算类比霍金温度、灰体因子及能量发射谱。

Result: 发现双重声学视界结构；声学霍金温度T_ac存在最大值T_h/8；灰体因子低频时随ξ增大而增大、随ℓ增大而减小，高频趋于1；能量辐射率呈类普朗克谱，峰值频率处达最大，且对ξ和ℓ亦呈相反依赖。

Conclusion: ξ与ℓ对辐射特性的相反调控是该模型最显著且具实验可检验性的特征，为未来通过类比系统探测真实黑洞物理及Lorentz破缺效应提供了新途径。

Abstract: We derive an acoustic Schwarzschild-like black hole and study its analogous Hawking radiation in Einstein-bumblebee theory by using Gross-Pitaevskii theory in the background of Schwarzschild-like spacetime. This black hole have two kinds of event horizons: one is the gravitational horizon $r_h=2M$(black hole horizon), the others are the internal and external acoustic horizons $2M<r_{ac-}\leq4M,\;r_{ac}\geq4M$(dumb hole horizon) which depend on the tuning parameter $ξ$ and Lorentz violation coupling constant $\ell$. The corresponding Hawking temperature $T_h$ depends only on $\ell$, however $T_{ac}$ depends on $\ell$ and $ξ$. The dumb hole temperature $T_{ac}$ has a maximum $T_{acm}=T_h/8$. When considering the scalar particles radiating from the dumb hole, we find that in low frequency, the grey-body factor increases with the tuning parameter $ξ$, but decreases with $\ell$. In the high frequency, it approaches to $1$. Its energy emission rate is similar to the Planck's black spectrum where there has a maximum at a given peak frequency $ω_m$. The energy emission rate also increases with the tuning parameter $ξ$, but decreases with $\ell$. This opposite dependence relationship is the most experimentally valuable feature and can directly benchmark against observations of real astrophysical black holes.

</details>

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the quasinormal mode (QNM) frequencies of dyonic Reissner-Nordström (RN)-like black holes associated with scalar and gravitational perturbations in the framework of Einstein-bumblebee-Maxwell gravity theory. The QNMs are calculated using the 6th-order Padé averaged WKB approximation method. We analyse the dependence of oscillation frequencies and damping rate of the BHs for the free model parameters. To further verify the QNM spectrum, we study the time evolution of both the scalar and gravitational perturbations around the BHs and extract the dominant QNM from the resulting ringdown signal. Moreover, we try to estimate the BH parameters using Fisher analysis method for the future gravitational wave detector named Laser Interferometer Space Antenna (LISA). We found that for supermassive BHs with mass around $10^6$ -- $10^8 M_\odot$ and at lower redshifts, the signal-to-noise ratio for the detection of a QNM signal by the LISA is high. Further, for a representative source at a luminosity distance of $3$ Gpc, the BH mass at the detector reference frame and the Lorentz violating parameter are well constrained for supermassive BHs with mass $\ge 10^6.$

</details>


### [100] [A no-go theorem for closed timelike curves in globally null-foliated spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33706)
*Hongsheng Zhang*

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有判定闭合类时曲线（CTC）存在的准则分为三类（时间函数、能量条件、拓扑），但均存在局限；作者旨在建立第四类更基本、仅依赖单个标量方程（而非能量条件）的新准则。

Method: 利用零协向量与类时向量永不正交这一代数事实，证明：若时空存在全局定义的零梯度函数（即满足零程函方程），则该函数沿任意类时曲线严格递增，故不可能存在闭合类时曲线；并分析该假设与稳定因果性的逻辑关系，将其应用于Kundt、Robinson-Trautman及Misner/Ori/Ahmed等典型时空模型。

Result: 1) 建立新因果性准则：全局零梯度函数的存在可排除CTC；2) 该准则逻辑上独立于稳定因果性；3) 首次证明Kundt与Robinson-Trautman时空在零坐标全局单值区域无CTC；4) 揭示Misner、Ori、Ahmed构造的本质是紧致零方向变为类时，宇宙学常数、膨胀与剪切均为旁观者。

Conclusion: 零程函方程本身即可作为强大而基本的因果性判据，拓展了传统时间函数方法的适用范围，并深化了对多种引力波与奇异性时空因果结构的理解。

Abstract: The study of closed timelike curves has proceeded one spacetime at a time, and the criteria available form three families, the time function criteria, the energy condition criteria, and the topological criteria. We initiate a fourth family of criteria, elementary in form and resting on a single scalar equation rather than any energy condition. We prove that a spacetime admitting a globally defined function whose gradient is everywhere null cannot contain a closed timelike curve, and that the hypothesis is logically independent of stable causality rather than weaker than it. The proof turns on one algebraic fact, that a null covector is never orthogonal to a timelike vector, so the function grows strictly along every timelike curve and cannot close. Because the null eikonal equation alone decides the criterion, we can use it where the classical time function theorem is silent. We establish for the first time that no Kundt spacetime and no Robinson--Trautman spacetime can contain a closed timelike curve on any region where the null coordinate is globally single valued, whatever the cosmological constant and the rotation one form, which settles a question left open for gyratonic waves. We further show that the Misner, Ori and Ahmed constructions reduce to the statement that a compact null direction has turned timelike, and that the cosmological constant, the expansion and the shear are spectators in all of them.

</details>


### [101] [Forecasting Strongly Lensed Binary Black Hole Rates from the Stochastic Gravitational-Wave Background with TianQin](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33751)
*Zheng-Cheng Liang*

Abstract: The millihertz stochastic gravitational-wave background (SGWB) provides a population-level probe of stellar-mass binary black holes (BBHs) and their cosmic merger history. We develop a framework using TianQin alone to propagate SGWB-informed BBH population constraints into forecasts of strongly lensed BBH mergers detectable by ground-based gravitational-wave detectors. The framework distinguishes between lensed events with at least one detected image and lens systems in which both images are detected and successfully associated. Using a one-year mock observation, we infer the BBH population and propagate the resulting posterior through a galaxy-scale lensing model with image-level selection. For the adopted system-magnification threshold, \(μ_{\rm sys}\ge 2\), the median predicted rate of lensed events with at least one detected image is \(R_{\rm event}^{\rm det}=5.50\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}\), while the median rate of associated two-image lens systems is \(R_{\rm sys}^{\rm det}=2.54\times10^{-4}\,\mathrm{yr}^{-1}\); the median detectable system fraction is \(\mathcal{F}_{\rm sys}^{\rm det}=3.35\times10^{-4}\). Compared with the prior-only propagation, the pooled 16th--84th percentile widths decrease by 6.5\% for the event rate, 6.4\% for the system rate, and 4.0\% for the system fraction. Our work establishes a quantitative link between millihertz SGWB inference and forecasts of strongly lensed BBH mergers for ground-based gravitational-wave observations.

</details>


### [102] [Massive Scalar Quasinormal Modes and Quasibound States of Charged Black Holes with Logarithmic corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33883)

Main category: gr-qc
