  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究长程度规变形如何影响有质量标量场的动力学行为，特别是其束缚与衰减特性；同时为非线性电动力学与暗物质模型提供可检验的引力波/场模本征谱基准。

Method: 采用对数形变Reissner-Nordström度规作为背景几何；结合一阶与六阶WKB近似、全局匹配法、改进Leaver方法（适配对数奇点）及时域数值演化；引入Padé逼近与Gamow隧穿解析估计；并基于复径向路径与适当视界正则化，在相对论双线性框架下验证QNM与QBS的正交性。

Result: 小标量质量使势垒外移、升高且曲率减小，导致各有限多极与泛音的阻尼减弱；多种数值/解析方法对QNM与QBS结果高度一致；Padé逼近束缚能误差仅约0.04%，而Gamow隧穿低估衰减约25%；增大标量质量或非线性耦合增强束缚但缩短寿命；首次在该类对数形变几何中实现QNM-QBS正交性验证。

Conclusion: 长程度规变形显著调控标量场模的束缚与耗散平衡；所用多方法交叉验证增强了结果可靠性；正交性证实为构建完备模展开与响应理论奠定基础，对引力波建模与类比引力实验具有启示意义。

Abstract: We study how long-range deformations in a black-hole metric affect the oscillation, trapping, and decay of a neutral, minimally coupled massive scalar field. We choose a logarithmic deformation of Reissner-Nordström (RN) sourced by nonlinear electrodynamics. It provides an exact solution of Einstein gravity and admits a finite completion. The same geometry also arises in a phenomenological perfect-fluid dark-matter model. In this setup, we analyze both quasinormal modes (QNMs), which describe relaxation, and quasibound states (QBSs), which describe trapped modes with small horizon absorption. We first show that a small scalar mass moves the potential barrier outward, raises it, and reduces its curvature. First-order WKB then predicts weaker damping at every fixed finite multipole and overtone. We then compare several approaches, namely sixth-order WKB, global matching, a generalized Leaver method (adapted to the logarithmic geometry) and time-domain evolution. All methods yield consistent QNM estimates within their ranges of validity. Global matching and Leaver also agree closely for QBSs. Further analytic estimates with a reference trapped state show that a local Padé approximation gives a binding-energy error of about $0.04\%$, while leading Gamow tunnelling underestimates damping by about $25\%$. Across the sampled QBSs, increasing the scalar mass or nonlinear coupling strengthens binding but shortens the lifetime through greater horizon absorption. Finally, using an appropriate horizon regularization and a common complex radial contour, we demonstrate orthogonality of an independently computed QNM-QBS pair using a suitable relativistic bilinear framework.

</details>


### [103] [Gravitational Radiation Coupled to a real massive scalar Field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33928)
*Yue Ma,Xianghao Wu,Weidong Zhang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了在波规范下，具有正质量实标量场的爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登系统的中小初值全局解的渐近行为，证明了度规扰动的线性化迹分量具有最优衰减率 $\hat t^{-1} \simeq t^{-1}$，并建立了初始克莱因-戈登能量由该主导系数定量控制的一点可观测性估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中物质场与引力场耦合系统的长时间行为，特别是其解的渐近结构和能量传播特性。

Method: 在波规范下分析爱因斯坦-克莱因-戈登系统；利用渐近分析和能量估计方法研究小初值解的衰减性质；建立线性化迹分量的最优衰减率及可观测性不等式。

Result: 证明了度规扰动的线性化迹分量具有 $\hat t^{-1} \simeq t^{-1}$ 的最优衰减率；建立了初始克莱因-戈登能量由该主导系数定量控制的一点可观测性估计。

Conclusion: 该结果揭示了该耦合系统中引力辐射主阶项的精确衰减规律，并提供了从远场观测反推初始能量的理论依据。

Abstract: We study the asymptotic behavior of small-data global solutions to the Einstein--Klein--Gordon system with a positive-mass real scalar field in wave gauge. We show that the linearized trace component of the metric perturbation admits a $\hat t^{-1}\simeq t^{-1}$ optimal decreasing rate. Moreover, a quantitative one-point observability estimate is established: the initial Klein-Gordon energy is controlled by this leading order coefficient.

</details>


### [104] [Comment on "Effect of perfect fluid dark matter..." [Phys. Dark Univ. 34 (2021) 100891]](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34019)
*Ming-Hsun Li,Kwei-Chou Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文澄清了Shaymatov等人所用黑洞时空度规的物理含义，指出该度规实际描述冷暗物质与幻影场背景构成的双组分系统，而非单组分完美流体暗物质（PFDM）；将其等效为总状态方程w=-1的PFDM在物理上不自洽，因无法产生暗物质晕所需的引力吸引；同时明确了原模型的不同参数适用范围，以防在极端天体物理场景中误用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Shaymatov等人所用黑洞度规的真实物理含义，纠正将其误释为单组分完美流体暗物质（PFDM）的错误理解，并防止在极端天体物理场景中的误用。

Method: 通过分析Li和Yang（2012）原始推导的度规结构及其对应的能量-动量张量，辨析其实际对应冷暗物质与幻影场组成的双组分系统，并考察其总状态方程与引力行为的物理一致性。

Result: 确认该度规不能合理地被解释为总状态方程w_total = -1的单组分PFDM；指出此类解释违背暗物质晕所需引力聚集的基本要求；厘清了原模型中不同参数区域的物理含义与适用边界。

Conclusion: 将该度规视为单组分PFDM是物理上不成立的；必须坚持其双组分本质（冷暗物质+幻影场），并在应用中严格区分参数区域，以确保理论自洽与天文建模可靠性。

Abstract: This Comment clarifies the physical interpretation of the black hole spacetime metric employed by Shaymatov \textit{et al.} [Phys. Dark Univ. 34 (2021) 100891]. We point out that the metric, originally derived in Li and Yang (2012), describes a specific dual-component system consisting of cold dark matter and a phantom field background. Treating this geometry as a single-component ``Perfect Fluid Dark Matter'' (PFDM) with a total equation of state $w_{\mathrm{total}} = -1$ is physically incompatible with the gravitational attraction required for dark matter halos. Furthermore, we clarify the distinct parameter regimes of the original model to prevent potential misapplications in extreme astrophysical scenarios.

</details>


### [105] [Repetitive Magnetic Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34164)
*Ke Wang,Xin-Yun Hu,Xiao-Xiong Zeng*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文将Ruffini等人近期提出的非线性迭代重复Penrose过程推广至磁Penrose过程，在Kerr度规与Wald磁场背景下，分析了赤道面上最大能量提取的最优条件，指出其核心机制是充电反馈；随着黑洞电荷积累，Wald单极电压线性下降并变号，最终中和磁增益，使迭代过程终止，回归纯机械Penrose过程，从而阻止单次磁Penrose过程所预言的超100%能量提取效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为理解强磁场下旋转黑洞能量提取的极限及迭代过程的可持续性，需将非线性迭代Penrose机制拓展至含外加磁场的情形，并考察电荷积累对过程自洽性的反馈影响。

Method: 在Kerr时空和Wald磁解背景下，将电磁场与粒子电荷均视为测试场/测试电荷；在赤道面分析最优能量提取条件；结合电荷守恒与Wald解的电压特性，研究黑洞电荷演化及其对磁增益的抑制效应。

Result: 发现迭代磁Penrose过程的核心是充电反馈；黑洞电荷渐近趋近于Wald电荷的一半；Wald单极电压随电荷线性减小并变号，导致磁增益被中和；迭代过程必然终止，退化为机械Penrose过程。

Conclusion: 单次磁Penrose过程所预言的超100%能量提取效率无法在迭代过程中持续实现，因电荷积累引发的自洽反馈会自然终止该过程，揭示了磁Penrose机制在真实物理场景中的内在限制。

Abstract: The nonlinear iterative repetitive Penrose process established recently by Ruffini et al.\ is generalized to the magnetic Penrose process. The spacetime background is the Kerr metric, the external magnetic field is described by the Wald solution, the electromagnetic field is treated as a test field, and the charge is treated as a test charge. We analyze the optimal conditions for maximum energy extraction on the equatorial plane and find that under these conditions the core mechanism of the iterative process is charging feedback. After charging, the black hole charge is approximately half of the Wald charge. When the neutral parent particle decays, charge conservation forces the infalling fragment to carry charge while the other fragment carries the opposite charge, so the black hole charge accumulates monotonically. The monopole voltage of the Wald solution decreases linearly with the charge $Q$ and crosses zero to reverse sign at a certain point, so the magnetic gain is gradually neutralized by the charge. The repetitive magnetic Penrose process is therefore forced to terminate and eventually falls back to the mechanical Penrose process. The energy extraction efficiency $>100\%$ predicted by the single magnetic Penrose process will therefore be prevented.

</details>


### [106] [Finite-Distance Imaging of Relativistic Equilibrium Tori around Genuine Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34331)
*Hao-Peng Yan,Xiang-Qian Li,Xiao-Jun Yue*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过广义相对论光线追踪方法，研究了中性Kerr-Bertotti-Robinson黑洞周围压力支撑环的有限距离成像特性，分析了外部磁场、填充度、观测倾角、自吸收及黑洞自旋对图像亮度、尺寸和不对称性的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解强引力场中磁化黑洞周围吸积流的辐射图像特征，特别是有限距离观测下的亮度、尺度与不对称性如何受物理参数联合调控。

Method: 采用广义相对论光线追踪，结合灰体发射与吸收模型，模拟静止、压力支撑环在不同外部磁场、填充度、倾角、自吸收强度及黑洞自旋参数下的有限距离（r=50M）图像。

Result: 外部磁场增强显著提升总亮度与表观尺寸；高填充度使亮度增5.9倍而尺寸仅增10%；倾角增大增强横向亮度对比；强自吸收可与高绝对亮度共存；黑洞自旋从0.5增至0.95时，逆行环变暗约7倍，顺行环亮度几乎不变（仅+2%）。

Conclusion: 环的平衡边界、流体运动与光学深度三者协同决定有限距离图像的亮度、尺度与不对称性，不同参数影响机制存在显著差异。

Abstract: We calculate finite-distance images of stationary, pressure-supported tori around genuine, electrically neutral Kerr--Bertotti--Robinson black holes using general relativistic ray tracing with gray emission and absorption. The emitting volume is bounded by a constant-specific-angular-momentum equilibrium free surface, and the observer is placed at a radius of $50M$. Along the investigated external-field sequence, integrated brightness and apparent size both increase strongly as the field modifies the equilibrium source and photon propagation. Higher torus filling has a different effect: in a representative sequence, brightness grows by a factor of $5.9$ while the intensity-weighted image size increases by only $10\%$. Increasing viewing inclination enhances lateral brightness contrast. Stronger self-absorption can coexist with higher absolute brightness because emission and absorption scale together in the adopted gray prescription. At common pressure center and filling, increasing the dimensionless spin from $0.5$ to $0.95$ dims the retrograde sequence by a factor of about $7$ as its equilibrium contracts, whereas prograde brightness changes by only $2\%$. These contrasting responses show how the equilibrium boundary, fluid motion, and optical depth jointly shape the brightness, scale, and asymmetry of finite-distance torus images.

</details>


### [107] [Topological analysis of novel charged-AdS black hole solutions embedded in dark matter halos with quintessence field](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34334)
*Muhammad Yasir,Hafiza Bushra Mujahid,Faisal Javed,Shahid Qaisar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了NGC 4649型暗物质晕与类幽灵场共同影响下带电AdS黑洞的几何与热力学性质，发现暗物质增强引力、幽灵场提供斥力并显著改变时空渐近行为，并利用拓扑理论与广义自由能方法分析其相变与稳定性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究暗物质晕和 Quintessence 场对带电 AdS 黑洞时空结构与热力学行为（尤其是相变与稳定性）的联合影响。

Method: 基于修正爱因斯坦场方程构建黑洞解；通过度规函数与视界结构分析几何性质；结合Duan-φ-映射拓扑理论与广义自由能方法研究热力学相变与稳定性；分析幽灵场状态方程参数ω_q变化对系统的影响。

Result: 暗物质晕增强引力吸引，Quintessence 场提供斥力并显著改变时空渐近行为；系统存在可由ω_q调控的临界行为与相结构；拓扑方法成功刻画了黑洞性质的稳定性条件与临界点。

Conclusion: 暗物质与Quintessence 共同塑造了带电AdS黑洞的几何与热力学特性，其中Quintessence的状态方程参数ω_q是调控其相变与稳定性的关键物理量。

Abstract: In this work, we investigate the properties of a charged anti-de Sitter (AdS) black hole in the presence of a NGC 4649-type dark matter halo and a surrounding quintessence field. Starting from the modified Einstein field equations (EFEs), we construct the corresponding black hole solutions by incorporating the effects of electric charge, cosmological constant, dark matter distribution, and quintessence characterized by a variable equation of state. We analyze the resulting spacetime geometry through the metric function and horizon structure, showing that the dark matter halo strengthens the gravitational attraction, whereas the quintessence field provides a repulsive contribution that substantially alters the asymptotic behavior of the spacetime. Furthermore, the thermodynamic properties are investigated by applying the Duan-\(φ\)-mapping topological theory together with the generalized free energy method. This framework allows us to examine the topological structure, critical behavior, and stability conditions of the black hole system in a consistent manner. We further study the fractional shifts relative to the reference value of \(ω_q\) and observe that variations in \(ω_q\) produce systematic changes which highlight the important role of the quintessence field in determining the phase structure and critical behavior of the black hole system.

</details>


### [108] [Relativistic diffusing particles, entropy and varying cosmological constants](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34341)
*Z. Haba*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文探讨了能量-动量张量非守恒情形下的爱因斯坦方程，引入时变牛顿常数G和宇宙学常数Λ，并将非守恒效应与热力学第一定律及粒子扩散机制相联系；通过构建含粘滞与热流的修正能量-动量张量，研究了扩散粒子气体在各向同性膨胀宇宙中的态方程、能量密度、熵及相空间分布演化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统广义相对论要求能量-动量张量协变守恒，但某些物理场景（如粒子扩散、暗能量演化）可能破坏该守恒律；本文旨在建立一个允许T^{μν}非守恒、同时自洽耦合时变G与Λ的引力框架，并将其与热力学和动力学演化相联系。

Method: 从修正的爱因斯坦方程出发，将右侧张量分解为8πGT^{μν}−g^{μν}Λ+Σ^{μν}，其中Σ^{μν}表征粘滞与热流；利用协变导数∇_μT^{μν}关联热力学第一定律（引入温度T与熵S）；建模扩散性理想流体，采用时变线性绝热态方程P_t = w_t ρ_t，并求解空间平坦FLRW背景下的质量为零/有质量相对论性粒子气体的解析解。

Result: 推导出协变守恒条件对G和Λ时空变化的约束关系；证明∇_μT^{μν}可由T和S表达，体现能量非守恒源于粒子扩散；获得扩散粒子气体在膨胀宇宙中能量密度ρ_t、熵S及相空间分布的显式演化规律；发现大时间极限下，有质量粒子气体趋于无质量极限；给出平直宇宙中无质量粒子气体的可解模型结果。

Conclusion: 能量-动量非守恒并非理论缺陷，而可自然源于微观扩散过程，并能与热力学和引力几何自洽统一；时变G与Λ可作为有效描述宏观耗散与宇宙演化的参数；该框架为理解暗能量、早期宇宙耗散机制及引力热力学提供了新视角。

Abstract: We discuss Einstein equations with a non-conserved energy-momentum $T^{μν}$. The energy-momentum tensor $\tilde{T}^{μν}$ on the right hand side of Einstein equations is expressed in the form $8πGT^{μν}-g^{μν}Λ+Σ^{μν}$ where $G$ is the Newton constant, $Λ$ is the cosmological term and $T^{μν}$ is the energy-momentum of an ideal fluid.
  The remaining part $Σ^{μν}$ describes the viscosity and a heat flow.
  We discuss space-time-dependent Newton ``constant" $G$ and the cosmological term $Λ$. The covariant conservation law for $\tilde{T}^{μν}$ imposes a relation between the energy-momentum (non)conservation and the variation of $G$ and $Λ$. The covariant derivative $\nabla_μT^{μν}$ can be related to the first law of thermodynamics and expressed by the temperature $T$ and the entropy $S$. The energy-momentum non-conservation in our models is a consequence of a diffusion of its constituting particles.
  We describe a gas of diffusing massive particles in a homogeneous isotropic space-time as a system with a time-dependent linear barotropic equation of state
  $P_{t}=w_{t}ρ_{t}$ with $0<w_{t}\leq \frac{1}{3}$.
  The gas of massive diffusing particles in an expanding universe tends in a limit of infinite time to the gas of diffusing massless particles.
  We obtain results on the energy density, entropy and phase space dstribution
  in a soluble model of massless relativistic particles in a spatially flat expanding universe.

</details>


### [109] [Geometric criticality and regular quasibound spectra of axial Proca fields in Schwarzschild spacetime](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34528)
*Antonio Sousa Ribeiro,Francisco Artur Pinheiro Alves Junior*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了奇宇称Proca势在Schwarzschild时空中的局域几何临界点与全局拟束缚谱之间的关系，发现其轴向势的临界结构与类时测地线有效势形式相同，并在特定参数条件下出现临界点合并；通过标度律和数值计算揭示了拟束缚频率在临界点附近的正则行为，并明确区分了几何势临界性与谱简并性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解奇宇称Proca场在弯曲时空（特别是Schwarzschild黑洞背景）中拟束缚态的物理起源及其与时空几何结构（如ISCO）的深层联系，尤其是局域势能临界行为如何影响全局离散谱。

Method: 理论分析结合数值计算：利用势函数归一化与参数展开（δ展开），推导临界点附近各项（分离距离、势垒-势阱深度、曲率）的精确标度律；采用连分式法计算轴向拟束缚模频率，并用Riccati shooting方法交叉验证；分析径向算符关于δ的仿射性以支持解析解释。

Result: 发现轴向Proca势的外极小与内极大在r=6M（ISCO半径）处合并，对应临界质量参数(Mμ_c)²=ℓ(ℓ+1)/12；得到δ^(1/2)、δ^(3/2)等精确标度律；数值证实拟束缚频率在阈值附近连续且近似线性变化；证实径向算符对δ呈仿射依赖；明确指出几何临界性不等价于谱简并。

Conclusion: 奇宇称Proca场在Schwarzschild时空中的拟束缚谱虽受势能几何临界点显著调制，但该临界性本身并不导致谱简并；谱的正则行为源于势形变的标度性质，而非本征值退化；这一结论深化了对弯曲时空中场论临界现象与量子化谱之间关系的理解。

Abstract: We investigate how a local geometric critical point of the odd-parity Proca potential relates to the global quasibound spectrum in Schwarzschild spacetime. After normalization by $μ^2$, the axial potential $V_{\rm ax}=(1-2M/r)[\ell(\ell+1)/r^2+μ^2]$ has the same radial form as the timelike-geodesic effective potential. Its outer minimum and inner maximum merge at $r=6M$, the Schwarzschild innermost stable circular orbit, when $(Mμ_c)^2=\ell(\ell+1)/12$. With $δ=1-(Mμ)^2/(Mμ_c)^2$, we derive exact fold scalings: stationary-point separation $\proptoδ^{1/2}$, barrier--well depth $\proptoδ^{3/2}$, and curvature magnitudes $\proptoδ^{1/2}$. Continued-fraction calculations for the first three axial quasibound branches with $\ell=1,2,3$, cross-checked for selected modes by independent Riccati shooting, show regular frequency continuation across the threshold and approximately linear near-critical behavior. At fixed radius the radial operator is affine in $δ$, supporting a conditional simple-root analytic interpretation. The main result is the explicit distinction between geometric potential criticality and spectral degeneracy for the branches examined.

</details>


### [110] [Existence of static axisymmetric solutions to the Einstein equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34555)
*Philippe G. LeFloch,Luc Rozoy*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构造了在正宇宙学常数下满足静态爱因斯坦方程的、具有圆对称性的光滑黎曼度量和标量势，定义在挖去两个内球的三维球体上，边界为三个非退化视界；通过调节两个黑洞质量参数控制内边界的面积，并克服了在收缩视界附近等多区域中线性与非线性项联合估计的分析难点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有物理意义（如多个黑洞共存）且数学严格（光滑、紧致、具明确几何边界）的静态爱因斯坦解，填补正宇宙学常数下多视界静态时空的构造空白。

Method: 采用加权Hölder估计控制线性化方程与非线性余项；将Kottler‘领圈’解与de Sitter空间拼接作为初值近似；引入约化椭圆爱因斯坦方程并辅以两个障碍张量；通过调节两个轴向位置消去其系数；利用Bianchi恒等式及关于辅助Killing场的能量估计确保解的适定性。

Result: 存在一族光滑静态解，其底流形为三球挖去两个开球，边界含三个非退化视界（两个内视界对应小质量黑洞，面积可控；外视界为宇宙学视界）；内视界半径线性依赖各自质量，中心间距按总质量的立方根缩放；解在固定宇宙学常数下由任一内边界内蕴度量唯一确定（模等距与势函数正标度）。

Conclusion: 成功构造了首类具有两个可控质量黑洞与正宇宙学常数共存的紧致静态爱因斯坦解，建立了多视界几何参数与物理质量间的定量关系，并发展了适用于收缩边界区域的加权分析技术。

Abstract: We construct smooth Riemannian metrics and scalar potentials that are invariant under a circle action and satisfy the static Einstein equations with positive cosmological constant. The underlying compact three-manifold is a ball with two disjoint open balls removed. The potential (or lapse function) is positive in the interior and vanishes at the boundary, consisting of three non-degenerate horizons. The areas of the two inner boundary components are controlled by two arbitrary parameters, namely the masses of the black holes, which are taken to be sufficiently small and comparable. Each inner horizon radius scales linearly with the individual mass parameters, while the separation of their reference centers scales as the cube root of the total mass. These two scales are assumed to be sufficiently small at different rates, whereas the cosmological length remains fixed. The main analytical challenge is to control the linearized equation and the nonlinear remainder simultaneously near the shrinking horizons, between the centers and in the outer region. Weighted Hölder estimates quantify the growth of the inverse and show that the error of an approximation joining Kottler collars to de Sitter space is small enough to close a contraction argument. Namely, we solve a reduced elliptic Einstein equation that is endowed with two obstruction tensors; varying the two axial positions removes their coefficients. The contracted Bianchi identity and an energy estimate for an auxiliary vector field (Killing field) introduced by the projection to vanish. Finally, for a fixed cosmological constant, the intrinsic metric of one of the two boundary components is sufficient to determine a static solution with simply connected interior and non-vanishing boundary gradient, up to isometry and multiplication of the potential by a positive constant.

</details>


### [111] [Exact spinning wormholes in five dimensions from Toda integrability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34672)
*Masato Nozawa*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个五维爱因斯坦-幻影标量场系统的精确解，描述连接两个渐近平直区域的旋转虫洞，是Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞在等角动量下的旋转推广；利用共形一度假设将径向方程约化为可积的A1⊕A2 Toda系统，从而解析地满足正则性和渐近平直性条件，得到由质量、等角动量和标量荷参数化的三参数解族；该解无奇点、无视界、测地完备且稳定因果，并具有唯一喉部、有角动量上限、存在唯一静止于一端的能层；在标量场消失的标度极限下退化为极值Myers-Perry黑洞外区。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构造具有物理合理性的五维旋转虫洞精确解，拓展经典Ellis-Bronnikov虫洞模型，并揭示其内在可积结构与几何物理性质。

Method: 基于共形一度（cohomogeneity-one）对称性假设，将五维Einstein-phantom-scalar方程约化为径向Toda系统；通过求解A1⊕A2 Toda方程获得闭式通解；严格施加正则性、渐近平直性、因果性及能层存在性等全局条件，筛选出物理可接受的三参数解族。

Result: 获得一个三参数（质量、等角动量、标量荷）的五维旋转虫洞精确解；证明其测地完备、无曲率奇点、无事件视界、稳定因果；确立喉部唯一性与角动量上限；证明存在唯一相对于某一无穷远静止的ergosurface；发现其标度极限对应极值Myers-Perry黑洞外区。

Conclusion: 五维Einstein-phantom-scalar系统中存在一类具有良好全局性质的可积旋转虫洞解，其Toda可积结构不仅保障了解析可解性，还深刻约束了几何与物理特征，为高维虫洞与黑洞的统一理解提供了新范例。

Abstract: We construct a new exact solution to the five-dimensional Einstein-phantom-scalar system that describes a rotating wormhole connecting two asymptotically flat regions. The solution represents a rotating generalization of the Ellis-Bronnikov wormhole with equal angular momenta. Under the cohomogeneity-one ansatz, we demonstrate that the radial equations reduce to an integrable $A_1\oplus A_2$ Toda system, from which the general local solution can be obtained in closed form. This integrable structure enables us to implement the regularity and asymptotic-flatness conditions analytically, thereby singling out a three-parameter family of solutions characterized by the mass, the equal angular momenta, and the scalar charge. The resulting spacetimes are geodesically complete, horizonless and stably causal, and contain no curvature singularities. The Toda structure further allows us to prove the uniqueness of the wormhole throat and to derive a sharp upper bound on the angular momentum for a fixed throat area. We also establish the existence and uniqueness of an ergosurface with respect to the stationary frame that is nonrotating at one asymptotic end, while the opposite end rotates relative to this frame. In a suitable scaling limit, the scalar field vanishes and the solution approaches the exterior region of the extremal Myers-Perry black hole with equal angular momenta.

</details>


### [112] [The $C^0$-inextendibility of Kerr spacetimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35012)
*Bobby Eka Gunara*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文证明了Kerr时空及其所有连通覆盖时空的C^0不可延拓性，适用于正质量与非零角动量的各种情形（亚极值、极值、超极值），并指出奇环处存在由因果同伦类中固端测地线固有时间无界所刻画的几何障碍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解广义相对论中黑洞时空边界（特别是Kerr解）的几何可延拓性，尤其是奇点附近的因果与度规行为。

Method: 利用固定端点因果同伦类中测地线固有时间的无界性作为C^0不可延拓性的判据，并分析共形扰动、因果锥加宽及轴对称定态扰动下的稳定性。

Result: Kerr时空及其所有连通覆盖时空在C^0意义下不可延拓；该不可延拓性对正连续共形缩放和因果锥连续拓宽均鲁棒；给出了扰动的坐标范数尖锐估计，并构造了趋近奇环时曲率任意大的完备类时测地线。

Conclusion: Kerr时空的奇环并非仅是一个坐标奇点，而是具有本质几何不可延拓性的物理奇点，其障碍不依赖全局因果条件，且在广泛扰动下保持稳定。

Abstract: We prove the $C^0$-inextendibility of the full Kerr realizations considered in this paper and all their connected covering spacetimes. The result holds for positive mass and nonzero rotation in the subextremal, extremal, and overextremal regimes. The obstruction at the singular ring is expressed by unbounded proper time in a fixed-endpoint causal homotopy class. It persists under every positive continuous conformal rescaling and under continuous widening of the Kerr causal cones, without any global causality assumption. We give sharp bounds in a fixed coordinate norm for stationary and axisymmetric perturbations and describe complete timelike geodesics that encounter arbitrarily large curvature near the ring.

</details>


### [113] [Quasinormal modes of thick branes in $f(R,T)$ gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35096)
*Yu-Peng E,Hai-Long Jia,Chun-Chun Zhu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了f(R,T)=R+αT引力理论中厚膜brane的张量扰动和准正规模（QNMs），推导出引力扰动的薛定谔类方程及对应的有效势，发现其呈现依赖参数的双势垒结构，并通过透射谱与准正规模实部的良好吻合验证结果；参数b和α分别主导势垒宽度与高度；采用时域演化与直接积分法提取QNMs，低阶模一致，高阶模因强阻尼和数值污染难以提取；表明f(R,T)修正显著影响有效势与准正规模谱，为膜世界中修改引力提供可观测印记。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究f(R,T)修正引力（特别是f(R,T)=R+αT形式）对厚膜brane中引力张量扰动及准正规模的影响，以寻找修改引力在膜世界模型中的潜在观测特征。

Method: 基于厚膜brane背景解，推导张量扰动满足的Schrödinger-like方程并构造有效势；分析其双势垒结构随参数b和α的变化；结合透射谱分析与准正规模实部对比；采用时间域数值演化和直接积分两种方法提取QNMs并交叉验证。

Result: 有效势呈双势垒结构，b控制势垒宽度、α控制高度；透射谱共振峰与QNMs实部高度吻合；低阶QNMs两种方法结果一致，高阶模因强阻尼和数值误差难以准确提取；f(R,T)修正显著改变有效势形貌与QNMs频谱。

Conclusion: f(R,T)=R+αT引力修正对厚膜brane的引力扰动动力学和准正规模谱具有显著影响，其双势垒有效势及相应QNMs特征可作为该类修改引力理论在膜世界场景下的潜在唯象印记。

Abstract: We study tensor perturbations and quasinormal modes (QNMs) of thick branes in $f(R,T)=R+αT$ gravity. Starting from the background thick brane solutions, we derive the Schrödinger-like equation for gravitational perturbations and construct the corresponding effective potential. The effective potential exhibits a double-barrier structure whose properties depend on the model parameters. We analyze transmission spectra and find good agreement between resonance peaks and the real parts of the quasinormal frequencies. The parameters $b$ and $α$ mainly control the width and height of the potential barriers, respectively. QNMs are extracted using both time-domain evolution and direct integration methods, showing consistent results for low-lying modes. Higher overtones become increasingly difficult to extract due to stronger damping and numerical contamination. Our results indicate that $f(R,T)$ corrections significantly affect both the effective potential and the quasinormal spectrum, providing a possible imprint of modified gravity in braneworld perturbations.

</details>


### [114] [An asymptotically conical regular black hole in four-dimensional nonpolynomial quasitopological gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35248)
*Jia-Zhou Liu,Shao-Wen Wei,Yu-Xiao Liu*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了一个四维非多项式拟拓扑引力中的渐近圆锥正则黑洞，并研究了其热力学性质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在构造一个具有德西特核心和有限曲率不变量的渐近圆锥正则黑洞，并研究其热力学行为。

Method: 从线性约化函数出发得到渐近圆锥Schwarzschild类解，再引入非线性变形以保持主导渐近锥结构并获得精确黑洞解；利用Hamiltonian和Wald框架建立相应黑洞热力学。

Result: 得到了具有德西特核心和有限曲率不变量的精确黑洞解；熵包含有理项和对数项；两个解均满足黑洞热力学第一定律。

Conclusion: 该工作成功构造出满足物理合理性的渐近圆锥正则黑洞，并验证了其热力学一致性。

Abstract: In this work, we construct an asymptotically conical regular black hole in four-dimensional nonpolynomial quasitopological gravity. Starting from a linear choice of the reduced function, which yields an asymptotically conical Schwarzschild-like solution, we introduce a nonlinear deformation that preserves the leading asymptotic cone while producing an exact black hole solution with a de Sitter core and finite curvature invariants. We further establish the corresponding black hole thermodynamics for both solutions within the Hamiltonian and Wald frameworks. The entropy contains rational and logarithmic terms in the regular model, and both solutions satisfy the first law of black hole thermodynamics.

</details>


### [115] [Bargmann-invariant witness of the graviton commutator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35253)
*Hiroki Matsui*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出利用Bargmann不变量的相位作为量子引力的标志性信号，该相位无法由任何经典引力场再现，并通过囚禁离子类比实验验证其可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找一种能明确区分量子引力与经典引力的可观测信号，因为仅靠退相干效应无法证实引力的量子化。

Method: 分析物质波干涉仪中Bargmann不变量的相位，将干涉相干性精确分解为牛顿相位、引力子态特征泛函和对易子相位三部分，并考察Wigner正定引力子态下的经典可再现性。

Result: Bargmann相位隔离出与引力子态和牛顿相互作用无关的对易子相位，该相位受路径信息退相干限制；虽真实引力场景中辐射反作用效应极小（低60个数量级），但囚禁离子类比系统可在单位量级上检验。

Conclusion: Bargmann相位是一种真正不可经典模拟的量子引力签名，为实验探测引力量子化提供了新路径。

Abstract: We identify the phase of a Bargmann invariant of two matter-wave interferometers as a quantum gravity signature that cannot be reproduced by any branchwise-definite classical gravitational field. For matter coupled linearly to linearized gravity, the interferometric coherence factorizes exactly into a conservative Newtonian phase, the characteristic functional of the graviton state, and a commutator phase. For Wigner-positive graviton states, the first two are classically reproducible; therefore, decoherence alone cannot certify that gravity is quantized. The Bargmann phase isolates the commutator, which is independent of the graviton state and of all Newtonian interactions and is bounded by which-path decoherence. For gravity, this radiation-reaction effect is sixty orders of magnitude below experimental reach, but trapped-ion analogs can test it at order unity.

</details>


### [116] [From Specific Heat Bounds to Penrose-like Inequalities: A Unified Thermodynamic Perspective](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35264)
*Kai-Peng Lu,H. Lu,Run-Qiu Yang*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文在明确且物理上合理的假设下，识别出一种局部到全局的热力学机制，统一了多个先前互不关联的黑洞不等式，并探讨了其在全息对偶中的含义。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 统一多个先前互不关联的黑洞不等式，揭示局部热力学约束与全局几何不等式之间的结构联系。

Method: 基于明确且物理上合理的假设，聚焦于渐近平直、静态球对称黑洞，提供统一热力学视角；推广至渐近Schwarzschild反德西特黑洞/膜，并在全息背景下分析其对偶场论含义。

Result: 建立了一个核心积分不等式，在NEC条件下可直接在一般静态平面对称渐近Schwarzschild反德西特黑膜中成立；揭示了归一化热力学量在对偶场论中的单调行为。

Conclusion: 本文旨在揭示局部热力学约束与全局几何不等式之间的统一结构视角，而非给出所提局部界的一般性证明；超出当前考虑类别的局部界建立仍是开放问题。

Abstract: Under explicit and physically motivated assumptions, we identify a local-to-global thermodynamic mechanism that organizes several previously disconnected inequalities on black holes. We first focus on asymptotically flat, static, and spherically-symmetric black holes, and provide a unified thermodynamic perspective on various Penrose-like inequalities. We also discuss the generalizations in asymptotically Schwarzschild anti-de Sitter black holes/branes. In holographic settings, these bounds imply monotonic behavior of normalized thermodynamic quantities in the dual field theory. Notably, one central integrated inequality can be established directly under the NEC in general static planar symmetric asymptotically Schwarzschild anti-de Sitter black branes. Our purpose is not to claim a fully general proof of the proposed local bounds, but to expose a unified structural perspective connecting local thermodynamic constraints to global geometric inequalities. Establishing the local bounds beyond the classes considered here remains an open problem.

</details>


### [117] [Gravitational-Wave Signatures and Periodic Orbits of Charged Particles Around a Five-Dimensional Rotating Black Hole: Comparison with the Kerr Black Hole](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35386)
*Bahromjon Shokirov,Tursunali Xamidov,Chen Zhou,Sanjar Shaymatov,Chengxun Yuan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了五维旋转Myers-Perry黑洞在均匀磁场中赤道面上带电粒子的周期轨道及其极端质量比旋进所辐射的引力波，并与Kerr黑洞情形对比。结果表明，五维黑洞对带电粒子束缚更弱、势阱更浅，轨道更靠近分界线；磁场可导致轨道出现远距离环状结构并改变频率关系；五维信号周期更长、频率更低、振幅小约18%；LISA信噪比略低于Kerr情形。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索高维黑洞（特别是D=5 Myers-Perry）在外部磁场中带电粒子动力学及相应引力波特征，以检验高维引力理论和未来空间引力波探测器（如LISA）对维度效应的敏感性。

Method: 基于广义相对论中的测地线方程与洛伦兹力耦合，分析赤道面内带电粒子的稳定圆轨道与周期轨道；利用数值拼凑法（numerical kludge method）计算极端质量比旋进的引力波波形；比较D=5 Myers-Perry黑洞与四维Kerr黑洞在相同磁场下的轨道参数、频谱特征与辐射反应寿命。

Result: 五维黑洞中粒子能量在ISCO处接近1（0.95–0.99），显著高于Kerr的0.78–0.94；势阱深度更小，轨道更靠近分界线；磁场可使方位角频率小于径向频率并产生远距离环状轨道；相同磁场下D=5引力波周期更长、频率更低（如βM=0.02时主谱线为7.9 mHz vs Kerr的9.9 mHz）、振幅低约18%；辐射反应寿命为小时至数天量级；LISA信噪比为7–31（D=5）vs 15–54（Kerr）。

Conclusion: 五维旋转黑洞在均匀磁场中的带电粒子轨道与引力波特征具有明显区别于Kerr黑洞的维度依赖性，虽信噪比略低，但仍处于LISA可观测范围，为高维引力模型提供了潜在观测判据。

Abstract: We study periodic orbits of charged particles in the equatorial plane of a five-dimensional ($D=5$) rotating Myers--Perry black hole (BH) immersed in a uniform magnetic field, and the gravitational waves emitted by these orbits in an extreme mass-ratio inspiral. All results are compared with the Kerr BH in the uniform magnetic field. We find that a charged particle is much more weakly bound to the $D=5$ dimensional BH, i.e. its energy at the innermost stable circular orbit is close to unity, $0.95$--$0.99$, compared with $0.78$--$0.94$ for the Kerr BH, and the depth of the potential well is several times smaller. As a result, the periodic orbits of the $D=5$ dimensional BH lie much closer to the separatrix. The magnetic field can make the azimuthal frequency of the orbit smaller than the radial one and produces loops in the orbits far from the BH. Using the numerical kludge method, we compute the gravitational waveforms for a compact object of $10M_\odot$ orbiting a BH of $10^6M_\odot$. At the same magnetic field, the $D=5$ dimensional signal has a longer period, lower frequencies, and an amplitude smaller by about $18\%$. We show that for the magnetic parameter $βM=0.02$ the strongest spectral line is at $7.9$\,mHz, compared with $9.9$\,mHz for the Kerr BH. An estimate of the radiation reaction shows that the periodic orbits survive from less than an hour to a few days in the presence of an external magnetic field. Over these lifetimes, the signal-to-noise ratio for LISA is $7$--$31$ for the rotating BH in $D=5$ dimensions, compared to the Kerr BH where it is about $15$--$54$.

</details>


### [118] [Phase-Space Constraints on a General Class of Eckart-like Viscous Cosmologies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35405)
*David R. Moreno,J. Bayron Orjuela-Quintana,Yeinzon Rodriguez*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文通过动力学系统分析研究了Eckart形式下具有体粘滞效应的暗物质宇宙学模型，发现其背景演化必须紧密跟踪标准ΛCDM模型，因而难以解决H₀张力，但在线性扰动层面，体粘滞可通过尺度依赖的耗散阻力抑制结构增长。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究粘滞暗物质模型能否在保持与观测一致的背景演化的同时，缓解如H₀张力等宇宙学疑难，并理解其对结构形成的物理影响。

Method: 采用动力学系统方法分析一类广义粘滞暗物质模型，在Eckart框架下以无量纲体粘滞压强Ω_Π(Ω_m)为特征函数；结合幂律参数化Ω_Π = -ξ̂₀ Ω_m^s进行具体验证，并分析相空间中的固定点及其稳定性、初值演化行为和线性扰动效应。

Result: 1）可行的辐射主导早期要求完全无粘滞；2）物质主导时期需小粘滞压强；3）唯一物理解的晚期吸引子是宇宙常数主导不动点，故背景演化几乎等同ΛCDM；4）当s < 1时模型出现非物理解（负密度、复粘滞压强）；5）线性扰动下，体粘滞在亚视界尺度引入耗散阻力，增强哈勃摩擦，抑制结构增长。

Conclusion: 粘滞暗物质模型虽不能显著改善背景演化以缓解H₀张力，但因其在扰动层面可有效抑制结构增长，仍具理论价值和观测区分潜力。

Abstract: We study the cosmological dynamics of a general class of viscous dark matter models using a dynamical system analysis. In the Eckart formalism, these viscous cosmologies are characterized by a dimensionless bulk viscous pressure $Ω_Π\equiv Ω_Π(Ω_m)$, where $Ω_m$ is the matter density parameter. Our analysis demonstrates that a viable early radiation era requires the complete absence of viscosity, a proper matter-dominated epoch demands a small viscous pressure, and the only physically admissible late-time attractor is the cosmological constant-dominated fixed point; consequently, the background expansion must closely track standard $Λ$CDM evolution. We validate these general results using the power-law parameterization $Ω_Π= -\hatξ_0 Ω_m^s$, where $\hatξ_0$ and $s$ are real parameters; for $s \geq 1$ and $\hatξ_0 \ll 1$, the model reproduces the $Λ$CDM background, while for $s < 1$ the system encounters a past-time integration boundary where the dark matter density transitions into negative values and its viscous pressure evaluates to nonphysical complex numbers. Since the background expansion essentially duplicates $Λ$CDM, the model is unlikely to resolve the $H_0$ tension. However, large differences arise at the linear perturbative level; indeed, on sub-horizon scales, bulk viscosity causes a scale-dependent dissipative drag on the matter density fluctuations; this increases the Hubble friction, offering a channel to suppress structure growth.

</details>


### [119] [Decoupled asymmetric thin-shell wormholes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35407)
*A. López-Hormazábal,Hishan Farfán-Bachiloglu,Kazuharu Bamba,Francisco Tello-Ortiz*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文构建了反射不对称的薄壳虫洞，通过将Schwarzschild外部时空与其最小几何形变（共享质量和红移函数）拼接实现，不对称性完全由引力解耦部分承载；形变侧为由耦合非线性电动力学的实标量场源的黑洞；研究了壳层与体部的径向零能量总和恒等式及其正定条件、动量流与体部径向零能量密度关系，并给出了线性稳定性判据；证明常规壳层响应下的稳定性需体部在喉部违反径向零能量条件，而形变可扩大稳定区域并使过渡半径移入光子球内；对Kerr时空的稳态推广中，拼接条件在自旋参数下精确求解，确定了角动量在壳层与体部间的分配及壳层类旋转流体行为；并基于自由进动模型估算了喉部的引力波信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建反射不对称薄壳虫洞，探索引力解耦方法在虫洞构造中的应用，并研究其能量条件、稳定性及可观测引力波信号。

Method: 采用最小几何形变与Schwarzschild时空拼接构造不对称薄壳虫洞；分析径向零能量条件、动量通量关系；推导线性稳定性判据；将模型推广至Kerr时空并精确求解拼接条件；建立自由进动模型估算引力波信号。

Result: 发现总径向零能量可为正；动量流等于体部径向零能量密度；获得闭式线性稳定性条件；证明形变可扩大稳定区域并使过渡半径进入光子球；Kerr推广中精确确定角动量分配与壳层流体行为；给出喉部引力波信号估计。

Conclusion: 引力解耦形变能有效改善薄壳虫洞的稳定性，放宽对体部能量条件的苛刻要求，并为探测类虫洞结构提供潜在引力波信号特征。

Abstract: We construct reflection-asymmetric thin-shell wormholes by joining a Schwarzschild exterior to its minimal geometric deformation, which shares the same mass and redshift function, so that the asymmetry is carried entirely by the gravitational-decoupling sector. The deformed side is a black hole sourced by a scalar field coupled to nonlinear electrodynamics, in its branch with a real scalar. We show that the total radial null energy of shell and bulk obeys an exact identity and becomes positive for a sufficiently strong deformation. The momentum flux across the shell equals the radial null-energy density of the bulk, and retaining it yields the linear stability condition in closed form. We prove that stability with a conventional shell response requires the bulk to violate the radial null energy condition at the throat; the deformation instead enlarges the stable domain and moves the transition radius inside the photon sphere. For a stationary extension built on Kerr, the junction is solved exactly in the spin, which fixes how the angular momentum is shared between shell and bulk and where the shell behaves as a rotating fluid. A free-precession model provides an estimate of the gravitational-wave signal of the throat.

</details>


### [120] [Quasibound modes of rotating acoustic black holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35493)
*H. S. Vieira,V. B. Bezerra*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种描述球坐标系下旋转声学黑洞的度规，并精确解析求解了该背景下无质量Klein-Gordon方程，进而导出准束缚态频率的解析表达式，为类比黑洞系统中波传播、模式稳定性和能量囚禁研究提供了理论基础与实验联系桥梁。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准束缚态能刻画声学黑洞类比系统中长期存在的共振模，有助于理解波传播、模式稳定性及能量囚禁；同时，声学黑洞是可实验实现的视界物理模型，研究其准束缚态可连接理论预测与实验室观测，深化对引力黑洞现象的理解。

Method: 构建球坐标系下旋转声学黑洞的度规，精确解析求解对应背景时空中的无质量Klein-Gordon方程，并施加适当边界条件推导准束缚态频率的解析表达式。

Result: 获得了准束缚态频率的精确解析表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作为声学黑洞类比系统中准束缚态的研究提供了严格解析框架，架起了理论黑洞物理与实验室可实现类比系统之间的桥梁。

Abstract: In this paper, we introduce a metric describing rotating acoustic black holes in spherical coordinates. This formulation allows us to obtain exact analytical solutions of the massless Klein--Gordon equation governing scalar perturbations in the corresponding background spacetime. By imposing the appropriate boundary conditions, we derive an exact analytical expression for the quasibound state frequencies of the system. Quasibound states play a central role in the dynamics of black hole analog systems, as they characterize long-lived resonant modes trapped in the vicinity of the effective horizon. Their investigation provides valuable insights into wave propagation, mode stability, and energy confinement in analog spacetimes. Moreover, because acoustic black holes offer experimentally accessible realizations of horizon physics, the study of quasibound states may establish a direct connection between theoretical predictions and laboratory observations, thereby contributing to a deeper understanding of phenomena that also arise in gravitational black hole environments.

</details>


### [121] [Gravitationally Induced Polarization-Angle Rotation in Pulsar-Black Hole Binaries](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35589)
*Jyotijwal Debnath,Amodio Carleo,Delphine Perrodin,Pratik Tarafdar*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了黑洞伴星对脉冲星偏振轮廓的影响，结合旋转矢量模型（RVM）与测地线数值积分，分析引力光弯曲、参考系拖曳和平行输运对偏振位置角（PPA）轨道相位依赖性的影响，发现其可显著扭曲典型S形PPA曲线，甚至反转变化趋势，为探测脉冲星-黑洞双星几何结构提供新途径。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索脉冲星-黑洞双星系统中广义相对论效应对偏振观测特征的影响，以期为未来此类系统的探测提供理论依据和诊断工具。

Method: 将旋转矢量模型（RVM）与零测地线数值积分相结合，计算光子传播路径上的偏振方向；分别考虑非自转与自转黑洞情形，并纳入相对论性自旋-轨道耦合引起的轨道几何演化。

Result: 引力光弯曲在近上合点附近显著扭曲S形PPA轮廓，甚至在超大质量黑洞系统中反转其整体变化趋势；参考系拖曳和平行输运引入额外几何偏移与偏振旋转；自旋-轨道演化亦调制PPA的轨道相位依赖性。

Conclusion: 相对论性光子传播可同时改变脉冲星偏振轮廓的形状与相位依赖关系，有望成为探测脉冲星-黑洞双星空间几何结构的有效探针。

Abstract: Radio pulsars emit highly polarized radiation whose polarization properties are commonly described by the Rotating Vector Model (RVM). In this work, we investigate the effects of gravitational light bending and parallel transport on the polarization profile of a pulsar in a hypothetical binary system with a black hole (BH) companion. We combine the RVM with numerical integration of null geodesics to calculate the polarization direction along the photon trajectory and model the orbital-phase-dependent variation of the observed polarization position angle (PPA). We consider both non-spinning and spinning black hole companions and include the evolution of the orbital geometry due to relativistic spin-orbit coupling. For a non-spinning black hole, the change in the observed polarization direction arises from the geometric shift associated with gravitational light bending. For a spinning black hole, frame dragging introduces an additional geometric shift, while parallel transport produces an additional polarization rotation measured relative to our chosen reference construction. We investigate the dependence of these polarization changes on the binary parameters and the orientation of the pulsar spin. We find that gravitational light bending can significantly distort the characteristic S-shaped PPA profile, particularly near superior conjunction, and can reverse its overall sense of variation for the supermassive black hole system considered here. Relativistic spin-orbit evolution also affects the orbital-phase dependence of the polarization profile, particularly in the supermassive black hole system. These results show that relativistic photon propagation can modify both the shape and the phase dependence of pulsar polarization profiles and may provide a useful probe of the geometry of pulsar-BH binaries when such systems are discovered.

</details>


### [122] [Nonlinear Tides of Binary Neutron Stars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35602)
*Guangzhou Guo,Zhen Pan*

Main category: gr-qc

TL;DR: 本文研究了非线性潮汐对中子星双星并合过程中引力波信号的影响，通过流体动力学模拟和模式耦合理论，发现非线性效应在晚期并合中可使基频降低约10%，并引入O(1)弧度的相位偏移，有望被下一代引力波探测器观测到。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性潮汐会耦合恒星振荡并重塑引力波信号，但其在强潮汐场下的行为超出线性微扰理论适用范围，需更精确建模。

Method: 采用流体动力学数值模拟结合二阶模式耦合模型，推导四极矩基模频率的非线性频移。

Result: 在晚期并合阶段，非线性潮汐使基模频率降低约10%（等质量双星），并产生O(1)弧度的额外引力波相位偏移；数值模拟与半解析模型在弱潮汐区一致，但在强场下显著偏离。

Conclusion: 非线性潮汐效应显著，是下一代引力波探测器的重要目标，有助于提升对中子星物态方程和强场引力的理解。

Abstract: Nonlinear tides couple stellar oscillations and reshape the gravitational wave (GW) signal from inspiralling binaries. We present hydrodynamic simulations of nonlinear tides in inspiralling neutron-star binaries and, within a mode-coupling model, derive a semi-analytic quadrupolar fundamental mode-frequency shift through second order in the tidal strength. The two approaches agree in the perturbative regime, but diverge at stronger tidal fields, where perturbation theory breaks down. In the late inspiral, nonlinear tides reduce the fundamental-mode frequency by ${\sim}10\%$ for equal-mass binaries and add a GW phase shift of $\mathcal{O}(1)$ radian beyond the linear tidal response. These effects make nonlinear tides a promising target for next-generation GW detectors.

</details>
