### [123] [One ring to rule them all: syzygy maximization for Argyres-Douglas](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33151)
*Monica Jinwoo Kang,Craig Lawrie,Jaewon Song*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种统一构造仿射概形的方法，用于编码Artyres-Douglas理论D_p(ADE)的Higgs分支和Macdonald指标，并通过单个不变多项式势的Jacobi环捕捉OPE解耦关系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为统一描述Artyres-Douglas理论的Higgs分支与Macdonald指标，并建立其与数学结构（如Jacobi环、Zhu C2-代数）的深刻联系。

Method: 利用Coxeter轨道空间的多项式扭曲周期构造不变多项式势；通过其Jacobi环的约化临界点得到Higgs分支；借助幂零根理想定义内在滤过以计算Macdonald指标；并将势限制在Slodowy切片上推广至幂零Higgs化理论。

Result: 获得经典型的行列式闭式表达及E6、E7、E8的具体势；验证了Schur与Hall-Littlewood极限的正确性；提出Jacobian环同构于边界适定水平下仿射VOA的Zhu C2-代数及其量子Drinfeld-Sokolov约化。

Conclusion: 该统一构造不仅系统刻画了AD理论的Higgs几何与超共形指标，还揭示了其与表示论、奇点理论及顶点代数间的深层对应。

Abstract: We propose a uniform construction of affine schemes encoding the Higgs branches and Macdonald indices of the Argyres--Douglas theories $\mathcal{D}_p(ADE)$, with $\gcd(p,h^\vee)=1$. The construction captures conjectural OPE decoupling relations via the Jacobian ring of a single invariant polynomial potential, obtained from a polynomial twisted period of the corresponding Coxeter orbit space. We show that these potentials are ``syzygy-maximizing'' in the first jet schemes and recover the Higgs branches as their reduced critical loci. We obtain closed determinant expressions for the classical types and explicit potentials for $E_6$, $E_7$, and $E_8$. Powers of the nilradical define an intrinsic filtration on the resulting Jacobian rings, from which we compute candidate Macdonald indices with the correct Schur and Hall--Littlewood limits, in the examples studied. Restricting the potentials to Slodowy slices extends the prescription to nilpotently Higgsed theories. We further conjecture that these Jacobian rings are isomorphic to Zhu's $C_2$-algebras of simple affine VOAs at boundary admissible levels and their quantum Drinfeld--Sokolov reductions.

</details>


### [124] [Construction of holomorphic SCFTs with minimal index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33165)
*Du Pei*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文构建了大量具有N=1超对称性和Witten指数为1的全纯顶点算子代数（VOA），解决了关于任意正中心荷c是否存在此类VOA的开放问题；特别是构造了c=288的理论，并通过三元码、AI辅助搜索与转移矩阵等技术，实现了高效构造和指数计算。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决是否存在具有N=1超对称性和Witten指数为1的全纯VOA这一长期开放问题，尤其关联TMF谱的576周期性及Segal-Stolz-Teichner猜想。

Method: 扩展Gaiotto与Johnson-Freyd基于三元码的方法；引入AI编码代理进行计算机辅助搜索；采用链式小单元构造三元码以突破指数时间障碍；利用转移矩阵技术计算指标；结合对称 orbifold 构造将k=12、24归约为k=6；并借助码与Landau-Ginzburg orbifold模型的联系分析非手征双VOA的超对称能隙化。

Result: 成功构造数千个满足条件的全纯N=1 VOA（含c=288）；对每个正整数k，构造出中心荷c=12k、指标为24/gcd(24,k)的VOA（达猜想最小值）；建立若干关于三元码及其指标的新结果；论证其非手征双VOA可超对称地完全能隙化。

Conclusion: 证实了对任意正中心荷c存在具有N=1超对称性和Witten指数1的全纯VOA；该工作不仅推进了VOA理论与拓扑模形式的联系，也为超对称凝聚态系统（如能隙化机制）提供了新工具与视角。

Abstract: It has been an open question whether there exist holomorphic VOAs with N=1 supersymmetry and Witten index 1 for any positive central charge c. The existence of such a theory with c=288 is closely related to the 576-periodicity of the TMF spectrum via the Segal-Stolz-Teichner conjecture. Here we report the construction of thousands of such theories by extending the approach initiated by Gaiotto and Johnson-Freyd via ternary codes. In addition, for every positive integer k, we also construct holomorphic N=1 VOAs of central charge c=12k with index 24/gcd(24,k) -- conjectured to be the minimum -- in a computer-aided search carried out by AI coding agents. We overcome the exponential-time barrier by using chains of smaller cells to build ternary codes, whose indices can then be evaluated via a transfer-matrix technique, and we use a symmetric orbifold construction to reduce the problem of k=12 and 24 to that of k=6. Along the way, we also establish several results on ternary codes and their indices. Using an observation relating codes and Landau-Ginzburg orbifold models, we argue that the non-chiral doubles of the ternary code VOAs can always be gapped supersymmetrically.

</details>


### [125] [The Free Scalar as a Thermal Envelope for Three-Dimensional CFTs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33179)
*Christopher P. Herzog,Andreas Stergiou*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出并检验了一个关于三维共形场论（CFT）中热熵密度与应力张量两点函数系数比值的上界猜想，并将其推广到超曲面 $S^1 \times H^2$ 上的归一化热容界限，结合共形碰撞器正定性、大N展开及全息对偶等方法进行验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 寻找三维单位模共形场论中基本物理量（如热熵密度s和应力张量系数C_T）之间普适的定量关系，以揭示CFT的通用结构约束。

Method: 基于共形碰撞器正定性推导近共形温度下的热容界；结合自由场、大N展开、全息对偶（AdS/CFT）及蒙特卡洛数值模拟（Ising与O(N)模型）进行多角度检验。

Result: 提出并验证了c_S/C_T ≤ 16πζ(3) 的平坦空间熵界，及其在S^1×H^2上的热容加强形式；该界在自由标量场取等号，并被各类理论（含数值模拟）所满足。

Conclusion: 该熵/热容上界可能是所有三维单位模CFT的普适性质，为理解CFT相空间结构和强耦合动力学提供了新约束。

Abstract: We conjecture that every unitary three-dimensional conformal field theory obeys $c_S/C_T\leq16πζ(3)$, where $s=c_ST^2$ is the thermal entropy density and $C_T$ the stress-tensor two-point coefficient, with equality for free scalars. We embed this in a stronger conjecture: on $S^1\times H^2$, the $C_T$-normalized hyperbolic heat capacity is bounded above by that of the free scalar for all $T\geq(2πR)^{-1}$. Near the conformal temperature, for theories smooth from the high-temperature side and without boundary terms in the modular Hamiltonian, the heat-capacity bound follows from conformal-collider positivity. We test the conjectures in free, interacting large-$N$, and holographic theories. Monte Carlo data for the three-dimensional Ising and $O(N)$ models also obey the flat-space bound.

</details>


### [126] [Spin-Induced Coordinate Noncommutativity of the Covariant Centroid](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33236)
*Daniel Guimaraes Tedesco,Luiz Paulo Colatto*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了具有自旋的重粒子Tulczyjew-Dixon质心坐标的非对易代数结构，明确了其精确适用域（凸类时内域Ω_μ），并提出‘二算子有效、三算子失效’的判据来界定各种构造（如投影、Moyal纤维、非结合积等）的有效性边界；所有结果均不超出标准QED框架。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 澄清Tulczyjew-Dixon质心坐标非对易代数的严格适用范围与有效性条件，解决此前因粗略固定p²而导致的代数闭合性与Jacobi恒等式失效问题。

Method: 基于正则Poincaré变量构建复合质心，分析其坐标泊松括号代数；通过Jacobi恒等式闭合性要求确定p⁻²必须保持算符性；系统检验不同近似（如壳上冻结p²）对二元/三元算符关系的影响，并逐层评估正能投影、Moyal纤维、Compton路径全息等几何构造是否满足‘二-三判据’。

Result: 确立了非对易代数的精确定义域Ω_μ；证明壳上冻结p²不改变任意两算符对易子，但破坏三算符Jacobi恒等式，残留项为2ε^{μνρσ}W_σ/(p²)²；给出各几何构造是否满足有效性的明确判定；最终物理振幅退化为标准QED结果。

Conclusion: Tulczyjew-Dixon质心的非对易结构虽具内在几何美感，但其所有可观测量和物理预言均未超越QED；‘二算子安全、三算子失效’是判断相关构造是否自洽的核心准则。

Abstract: The Tulczyjew-Dixon centroid of a massive spinning particle, built as a composite of canonical Poincaré variables, carries the exact coordinate algebra $\{X^μ,X^ν\}=-S^{μν}/p^2$, whose noncommutativity bivector is the transverse spin tensor of the particle itself rather than an external background. That bracket and its Compton-scale magnitude are already known, and we recover them here as the starting point of the analysis, not as a result. What the composite realization settles is the domain on which the algebra may be used. Closure of the Jacobi identity requires $p^{-2}$ to remain operator valued, and the exactness domain is the convex timelike interior $Ω_μ$, on which states supported on a single mass shell are distributional. We show that freezing $p^2$ to its shell value inside the centroid leaves every two-operator commutator unchanged on shell, so that the projected position dictionary, the Berry cancellation and the endpoint transversality identity are untouched, while the same substitution fails from three insertions onward, with residue the truncated Jacobiator $2ε^{μνρσ}W_σ/(p^2)^2$. This two-versus-three criterion replaces the qualitative restriction to shell-preserving matrix elements and states which constructions built on a fixed-shell bivector $Θ_m$ remain valid. We then follow the algebra through positive-energy projection, packet-conditioned Moyal fibers, Compton routing holonomy and projected nonassociative products, recording for each layer whether it falls on the safe side of the criterion. No prediction beyond QED follows. In the geometric branch the physical amplitude reduces to the QED amplitude, and the transverse matching coefficients remain uncomputed.

</details>


### [127] [Berends-Giele currents for higher derivative gauge theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33693)
*Pratik Chattopadhyay*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文推导了高导数标量QED（HDQED）和高导数Yang-Mills（HDYM）理论中的Berends-Giele（BG）流，验证了其满足Ward恒等式，并发现当所有外线为无质量胶子时，HDYM的BG流与纯Yang-Mills相同；而当包含Merlin模时，高导数效应才显现。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建高导数场论中高效计算散射振幅的工具，特别是推广Berends-Giele递归方法至含高阶导数项的规范理论，并检验其一致性与新物理特征（如Merlin模）

Method: 基于高导数拉格朗日量推导传播子与顶点，采用费曼图法和perturbiner形式主义分别计算BG电流，利用BG递归关系证明Ward恒等式及与标准理论的等价性，并分析Merlin模参与的振幅

Result: 1) 成功构造HDQED与HDYM的BG电流并验证Ward恒等式；2) 证明所有外线为无质量胶子时HDYM BG电流等于纯YM结果；3) Merlin模导致非平凡高导数效应，且单Merlin树图振幅为零，三、四Merlin振幅与先前结果一致

Conclusion: BG递归可成功推广至高导数规范理论，揭示了在特定外线条件下对低导数理论的‘隐藏一致性’，同时Merlin模成为探测高导数物理的关键通道；该框架有望延拓至高导数引力与离壳色-动量对偶研究。

Abstract: We derive Berends-Giele (BG) currents for higher derivative scalar QED (HDQED) and Yang-Mills (HDYM). In HDQED, we first compute the propagators and the vertices from the higher derivative Lagrangian and discuss the pole structures of the photon and the scalar. We then compute the BG currents using the Feynman diagram setup and prove the Ward identities for these currents at two and three points. Next, we used the perturbiner formalism to re-compute these currents and show that they are consistent with the Feynman diagram approach. In a similar fashion, we derive the BG currents in higher derivative Yang-Mills and find that remarkably, these currents are equal to pure Yang-Mills currents when all external on-shell legs are massless gluons. We prove this in all generality using the BG recursion and explicitly show this at lower points. However, the genuine higher derivative structure enters when one or many on-shell states are the so called massive Merlin modes. Using the multi-particle currents, we reproduce the result that all tree level amplitudes with one Merlin particle vanishes and the three and four Merlin amplitudes match the previously computed result. The future prospects of BG recursion for higher derivative gravity and off-shell colour- kinematics duality is also discussed.

</details>


### [128] [Fixed Points of the Yang-Mills Gradient Flow in the Vortex Sector](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33874)
*A. M. Silva*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了SU(2)规范理论中中心涡旋的杨-米尔斯梯度流行为，发现轴对称涡旋的演化可精确简化为线性扩散方程；高斯型核心保持形状但宽度随时间增长，磁通守恒；单圈涡旋是边缘稳定的Abelian核心，而多圈涡旋具有不稳定性，梯度流最终驱使所有涡旋趋向于模2余数决定的Abelian核心。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解中心涡旋在Yang-Mills梯度流下的稳定性与演化行为，特别是其非阿贝尔结构如何随流演化，以及涡旋拓扑（如缠绕数）对动力学的影响。

Method: 利用轴对称性将Yang-Mills梯度流约化为径向扩散方程；分析扰动谱，引入径向Schrödinger算子；采用变分法和自相似变量求解不稳定性模式；结合标度论证与几何解释分析涡旋演化路径。

Result: 证明薄涡旋是唯一有界的不动点但非吸引子；Abelian核心对任意n≥1存在快子模；自相似变量下得到闭式不稳定模及指数μ_{n,m}=n−1+m；多圈涡旋不稳定，单圈涡旋边缘稳定且保持Abelian；梯度流使任意涡旋趋于n mod 2决定的Abelian核心，非Abelian结构仅为瞬态。

Conclusion: Yang-Mills梯度流不保留高阶拓扑结构，而是通过几何上缩短SU(2)中环路全息路径的方式，将多圈涡旋‘解缠’为模2等价的Abelian核心，揭示了非Abelian涡旋的瞬态本质与拓扑简化机制。

Abstract: We study the Yang-Mills gradient flow of center vortices in SU(2) gauge theory. For axially symmetric vortices the flow reduces exactly to a linear diffusion equation for the radial profile: a Gaussian core keeps its shape while its width grows as $\sqrt{R^2+4t}$, the flux is exactly conserved, and the thin vortex is the unique bounded fixed point, though not an attractor. Off-diagonal perturbations are governed by a pair of radial Schrödinger operators, and a variational computation shows that, frozen at any instant, the Abelian core has a tachyonic mode for every winding $n\geq 1$. Since the core spreads while the mode grows, we pass to self-similar variables, where the unstable modes are known in closed form, with growth exponents $μ_{n,m}=n-1+m$. Multiply wound vortices are therefore unstable, while the fundamental vortex is exactly marginal and stays Abelian. A scaling argument rules out any static vortex in the transverse plane, and the instability acquires a simple geometric meaning that shortens the path traced in SU(2) by the holonomy of a growing loop, lowering the winding by two. The flow thus drives every vortex toward the Abelian core of winding $n \bmod 2$, with non-Abelian structure appearing only as the transient through which multiply wound cores unwind.

</details>


### [129] [Exact Stress Tensor Flows in Causal Electrodynamics: Courant-Hilbert Approach](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33887)
*Zahra Pezhman,Komeil Babaei Velni,H. Babaei-Aghbolagh*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了单维度耦合理论中能量-动量张量的结构，发现其二次不变量X和Y仅通过比值Y/X决定流方程，导出了不显含耦合参数的自主流∂_λℒ = X ℱ(Y/X)，并解析求解了四个典型例子（广义Born-Infeld、q-变形族、无τ极大值理论、Russo-Townsend对数理论），分析了ℱ的奇点与临界场、能量条件及强场行为的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解单维度耦合有效场论中能量-动量张量约束下的普适流结构，揭示应力张量不变量间内在关系及其物理含义（如临界场、因果性、能量条件）。

Method: 利用Courant-Hilbert函数单耦合特性，推导出由τℓ̇和ℓ−τℓ̇控制的能量-动量张量结构；将二次不变量X和Y约化为单变量Y/X，建立自治微分方程；对四类具体非线性电磁理论求解ℱ的闭式表达并分析其解析性质（如Lambert W函数分支、幂级数展开奇偶性）。

Result: 获得普适自主流方程∂_λℒ = X ℱ(Y/X)；给出四个理论的ℱ闭式解：广义Born-Infeld（ℱ为Y/X的二次函数）、q-变形族（q=1/2时奇次项消失）、无τ极大值理论、Russo-Townsend对数理论（涉及Lambert W函数下实支）；指出ℱ奇点对应各理论的临界场与强场渐近行为。

Conclusion: 单维度耦合非线性电磁理论的RG流具有高度普适结构，仅由X和Y的比值驱动；ℱ的解析形式编码了理论的关键物理性质（因果性、能量条件、紫外行为），为分类和构造新理论提供了统一框架。

Abstract: It has been shown that for theories whose Courant-Hilbert function depends on a single dimensionful coupling, the energy-momentum tensor is controlled by the two combinations $τ\dot{\ell}$ and $\ell-τ\dot{\ell}$, so that the quadratic invariants $X=T_{μν}T^{μν}$ and $Y=(T^μ_μ)^2$ depend on a single auxiliary variable. Eliminating this variable in favour of $Y/X$ yields the exact autonomous flow $\partial_λ\mathcal L=X\,\mathcal F(Y/X)$, with no explicit dependence on the coupling, the electromagnetic invariants or the CH parameter, on every branch where the map is invertible. The expansion of $\mathcal F$ in powers of $\sqrt{Y/X}$ shows which flows are built from integer powers of $X$ and $Y$ and which require half-integer powers. In this paper, we work out four examples in closed form, each with its analytic structure and physical interpretation: the generalized Born-Infeld theory, whose flow is quadratic in the stress tensor; the $q$-deformed family, in which the odd coefficients of the expansion vanish at $q=1/2$ and are nonzero otherwise; the no-$τ$-maximum theory; and the causal Russo-Townsend logarithmic theory, whose flow involves the lower real branch of the Lambert $W$ function and reproduces the known perturbative result. The singular points of $\mathcal{F}$ are related to the critical fields, energy conditions, and strong-field behavior of each theory.

</details>


### [130] [Holographic entropy inequalities & typical states](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33905)
*Guglielmo Grimaldi,Matthew Headrick*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了Ryu-Takayanagi公式导出的AdS量子引力中静态半经典态的纠缠熵线性不等式性质的普适性，分析其在RT公式适用范围外（如非静态或非经典引力情形）以及非全息态中是否依然成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探究Ryu-Takayanagi公式所导出的纠缠熵线性不等式在更广泛物理情境（如脱离静态、经典爱因斯坦引力假设，甚至非全息系统）下的适用边界与普适性。

Method: 综述与概念分析，梳理现有理论结果并提出关键问题。

Result: 总结了当前关于这些不等式在不同引力与量子态设定下成立与否的部分已知结论。

Conclusion: 这些不等式并非普遍成立，其有效性高度依赖于RT公式的适用条件；在更一般情形（如动力学态、量子修正显著或非全息系统）中可能失效，有待进一步研究。

Abstract: As a consequence of the Ryu-Takayanagi formula, static semiclassical states in AdS quantum gravity share a set of special properties expressible as linear inequalities on their entanglement entropies. How general are these properties? Do they still hold when we move away from the regime of validity of RT, i.e. static states in classical Einstein gravity? Do they perhaps hold even for some class of non-holographic states? We will motivate these questions and review what's known about the answers.

</details>


### [131] [Revisiting monodromy defects in $\text{O}(N)$ conformal field theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33972)
*Jiechao Feng,Nayan Myerson-Jain,G. Shankar,Taige Wang,Meng Cheng*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究O(N)共形场论中的单值性缺陷，特别是部分单值性缺陷，并通过重整化群和大N展开发展了缺陷相的解析理论；在3≤d<4维中识别出多个缺陷CFT，并通过蒙特卡洛模拟验证了理论预测。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单值性缺陷是具有全局对称性的扩展量子系统中的基本对象，本文旨在理解其在O(N) CFT中的行为及其相结构。

Method: 结合重整化群方法与大N展开发展解析理论，并利用改进格点模型进行大规模经典蒙特卡洛模拟。

Result: 在3≤d<4维中识别出多个由不同边界条件描述的缺陷CFT；在d=3附近的大N极限下，发现一个由‘交替’边界条件表征的稳定缺陷CFT；数值模拟结果定性支持该理论预言。

Conclusion: O(N) CFT中部分单值性缺陷存在稳定的缺陷CFT相，其性质可通过解析理论与数值模拟共同刻画。

Abstract: Monodromy defects are fundamental objects in extended quantum systems with global symmetries. We study such defects in $\text{O}(N)$ conformal field theories (CFT), which describe the universality class of $\text{O}(N)$ spontaneous symmetry breaking transitions. In particular, we consider partial-monodromy defects, whose corresponding symmetry transformations only act on a subset of the fundamental $\text{O}(N)$ vector field. We develop an analytical theory of the defect phases, using both renormalization group and large $N$ expansion. In general (spacetime) dimensions $3\leq d<4$, multiple defect CFTs are identified, which are described by appropriate boundary conditions for the vector field near the defect. Near $d=3$, we show that in the large $N$ limit, a stable defect CFT always exists, characterized by the so-called ("standard") "alternate" boundary conditions for the (twisted) untwisted fields, whose defect correlations are (suppressed) enhanced. We then perform large-scale classical Monte Carlo simulations of monodromy defects in the O(3) CFT in an improved lattice model and numerically extract the defect scaling dimensions. The numerical results are in qualitative agreement with the stable defect CFT identified in the field theory.

</details>


### [132] [Lifshitz Phase of Quantum Gravity: The Infrared Fixed Point of the (2+1)D Unimodular Hořava-Chern-Simons Model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34024)
*Maxim Zelenko,Dam Thanh Son*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种具有$w_\infty$规范对称性的二维空间加一维时间非相对论引力理论——幺模Hořava-Chern-Simons引力，并研究其在大Chern-Simons水平$|κ|$下的紫外与红外行为及可能的相变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建具有新规范对称性（$w_\infty$）和可控重整化行为的低维非相对论引力模型，探索其尺度不变性与相变特性。

Method: 基于(2+1)维非相对论时空框架，构造具$w_\infty$规范对称性的幺模Hořava-Chern-Simons引力作用量；通过大$|κ|$展开分析其RG流与临界指数$z$；推测有限$κ$下的相变行为。

Result: 发现该理论在大$|κ|$下存在渐近自由紫外不动点，并流向具有各向异性标度不变性的红外不动点，动力学临界指数为$z=2-\tfrac32|κ|^{-1}$；推测当$κ$低于某临界值时可能发生向禁闭相的相变。

Conclusion: 幺模Hořava-Chern-Simons引力提供了一个兼具可解RG流与潜在强耦合相变的非相对论引力范例，有助于理解量子引力中的对称性、标度律与相结构关系。

Abstract: We consider a nonrelativistic gravity theory in (2+1) dimensions with $w_\infty$ gauge symmetry that we call unimodular Hořava-Chern-Simons gravity. The theory has three dimensionless couplings, one of which is the Chern-Simons level $κ$. At large $|κ|$, the theory flows from an asymptotically free UV fixed point to an IR fixed point with anisotropic scale invariance characterized by a dynamical critical exponent $z=2-\tfrac32|κ|^{-1}$. We speculate that unimodular Hořava-Chern-Simons gravity undergoes a phase transition from this scale-invariant phase to a confining phase when $κ$ drops below a critical value.

</details>


### [133] [Perturbative Holonomy Potentials and Vacua in Chiral Supersymmetric Gauge Theories](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34240)
*Arash Arabi Ardehali*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文建立了在R^3×S^1上手征超对称规范理论中微扰D项数据的全局控制，提出了一种闭式、单圈精确的D项系数公式，并将其应用于提升3维Intriligator-Seiberg真空分类至紧致化情形，通过多个模型验证了该框架的有效性与重要性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为解决在R^3×S^1上手征超对称规范理论中，因紧致化导致的D项行为复杂性及真空结构难以全局刻画的问题，需发展能统一处理远离和靠近质量阈值区域的D项控制方法。

Method: 推导出包含四维Fayet-Iliopoulos参数的闭式、单圈精确D项系数；结合局部FI参数与Chern-Simons耦合的确定，实现对紧致全息空间上半经典真空的全局刻画；应用该框架分析阿贝尔模型与SU(2) Maruyoshi-Song理论的R-扭结约化。

Result: 成功将3维Intriligator-Seiberg真空分类提升至R^3×S^1；发现简单阿贝尔模型中存在异常丰富的真空结构；复现了SU(2) Maruyoshi-Song理论R-扭结约化到Gang-Yamazaki理论的结果；揭示真正真空可位于非平凡全息处，即使D系数在平凡全息处为零或在库仑切片中阈值非零。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了刻画紧致化下超对称理论真空结构的系统性工具，强调全局匹配的重要性，突破了仅依赖局部分析的局限，对理解AdS/CFT、真空选择及对偶性具有深远意义。

Abstract: We establish global control of perturbative D-term data governing semiclassical vacuum sectors in chiral supersymmetric gauge theories on $\mathbb{R}^3\times S^1$. A closed-form, one-loop-exact D-term coefficient, including 4d Fayet--Iliopoulos (FI) parameters, gives a uniformly controlled leading potential away from massless thresholds and fixes local FI and Chern--Simons couplings near them. This allows lifting the 3d Intriligator--Seiberg classification of semiclassical vacua to $\mathbb{R}^3\times S^1$ across the compact holonomy space. The framework is showcased by finding a remarkably rich set of vacuum sectors in a simple abelian model and reproducing on flat space the R-twisted reduction of an $\mathrm{SU}(2)$ Maruyoshi--Song theory to the Gang--Yamazaki theory, first found on $S^3\times S^1$. The examples demonstrate the stakes of global matching: true vacua may lie at nontrivial holonomy even with nonzero threshold values of the Coulomb-slice D-coefficient, and despite vanishing D-coefficient at trivial holonomy.

</details>


### [134] [All-plus Yang--Mills and gravity wavefunctions in de Sitter space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34261)
*Song He,Jiajie Mei,Yuyu Mo*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了四维德西特空间中树级全正波函数的缝合表示法，分别针对杨-米尔斯理论和爱因斯坦引力理论给出任意点数的公式，并从自对偶解出发进行推导。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示四维德西特空间中全正波函数的结构，特别是避免总能量极点并明确部分能量留数的物理起源。

Method: 基于自对偶杨-米尔斯和自对偶爱因斯坦理论的解，构造非交叉多边形剖分（杨-米尔斯）与带符号缝合树加局部陈-西蒙斯项（引力）的表示。

Result: 得到杨-米尔斯全正波函数的显式非交叉剖分表示；提出引力情形下含交替求和与局部Chern-Simons项的缝合公式，并给出至七点的显式表达式。

Conclusion: 缝合表示能自然展现全正波函数的能量结构特性，为德西特空间散射振幅研究提供了新工具和几何视角。

Abstract: We present sewing representations for tree-level all-plus wavefunctions in four-dimensional de Sitter space. For Yang--Mills theory, the arbitrary-multiplicity formula is a sum over noncrossing polygon dissections, with each cell carrying energy differences of a full lower-point wavefunction. The absence of a generic total-energy pole is manifest, while partial-energy residues are determined by the lower-point factors. We derive this representation from self-dual Yang--Mills solutions. For Einstein gravity, we propose an arbitrary-multiplicity formula consisting of an alternating sum over labelled sewing trees plus a local gravitational Chern--Simons term. This local term arises from the self-dual Einstein action and supplies boundary data not fixed by internal-energy cuts. We derive a generating formula for it from the boundary connection and give explicit expressions through seven points.

</details>


### [135] [Exact lattice bosonization of the double-scaled $c=1$ matrix model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34376)
*Adwait Gaikwad,Gautam Mandal,Ajay Mohan*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了c=1双缩放矩阵模型中纠缠熵（EE）与格点玻色子理论的关系，提出了一种基于位置依赖弦耦合的有限非均匀格点玻色子描述，并由此导出有限的EE及密度关联函数，区别于发散的集体变量理论。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决集体变量理论在计算纠缠熵和密度关联函数时出现的发散问题，建立更自然、有限的玻色化框架来描述c=1矩阵模型中的局域可观测量。

Method: 基于Wigner分布思想，构建具有位置依赖格点间距g_s(x)l_s的格点玻色子模型；在双缩放极限下推导纠缠熵、一阶和二阶费米子密度关联函数；对比集体变量理论的结果。

Result: 成功导出有限且显式的c=1模型纠缠熵和密度一、两点函数；揭示局域可观测量由尺度g_s(x)l_s刻画，而渐近S矩阵则由基本尺度l_s刻画；提出该现象可能对应D0膜物理图像。

Conclusion: 格点玻色子框架为c=1矩阵模型提供了更优的短距描述，统一处理局域可观测量并规避发散；其尺度分离特征暗示深层的全息或膜物理结构。

Abstract: In a recent paper by two of us, it has been shown that entanglement entropy (EE) of matrix quantum mechanics (or, equivalently, of a non-relativistic fermion model) has a natural interpretation in terms of an exact bosonization of the fermion theory in terms of a lattice boson with lattice spacing $\sim 1/N$ (where $N$ is the rank of the matrix, equal to the number of fermions). The theory of this lattice boson is distinct from a latticized version of the "collective variable'' theory which is formulated in terms of the fermion density $ρ(λ)$ ("collective variable''); it is, in fact, more closely related to the Wigner distribution of the fermions. In this paper, we develop this connection further for the $c=1$ double scaled matrix model. Even though $N$ becomes infinite, the double scaled theory is described by a boson on a finite and non-uniform lattice spacing, of order $\sim g_s(x) l_s$ (where $g_s(x) \equiv g_s {\rm cosech}^2(x/l_s)$ is a position dependent string coupling). We explicitly derive the $c=1$ EE, as well as the one and two-point functions of fermion density from the lattice boson. The results are explicitly finite, unlike the collective variable theory which is shown to yield divergent answers. Our results suggest that while local observables such as the EE and $\langle ρ(λ) \rangle$, are characterized by a unique short distance scale $g_s(x) l_s$, the asymptotic observables such as the S-matrix at every genus are characterized by a short distance scale $l_s$. We show how this arises and discuss a possible interpretation in terms of D0 brane physics.

</details>


### [136] [Spiralling into Dark Energy: Axio-Dilaton Attractors in Light of Current Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34380)
*Mario Ramos-Hamud*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文研究了弦论紧致化中轴子-胀子系统的宇宙学，发现其晚期吸引子具有非测地轨迹和特定状态方程，且当前观测数据略微偏好该模型而非ΛCDM。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在弦论紧致化中，胀子类模场总是与轴子配对，并通过弯曲的场空间度规相互作用，需研究其宇宙学行为。

Method: 采用动力学系统分析研究胀子沿带多项式修正的指数势滚动并耦合无质量轴子的动力学；结合DESI DR2 BAO、Pantheon超新星及Planck 2018距离先验进行贝叶斯分析。

Result: 晚期吸引子为场空间中的非测地轨迹，状态方程为ω_φ=(λ−2ξ)/(λ+2ξ)；加速膨胀在陡峭势下可能（当ξ≫λ）；贝叶斯分析给出λ≳7–9、log₁₀ξ≳2.5（95%置信），两参数存在简并；模型略优于ΛCDM。

Conclusion: 轴子-胀子系统可提供可行的暗能量机制，当前数据支持较大λ和ξ参数区域，但尚无法区分不同多项式修正形式。

Abstract: In string compactifications, dilaton-like moduli always come paired with axions, and the two fields interact through a curved field-space metric. We study the cosmology of such an axio-dilaton system, in which the dilaton rolls down an exponential potential corrected by a polynomial prefactor and couples to a massless axion through the kinetic function $W=e^{-ξφ_1/M_p}$. A dynamical systems analysis shows that the late-time attractor follows a non-geodesic trajectory in field space with equation of state $ω_φ=(λ-2ξ)/(λ+2ξ)$, so accelerated expansion is possible for steep potentials provided $ξ\ggλ$. No physical fixed point is phantom. A Bayesian analysis with DESI DR2 BAO, binned Pantheon supernovae and Planck 2018 distance priors gives $λ\gtrsim7$--$9$ and $\log_{10}ξ\gtrsim2.5$ at 95% confidence, together with a degeneracy between the two parameters. The model is slightly favoured over $Λ$CDM by current data, while the different polynomial corrections cannot yet be distinguished. This contribution is based on arXiv:2609.22539.

</details>


### [137] [Nonlinear Higher-Spin Theory in $O(4,1)$ de Sitter Space](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34475)
*O. A. Gelfond,M. A. Vasiliev*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了四种与O(4,1)德西特几何相容的高自旋规范理论版本，其中两种具有Z2×Z2李超代数结构，另两种在玻色子部分是幺正的，并在引入费米子时表现出伪对合性质，暗示可能与时间反演或时空加倍相关的（反）幺正性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建与德西特时空几何相容且包含费米子的高自旋规范理论，并探索其代数结构与幺正性特征。

Method: 基于O(4,1)对称性和Z2×Z2李超代数框架，构造四种高自旋规范理论模型；分析其玻色子幺正性及费米子引入后的伪对合性质。

Result: 得到四种新高自旋理论模型：两类具Z2×Z2超代数结构；两类在玻色子扇区幺正，并在含费米子时呈现伪对合，暗示潜在(反)幺正性。

Conclusion: 这些模型为德西特背景下的高自旋引力与超对称扩展提供了新代数结构和幺正性线索，尤其关联时间反演或时空加倍机制。

Abstract: Four versions of the higher-spin gauge theory compatible with $O(4,1)$ de Sitter geometry in presence of fermions are proposed. Two of them exhibit the structure of $\mathbb{Z}_2\times \mathbb{Z}_2$ Lie superalgebra proposed originally by one of the authors for the description of de Sitter supergravity \cite{Vasiliev:1985vtv}. Two of the proposed models are unitary in the bosonic sectors in analogy with the recent proposal by Lang and Neiman \cite{Lang:2026inn} and admit a pseudo involution in presence of fermions suggesting possible (anti)unitarity associated either with the time reversal in $dS_4$ or with the space-time doubling.

</details>


### [138] [Two-node Dualities from Elementary Dualities in 3d and 4d](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34752)
*Yuanyuan Fang,Sungjoon Kim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的三维N=2双节点Seiberg类对偶性，类比于四维N=1的ILS对偶性，并通过椭球面分割函数验证了其正确性，推导仅依赖于两个基本对偶性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 构建与四维ILS对偶性相对应的三维N=2对偶性，深化对偶性间关系的理解。

Method: 利用s-禁闭方法检验三维N=2椭球面分割函数的一致性，并基于Aharony及Benini-Benvenuti-Pasquetti对偶性进行推导；同时对原始ILS对偶性采用逐节点Seiberg与Kutasov-Schwimmer对偶、中间Higgs机制及重场积除等步骤重新推导。

Result: 成功提出并验证了一种新的三维N=2双节点Seiberg类对偶性；揭示了该对偶性可由两个基本三维对偶性导出；为原始ILS对偶性提供了基于逐节点对偶的新推导路径。

Conclusion: 三维N=2对偶性具有深层结构关联性，‘对偶性源于对偶性’的思想得到进一步支持；该工作拓展了低维超对称规范理论中对偶性的认知框架。

Abstract: A new 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ two-node Seiberg-like duality, analogous to the 4d $\mathcal{N}=1$ Intriligator--Leigh--Strassler~(ILS) duality, is proposed. As strong evidence, we check the agreement between the 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ ellipsoid partition functions for numerous cases using s-confinement method. Surprisingly, our derivation assumes only two elementary 3d $\mathcal{N}=2$ dualities: Aharony and Benini--Benvenuti--Pasquetti, which reinforces the idea dualities from dualities. We also revisit the original ILS duality and provide a derivation based on nodewise dualities. In particular, we apply a sequence of Seiberg and Kutasov--Schwimmer dualities to individual gauge nodes, with Higgsing at an intermediate step, and subsequently integrate out the massive fields.

</details>


### [139] [Reparametrisation invariance, anomalies and pseudoscalar states in massless quantum electrodynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34858)
*Joshua Cesca,Archil Kobakhidze,Elden Loomes*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了规范理论中轴向反常的分类与处理，提出通过轴向重参数化将全局轴向对称性提升为规范冗余，并利用该规范对称性消除无关反常，同时引入一个作为拉格朗日乘子的非传播赝标量场来实现轴向反常；在拓扑非平凡时空（如具有非平凡二阶上同调的空间）中，该轴向规范对称性可发生希格斯机制，使赝标量场成为涌现的传播自由度；文中还简要提及与共形反常相关的类似涌现态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决规范理论中因量子化引入的轴向与矢量流反常的分类模糊性，尤其是区分红外相关（相关）与重整化方案依赖（无关）的反常，并寻求一种能自然保留物理反常、消除人为反常的量子一致性框架。

Method: 采用轴向重参数化变换将费米子场的全局轴向对称性提升为局域规范对称性；在此扩展的规范框架下，要求量子理论保持该轴向规范对称性；结合微分几何与拓扑（特别是第二上同调群）分析其在非平凡时空中的对称性破缺行为（希格斯机制）。

Result: 1）无关反常被系统性消除；2）轴向反常由一个非传播赝标量拉格朗日乘子精确实现；3）在具有非平凡H²(M)的时空中，该赝标量场可通过轴向规范对称性的希格斯机制获得动力学，成为传播的涌现自由度；4）指出共形反常存在类似结构。

Conclusion: 轴向反常本质上是量子理论对规范对称性保持的要求所导致的必然结果，其表现形式（是否传播）取决于时空拓扑；该观点统一处理了反常的物理本质与数学结构，并暗示反常可能关联新的低能有效自由度。

Abstract: In electrodynamics, and more generally in gauge theories, quantisation introduces an ambiguity whereby the vector current, the axial current, or both may acquire an anomalous divergence. These anomalies can be classified as relevant, conventionally associated with the infrared properties of the theory, and irrelevant, arising from the freedom in the choice of renormalisation scheme. We show that, under a axial reparametrisation of the fermion fields, the global axial symmetry can be promoted to a gauge redundancy. Requiring this gauge symmetry to be preserved at the quantum level eliminates the irrelevant anomalies, leaving a non-propagating pseudoscalar field that acts as a Lagrange multiplier enforcing the familiar axial anomaly. We further argue that, on topologically non-trivial spacetimes, in particular those with non-trivial second cohomology, this axial gauge symmetry can undergo a Higgs mechanism, rendering the pseudoscalar field an emergent propagating degree of freedom. Finally, we briefly point out an analogous emergent state associated with the conformal anomaly.

</details>


### [140] [Graph-Based Algorithms for Worldline EFT Integrands](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35007)
*Gregor Kälin,Jungwon Lim*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种以图为中心的新型算法，用于构建和表示世界线有效场论（WEFT）的被积函数，将拓扑结构与代数结构解耦为独立的图-分子对；通过推广Berends-Giele构造、序列化传播子及“自拍”子图恒等式简化，实现被积函数的高效生成与规范表达，并自然推广积分族概念以优化IBP约化。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为提升世界线有效场论（WEFT）中高阶后闵可夫斯基（PM）圈图计算的效率与系统性，需分离被积函数的拓扑与代数结构，并支持更灵活、最小化的积分族定义与IBP约化。

Method: 提出图-分子表示法：图编码拓扑信息（传播子、源汇顶点、动量路由、i0规则、固有时依赖），分子仅含必要张量代数内容；设计广义Berends-Giele构造以减少张量缩并；引入传播子序列化与‘自拍’子图部分分式恒等式进行简化与规范；重新定义积分族与扇区，允许多重传播子标号与局部独立标量积。

Result: 实现了高效、规范的WEFT被积函数生成；显著减少IBP约化所需积分扇区数；支持最优扇区对称性利用与传播子标号独立优化；算法已集成于Rust库FeynRIR，可高效计算有效作用量变分、世界线运动方程及辐射反冲冲量。

Conclusion: 图-分子表示法为WEFT提供了结构清晰、计算高效且IBP友好的新框架，推动高精度引力波建模中后牛顿/后闵可夫斯基计算的自动化与规模化。

Abstract: We present novel graph-centered algorithms for the construction and representation of worldline effective field theory (WEFT) integrands that separate the topological and algebraic structure of loop integrands into independent graph-numerator pairs. In this representation, the graph encodes all propagators, worldline source and sink vertices, momentum routing, i0 prescriptions, and proper-time dependence, while the numerator carries only the minimal algebraic, tensorial content. To construct integrands at fixed post-Minkowskian order we propose a generalized Berends-Giele construction that minimizes the number of expensive tensor contraction operations. We also discuss simplification and canonicalization strategies through "serialization" of worldline propagators and the application of "selfie"-subdiagram partial-fraction identities, yielding a minimal canonical topology basis. We further show that the graph-numerator representation naturally generalizes the notion of an integral family and its sectors, relevant for integration-by-parts (IBP) reduction: propagators are not constrained to a single master labeling, and each sector carries its own minimal set of independent scalar products, completing into an overcomplete global family. This setup minimizes the total number of required integral sectors through an optimal use of sector symmetries and enables independent optimization of propagator labeling for IBP reductions. The algorithms are implemented in the Rust crate FeynRIR, which is currently able to efficiently compute integrands for the variation of the effective WEFT action, the equations of motion for the worldlines and the (radiation-reacted) impulse.

</details>


### [141] [Relating Dark Dimension Size with Dark Energy via 5D Quintessence Potential and Casimir Energy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35124)
*Rashmikanta Mantry,Kazuyuki Furuuchi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文探讨了暗维度（DD）场景下有效场论（EFT）的实现，重点研究Casimir能与5D幽灵势之间的竞争如何决定DD尺寸，并分析其对4D暗能量（DE）和真空能（VE）的影响；指出简单5D模型中VE无法充当标准宇宙学中的DE，主因在于径子对4D牛顿引力常数的依赖性；进而提出量子诱导主导的5D模型及固定总体积的6D模型以改善该问题，但后者在存在大尺度层级时需人为调谐才能匹配观测到的DE能标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决暗维度模型中4D真空能（VE）无法自然充当观测暗能量（DE）的问题，特别是径子对4D牛顿常数的强依赖性所导致的宇宙学不兼容性。

Method: 构建并比较多种高维有效场论模型：（1）基础5D模型，含5D幽灵势与质量为零场的Casimir能；（2）量子效应主导爱因斯坦-希尔伯特作用量的改进5D模型；（3）总内体积固定的6D模型，分等尺度与大层级两种情形分析。

Result: 发现基础5D模型所得VE不满足DE的宇宙学行为；改进5D模型可缓解径子依赖问题，获得更自洽的DE机制；6D等尺度模型未明确DD尺寸起源；6D大层级模型虽可解耦径子对牛顿常数的影响，但需人工减去常数项以匹配观测DE能标，破坏dSDC预期的标度关系。

Conclusion: 单纯几何+Casimir机制难以自然生成符合观测的暗能量；需引入量子诱导引力或更高维结构来弱化径子依赖；但精细调谐或UV补全仍不可避免，凸显暗维度解释暗能量的理论挑战。

Abstract: We explore effective field theory (EFT) realizations of the dark dimension (DD) scenario in which the size of the DD is determined by the competition between a 5D quintessence potential and the Casimir energy. When only contributions from massless fields are considered, the Casimir energy does not have a typical energy scale, and the 4D dark energy (DE) scale is set by the height of the 5D quintessence potential. The simple 5D model we first study has an attractive feature that the size of the DD is related to the 4D vacuum energy (VE), as outlined above. However, it turns out that the 4D VE thus obtained cannot work as the 4D DE in the standard cosmological setting. One of the discrepancies between the VE and the DE manifests in the radion dependence of the effective 4D Newton's gravitational constant. This leads us to explore a 5D model in which the 4D Einstein-Hilbert (EH) action is dominantly induced by quantum effects, leaving the radion dependence of the 4D Newton's constant subdominant. This model leads to an attractive cosmological model, realizing the mechanism explained in the beginning. We also explore a 6D model with the total internal volume fixed, to make the 4D Newton's constant independent of the radion. We first study a scenario in which the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions are of the same order. In this scenario, the physical origin of the size of the DD is left to its UV completion. Next, we turn to a scenario in which there is a large hierarchy between the sizes of the 5th and the 6th dimensions. In this scenario, the energy scale of the 5D quintessence potential turns out to be much higher than the energy scale of the 4D DE as inferred from the cosmological observations, so we need to subtract a constant term in 4D to tune the 4D DE. This prevents us from obtaining the scaling relation for the DE expected in the dSDC.

</details>


### [142] [Exact Stress Tensor Flows in Causal Electrodynamics: Auxiliary Field Formalism](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35135)
*Zahra Pezhman,H. Babaei-Aghbolagh,Komeil Babaei Velni*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于辅助场的因果自对偶非线性电动力学框架，研究了类似 $T\bar{T}$ 的非微扰流形变形，给出了精确的流方程、因果性约束及物理量（如能量条件、特征锥）与核函数的关系，并构造了多种具体模型的精确解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为系统理解非线性电动力学中因果性、自对偶性和$T\bar{T}$型无关算符变形之间的深层联系，需一个简洁且具普适性的形式化框架。

Method: 采用单辅助变量$\varphi$的线性Lagrangian表示，将动力学编码于势函数$W(\varphi)$；通过分析应力张量不变量比$r$导出精确流方程；结合Legendre变换、Hamiltonian形式与Born自对偶性进行结构分析。

Result: 得到自治形式的精确$T\bar{T}$类流方程$\partial_λ\mathcal{L}=X\,F(r)$；给出核函数$F(r)$与辅助势$W$的显式重构关系；导出因果性微分不等式$D(r)>1/(1-r)$及单调性要求；阐明$\varphi$-宇称、Lagrangian解析性与流偶性之间的对应；并为广义Born-Infeld等四类模型给出精确解。

Conclusion: 该辅助场形式为因果非线性电动力学提供了统一描述工具，揭示了$T\bar{T}$类变形、自对偶性与能量条件在几何与动力学层面的内在一致性。

Abstract: Causal self-dual nonlinear electrodynamics admits a simple auxiliary-field description: the Lagrangian is linear in the two Lorentz invariants of the field strength, and the dynamics is encoded in a potential $W(\varphi)$ of a single auxiliary variable. We study non-perturbative $T\bar T$-like flows of these theories in this formalism. The irrelevant (stress-tensor) deformation acts on the potential by a rescaling, while the marginal ModMax-type deformation translates its vacuum. We derive the resulting flow equations exactly, show that the irrelevant exact $T\bar T$-like flow takes the autonomous form $\partial_λ\mathcal L=X\,F(r)$, where the closed form kernel $F$ depends only on the ratio $r=-T^μ_μ/\sqrt{4X-Y}$ of stress-tensor invariants. We also give an explicit reconstruction of the auxiliary potential from a given kernel. Causality imposes the differential inequality $D(r)>1/(1-r)$, while a globally single-valued kernel requires the ratio $r$ to be monotonic along the auxiliary branch. We examine the relation between the evenness of the exact $T\bar T$-like flow , the $\varphi$-parity of the potential, and the analyticity of the Lagrangian in the electromagnetic invariants, including the effect of a marginal ModMax parameter. We construct the linear auxiliary and Hamiltonian formulations and show that the Hamiltonian flow is governed by the same exact $T\bar T$-like flow with the opposite sign. We also express Born's Legendre self-duality, the two characteristic cones, and the energy conditions in terms of the kernel. In particular, $r=(\varepsilon-p_\perp)/(\varepsilon+p_\perp)$ has a direct physical interpretation. Exact $T\bar T$-like flow s are obtained for generalized Born-Infeld, logarithmic, q-deformed, and no-$τ$-maximum electrodynamics.

</details>


### [143] [The one-loop QCD scale anomaly on ADE orbifolds as an index](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35166)
*Herbie Warner*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文通过twistor空间上的等变指标定理，推导了R^4/Γ上SU(N)杨-米尔斯理论的一圈尺度反常，发现其由QCD β函数和固定点处的局域贡献组成，后者继承了ALE流形的Weyl引力反常并含额外热核系数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解规范场论在 orbifold 空间上的量子反常行为，特别是尺度反常如何受几何奇点影响。

Method: 利用 twistor 空间上的 equivariant index theorem 计算 SU(N) Yang-Mills 理论在 R^4/Γ 上的一圈尺度反常。

Result: 得到一环尺度反常分解为标准 QCD β-函数与一个局域于 orbifold 固定点的项；该局域项包含来自 ALE 分辨的引力 Weyl 反常及一个非保护的热核系数。

Conclusion: orbifold 奇点处的量子反常不仅反映平滑分辨的几何反常，还引入新的、不被对称性保护的量子效应。

Abstract: We derive the one-loop scale anomaly of $\SU(N)$ Yang--Mills on the orbifold $\R^4/Γ$, with $Γ\subset \SU(2)$ a finite subgroup, from an equivariant index theorem on twistor space. The anomaly separates into the QCD $β$-function and a contribution localized at the orbifold fixed point. This localized term inherits the gravitational Weyl anomaly of the smooth ALE resolution, which concentrates under the singular degeneration, along with an additional unprotected heat-kernel coefficient.

</details>


### [144] [On memory effects in odd spacetime dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35283)
*Ana-Maria Raclariu,Binsong Xiong*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文在所有奇数时空维度中发现了一种新的电磁记忆效应，该效应源于匀速运动带电粒子产生的电磁场，在库仑阶出现，并与Weinberg软光子定理直接相关；作者通过在大有限距离处对零超曲面上的麦克斯韦约束进行分析，给出了统一推导，并指出在奇数维中光线积分与大r展开不可交换，强调积分需在取无穷远极限前完成；还计算了高低维中无质量与有质量源的库仑阶记忆效应，阐明其与软光子定理傅里叶变换的关系，并提出可在有限时间内（远大于探测器到源的距离）测量该效应的方案；最后简要讨论向引力理论的推广。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 揭示电磁记忆效应在更高奇数维时空中的普适性及其与软定理的深层联系，弥补此前仅限于四维的研究空白，并澄清极限顺序对记忆效应存在性的影响。

Method: 在任意奇数维时空下，于大但有限距离处的零超曲面上分析麦克斯韦约束方程；严格处理光线积分与大r渐近展开的顺序问题；结合动量空间分析，建立记忆效应与软光子定理傅里叶变换的对应；设计基于有限时间观测的可测量性协议。

Result: 证实了所有奇数维时空均存在库仑阶电磁记忆效应；明确该效应仅在先做光线积分、再取r→∞极限时非零；给出无质量与有质量源下该效应的显式表达式；建立了其与Weinberg软定理的精确傅里叶对应关系；提出切实可行的实验测量方案。

Conclusion: 电磁记忆效应是奇数维时空的普适现象，其存在性强烈依赖于极限顺序，本质上植根于软光子定理；该工作为高维软物理与记忆效应研究提供了统一框架，并为引力记忆效应的高维推广奠定基础。

Abstract: We establish the existence of a new electromagnetic memory effect in all odd spacetime dimensions. This effect is a universal property of electromagnetic fields sourced by charged particles of constant momenta, appears at the Coulombic order and is directly related to the Weinberg soft theorem. We provide a unified derivation of this effect in all higher dimensions by evaluating the Maxwell constraint on a null plane at large, finite distance. We observe that in odd spacetime dimensions, the lightray integral computing the memory effect does not commute with the large-$r$ expansion. Our results necessarily assume that the lightray integral is taken prior to the $r \rightarrow \infty$ limit. The effect vanishes in the opposite order of limits, in agreement with previous results. We compute the Coulombic order electromagnetic memory effect for both massless and massive sources in all higher dimensions, explain its relation to the Fourier transform of the leading soft photon theorem and present a protocol for its measurement in a finite time parametrically larger than the distance of a detector from the sources. We conclude with comments on the generalization to gravity.

</details>


### [145] [Theory and Cosmology of Biverse Volume-Normalized Gravity](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35384)
*Siqing Yu*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新的引力理论——双宇宙体积归一化引力，通过将我们的四维宇宙置于五维体空间的边界上，结合体积归一化作用量与无鬼大质量引力，解决了宇宙学常数问题，并预言张量标量比为零。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决粒子物理预测的真空能密度与宇宙学观测之间高达60个数量级的宇宙学常数问题。

Method: 扩展Tseytlin的体积归一化作用量思想，将(3+1)维宇宙嵌入(4+1)维体空间边界；融合无鬼大质量引力，使第二度规自然成为体空间度规在边界的回拉，并缓解强耦合问题。

Result: 成功抑制大量子引力圈图发散，自然导出第二度规，解决强耦合问题，与已验证的粒子宇宙学模型一致，并非平凡地预言张量标量比为零。

Conclusion: 双宇宙体积归一化引力为经典与量子引力提供了新框架，在理论上自洽且具有可观测预言，是解决宇宙学常数问题的一个有前景的新途径。

Abstract: This dissertation expounds the biverse volume-normalized gravity, a novel theoretical framework of classical and quantum gravity that provides an unusual solution to the cosmological constant problem, the 60-degree-of-magnitude discrepancy between the value of the vacuum energy density in our universe predicted by particle physics and that implied by cosmological data. In the literature, there exist two interesting ideas: 1) to use the "volume-normalized" action functional, the usual action of the particle Standard Model and general relativity but divided by the spacetime volume, first proposed by Tseytlin; 2) to generate the vacuum energy density dynamically by assigning a ghost-free massive potential to the graviton. However, each has a problem: Tseytlin's attempt suffers from diverging quantum loops and the massive gravity theory has a second metric tensor with questionable origin and the quantum strong coupling problem. Inspired by these two ideas, as well as the AdS/CFT correspondence in string theory, the biverse volume-normalized gravity extends Tseytlin's idea by placing our (3+1)-dimensional universe on the boundary of a (4+1)-dimensional bulk space. This new adaptation controls the large quantum gravity loops in the limit of vanishing ratio of the spacetime volume of our universe to that of the other universe. The ghost-free massive gravity can then be seamlessly incorporated, not only that its otherwise nebulous second metric tensor appears naturally as the pullback of the bulk metric on the boundary, but its strong coupling problem also solved. The biverse volume-normalized gravity is also consistent with all tested models of particle cosmology and the evolution of the universe, and nontrivially predicts that the tensor-to-scalar ratio of our universe is exactly zero.

</details>


### [146] [The topological vertex is finitely generated](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35719)
*Kunal Gupta,Pietro Longhi*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文揭示了拓扑顶点可由三个圆盘和三个环面的边界相互作用生成，并将该结论推广至精炼拓扑顶点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解拓扑顶点的物理起源及其在开放和闭合弦振幅计算中的作用机制。

Method: 通过分析边界相互作用（三个圆盘与三个环面）推导拓扑顶点的构造，并推广至精炼情形。

Result: 证明拓扑顶点可由特定低维几何对象（圆盘与环面）的边界相互作用生成；该结论适用于普通及精炼拓扑顶点。

Conclusion: 拓扑顶点具有清晰的几何与物理起源，其结构可还原为基本边界相互作用，为理解其在弦论与拓扑弦理论中的本质提供了新视角。

Abstract: The topological vertex computes all genus amplitudes for the open topological A-model in $\mathbb{C}^3$ with branes on three toric Lagrangians and, via gluing, both open and closed string amplitudes on any toric Calabi-Yau threefold. In this note we show that the topological vertex is generated by boundary interactions of three disks and three annuli. A generalization of this result is shown to hold for the refined topological vertex.

</details>


### [147] [Compositional Operational Rank, Smooth Realizability, and Dimensional Reduction](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35764)
*Nikolay D. Tulupov,Alexander M. Tishin*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种在高维理论中保持操作独立性的维度约简框架，强调依赖结构而非单纯坐标数量的保留，并通过闭包保真性等条件确保操作秩在不同描述层级间一致。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 高维理论中微观描述维度与观测者可及方向数存在差异，需在维度约简过程中一致保持操作独立性，而现有方法常仅关注坐标数量，忽略依赖结构的保留。

Method: 建立操作秩在光滑积分叶维度和Fisher信息秩之间保持的充分条件，核心是闭包保真性；结合实可表示性、常秩、对合性及观测者/尺度兼容性，构造局部积分叶；再在测量层面引入主识别条件，要求观测图像与主积分分布局部重合，并满足协议分辨率等约束。

Result: 证明了任意有限秩下该构造均成立；当秩r=4时，给出高维理论中维度约简的条件性操作判据；并通过量子硬件负对照实验表明，相同受限Fisher秩可能掩盖不同更高秩，说明不能以四维为普适上限。

Conclusion: 该框架从操作角度约束了维度约简的结果，强调必须保持相关操作依赖结构，而非仅产出四个有效坐标，从而避免对微观态空间施加普适的四维限制。

Abstract: Higher-dimensional theories distinguish the dimensionality of a microscopic description from the number of directions accessible to a physical observer. Relating these notions requires not only dimensional reduction, but also a consistent preservation of operational independence across different levels of description.
  We establish sufficient conditions under which a compositional operational rank is preserved first as the dimension of a smooth integral leaf and then as an observable Fisher-information rank. The key requirement is closure-faithfulness: the smooth realization must preserve the dependency relations of the operational closure, rather than merely its total rank. Together with real representability, constant rank, involutivity, and observer/scale compatibility, this yields a local integral leaf whose dimension equals the operational rank.
  A further primary-identification condition is required at the measurement level. If an independently specified observer image coincides locally with the primary integral distribution and the admissible protocols provide local resolution, finite labeled-mixture closure, and locally constant outcome support, then the same rank can be realized by a finite composite Fisher-information protocol.
  The construction holds for arbitrary finite rank. At r = 4 it provides a conditional operational criterion for dimensional reduction in higher-dimensional theories: a viable reduction must preserve the relevant operational dependency structure, not merely produce four effective coordinates. Quantum-hardware negative controls further show that the same restricted Fisher rank can conceal different larger ranks under a broader pre-specified measurement protocol. The framework therefore constrains the operational outcome of dimensional reduction without imposing a universal four-dimensional bound on microscopic state spaces.

</details>


### [148] [Quantum Field Theory on the Tangent Bundle](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35778)
*Joachim Herrmann*

Main category: hep-th

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于切丛的量子场论框架，将引力与内禀规范结构分别关联于仿射切纤维几何的不同部分；通过平移部分联系到挠率引力（teleparallel gravity），并通过扩展欧氏群E^c(2)的垂直谱问题引入手征结构、弱SU(2)与色SU(3)_c对称性，成功复现夸克分数电荷；该框架统一描述粒子物理与引力几何结构，并预言额外规范玻色子与暗物质态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在统一引力（特别是挠率引力）与粒子物理标准模型的规范结构，从几何基础出发自然导出夸克分数电荷、手征性及色对称性，而非人为引入。

Method: 构建切丛上的仿射纤维几何框架，分离平移（对应coframe与teleparallel引力）与垂直（内部对称） sector；利用中心扩展欧氏群E^c(2)的投影表示构造垂直谱；引入三重夸克多重态实现局部色SU(3)_c规范对称性；通过全局商空间关联色中心与三重旋转覆盖；结合BRST量子化与反常约束分析。

Result: 成功复现标准模型中左手中微子与夸克的分数电荷分配；色SU(3)_c部门具有标准QCD结构，含单圈β函数与渐近自由；预言额外规范玻色子与暗物质候选态；提出挠率与洛伦兹扇区势之间的结构作用量。

Conclusion: 该几何框架为引力与粒子物理提供共通数学基础，但其动力学演化与实验检验仍待进一步研究。

Abstract: We develop a quantum-field-theoretical framework on the tangent bundle in which gravitational and internal gauge structures are associated with different sectors of an affine tangent-fiber geometry. The translational sector supplies the coframe and its relation to teleparallel gravity. A reduced vertical spectral problem is based on the centrally extended Euclidean group \(E^{c}(2)\), motivated by projective representations of the stabilizer of a nonzero null vector. Twofold multiplicities of equivalent vertical representations in selected left-chiral sectors admit weak \(SU(2)\) actions, while an assumed threefold quark multiplicity admits a locally gauged color \(SU(3)_c\) symmetry. A proposed global quotient correlates the color center with vertical rotations on a threefold rotation cover. Together with the selected chiral spectrum and anomaly conditions, this construction reproduces the observed fractional quark charge assignments. We formulate matter and gauge-field quantization, BRST symmetry, and anomaly constraints. Under the stated projection assumptions, the color sector has the standard local QCD structure, including its one-loop beta function and asymptotic freedom. Additional gauge bosons and dark-matter states are proposed as hypothetical sectors. We also formulate a structural action for possible couplings between teleparallel torsion and additional Lorentz-sector potentials. The framework relates selected structures of particle physics and gravity within a common geometric setting; their further dynamical and phenomenological development remains open.

</details>


### [149] [How to get an attractive model](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35798)
*Joseph McGovern*

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>

Abstract: We give a fourth-order Calabi-Yau differential operator, which seems to have been missed by earlier tabulations. We draw attention to this operator because of its clear geometric interpretation and evidence that it possesses a rank-two attractor, conjecturally giving a new example of a Calabi-Yau threefold with modularity properties analogous to those of an elliptic curve over $\mathbb{Q}$.

</details>

### [150] [Identifying Cosmic Ray Over-Flagging in STIS CCD Spectroscopic Observations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33184)
*Matthew M. Dallas,Sean Lockwood*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一种新工具ocrreject_exam()，用于快速识别因子曝光未对齐导致的宇宙射线过标记问题，并估计约31%的STIS CCD光谱数据受此影响，同时探讨了重新校准以缓解该问题的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准的宇宙射线剔除方法在子曝光未良好配准时会导致大量有效光谱数据被误标为宇宙射线，亟需一种快速识别该问题的工具。

Method: 开发并应用stistools包中的新任务ocrreject_exam()，结合自动监控网站对1997–2025年STIS CCD光谱数据进行批量分析，并评估过标记比例及再校准策略。

Result: 识别出约31%的STIS CCD光谱数据存在宇宙射线过标记问题，并验证了ocrreject_exam()在已知案例中的有效性；同时建立了自动监测平台。

Conclusion: ocrreject_exam()可高效识别STIS数据中由配准不良引发的宇宙射线过标记问题，为后续数据再处理和校准提供关键支持，显著提升光谱数据质量。

Abstract: The standard method for rejecting cosmic rays in CCD data taken by the Hubble Space Telescope's Space Telescope Imaging Spectrograph (STIS) instrument relies on combining multiple sub-exposures while flagging outliers. A known caveat of this method is that when the sub-exposures are not well registered, large portions of valid spectroscopic data can be flagged as cosmic rays. In this work we discuss the new ocrreject_exam() task in the stistools Python package designed to quickly identify datasets impacted by this misregistration-induced over-flagging. We demonstrate its effectiveness on known cases of over-flagged data, introduce a new monitoring website which automatically runs the tool on STIS CCD spectroscopic data, and analyze the tool's output on STIS CCD spectroscopic data from 1997 through 2025. From this analysis we estimate that ~31% of datasets will exhibit cosmic ray over-flagging. Lastly, we discuss various methods of recalibrating impacted data to mitigate the effects of over-flagging.

</details>


### [151] [Faint Companions to Hot Stars. Spectroscopic Detection Sensitivity and Implications for Survey Design](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33225)
*Sahar Shahaf,Hans-Walter Rix,Johanna Müller-Horn,Jaime I. Villaseñor,Tomer Shenar,Rhys Seeburger*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于傅里叶域的光谱分析方法，用于在双星系统中探测暗伴星（如黑洞），通过分析谱线的相位与对数振幅扰动来区分单星与双星模型，并在实际数据中验证了其有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在双星系统中搜寻黑洞需将暗伴星与微弱亮星区分开，而高温恒星谱线少且宽，传统方法难以分辨，因此需要新方法提升探测能力。

Method: 发展了一种傅里叶域方法：利用多普勒频移编码于傅里叶相位、谱线轮廓定义平滑振幅包络；暗伴星引入对数振幅上的振荡扰动；在一阶近似下建立关于线深比α的近似线性似然比检验模型；适用于单次或多历元数据，无需完全谱分离。

Result: 在CPD-41°7742的FEROS存档光谱中成功探测到已知He I 6678 Å伴星信号，并在He II 5411 Å获得严格非探测结果；推导出检测效率随线深比、速度分离、噪声及仪器参数的变化关系。

Conclusion: 该方法为光谱黑洞搜寻提供了可量化、可组合的检测框架，适用于SDSS-V、Gaia等大规模巡天项目，提升了对小速度分离和微弱伴星的探测能力。

Abstract: Searches for black holes in binaries must distinguish dark companions from faint luminous stars. This is challenging for hot stars, whose spectra contain few broad lines. We develop a Fourier-domain method that converts isolated, broadened lines into calibrated constraints on faint companions. Doppler shifts are encoded in the Fourier phase, while the line profile defines a smooth amplitude envelope. A faint secondary introduces an oscillatory perturbation to the log-amplitude. Expanding to first order in the secondary-to-primary line-depth ratio, $α$, yields a nearly linear model for a likelihood-ratio test against the single-star model. The method applies to single or multiple epochs without full spectral disentangling. At small velocity separation $v$, the companion signal becomes degenerate with the primary line profile, and the fractional uncertainty from the log-amplitude scales as $Δα/α\propto v^{-4}$. If the primary velocity is known, the Fourier phase provides a more favorable detection channel whose sensitivity scales as $v^{-1}$. We demonstrate the method on archival FEROS spectra of CPD$-41^\circ$7742, detecting the known secondary in He I 6678 Angstrom and obtaining a stringent non-detection in He II 5411 Angstrom, where its contribution is expected to be negligible. The framework yields detection efficiencies as functions of line-depth ratio, velocity separation, noise, and instrumental setup. These can be combined across lines, epochs, and targets to construct selection functions for spectroscopic black-hole searches, including those enabled by SDSS-V and Gaia.

</details>


### [152] [O Mirror, Where Art Thou?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.33942)
*Sai Krishanth P. M.,Sanchit Sabhlok,Jaren N. Ashcraft,Kyle Van Gorkom,Ewan S. Douglas*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于多视场PSF测量和贝叶斯推断的望远镜镜面失调反演方法，利用嵌套采样与差分进化算法，在Hubble和TMA模型上验证了其直接感知光学系统状态并实现自动对准的能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 波前传感可发现望远镜失调，但无法确定具体哪一面镜移动及移动量；需从PSF数据中直接反演镜面位姿以实现闭环对准。

Method: 构建仿真框架，将多视场PSF投影到圆形贝塞尔与泽尼克基上，建立场相关的多元高斯似然函数，结合嵌套采样（nested sampling）与差分进化（differential evolution）进行参数推断与优化。

Result: 在Hubble模型中，失调恢复精度达0.01微米，轴上OPD误差从2.3波降至3e-8波；在TMA模型中，恢复精度约17微米，OPD误差从8.24波降至0.002波；后验显示高维下存在参数简并。

Conclusion: 该方法具通用性，可直接感知光学系统状态，支持自动化望远镜对准。

Abstract: Wavefront sensing reveals that a telescope is misaligned, but not which mirror moved or by how much; inferring the mirror positions themselves turns alignment into a direct correction. We describe a simulation framework for inferring telescope mirror misalignment from multi-field Point Spread Function (PSF) measurements projected onto Circular-Bessel and Zernike basis vectors. Two case studies are presented: a Hubble Space Telescope model with five secondary-mirror degrees of freedom and a three-mirror anastigmat with ten primary and secondary mirror degrees of freedom. In both cases, field-dependent multivariate Gaussian likelihoods are explored with nested sampling and refined with differential evolution. Recovered states are validated with residual PSF images and optical path difference (OPD) measurements. In the Hubble benchmark, differential evolution recovers injected misalignments to less than 0.01 microns and reduces on-axis OPD error from 2.3 waves to 3 x 10E-8 waves. In the TMA study, the posterior shows broad marginals and correlated parameter pairs that suggest partial degeneracies at higher dimension; differential evolution recovers the truth alignment state less precisely than in the Hubble benchmark (less than 17 microns) and reduces on-axis OPD error from 8.24 waves to 2 x 10E-3 waves. This generalizable algorithm enables direct sensing of the state of an optical system and automated alignment.

</details>


### [153] [Atmospheric Turbulence Profiling in the Lenghu Site Area Using MERRA-2 Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34030)
*Chen Yang,Licai Deng,Kun Wang,Fei He,Fan Yang,Chunguang Zhang,Xiaodian Chen,Ruiyue Li,Linqing Tian*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文利用MERRA-2再分析数据研究青藏高原北缘冷湖天文台址及其周边大气湍流剖面，评估其在自适应光学（AO）应用中的适用性；结果表明MERRA-2适用于台址间相对比较和气候学表征，但不适用于定量AO设计或逐夜观测评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为支持冷湖4米级以上望远镜项目中自适应光学系统的规划与设计，需深入理解该台址上空大气湍流的分布与变化规律，而卫星再分析数据可与实地测量互补，用于优化台址规划。

Method: 采用MERRA-2再分析数据研究冷湖台址及周边大气湍流剖面的季节性和长期变化，并与实地地面仪器测量结果进行交叉验证。

Result: MERRA-2高估自由大气视宁度中位数+0.22角秒（+50%），夜际相关性弱（r~0.2）；低估总视宁度中位数−0.24角秒（−27%）；仅捕捉约22%的地层湍流，且几乎无法再现自由大气湍流的观测季节振幅。

Conclusion: MERRA-2数据适用于不同候选台址间的空间差异相对比较和气候学特征描述，但不适用于自适应光学系统的定量设计或逐夜大气剖面评估。

Abstract: The Lenghu site, in the Saishiteng Mountains of the northern Tibetan Plateau, has been identified as an excellent astronomical site from preliminary statistics of clear nights, sky background brightness, precipitable water vapour, total seeing and weather. Since 2022, several projects with apertures larger than 4 m have started construction or are being planned. Given the use of adaptive optics (AO) on these projects, in-depth measurements and analysis of the atmospheric turbulence above the site are required: the distribution and variation of the turbulence profiles provide a large-scale, climatological basis for site planning and for comparing candidate locations, complementing rather than replacing the on-site measurements needed for instrument design and operations. Predicting the atmospheric profiles of the site and its surroundings from satellite data, combined with on-site measurements, can help to optimise the site planning. This paper uses MERRA-2 reanalysis data to study the atmospheric profiles of the site and its environment, revealing the seasonal and long-term variations of the observing conditions. The reanalysis results are cross-checked against on-site ground-level instruments serving the same purpose. MERRA-2 overestimates the median free-atmosphere seeing by +0.22 arcsec (+50 %) while keeping the night-to-night correlation weak (r ~ 0.2), and underestimates the median total seeing by -0.24 arcsec (-27 %). It captures only ~22 % of the ground-layer turbulence and little of the observed seasonal amplitude of the free atmosphere. MERRA-2 is therefore suited to relative comparisons of spatial variability between locations and to climatological characterisation, not to quantitative AO applications or night-by-night assessment of profiles.

</details>


### [154] [Automated, auditable dating of the Babylonian Astronomical Diaries with language models and astronomical tools](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34297)
*Lucas Carrit Delgado Pinheiro,Yuan-Sen Ting,Jon Clindaniel,Lu Li,Yingbin Liang,Ness Shroff*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自动化系统，利用巴比伦天文日记中的天象记录，通过规则解析、语言模型补全和天文计算，在公元前626年至公元75年共701年间对残缺日期的泥板进行年代推断，准确率达96%，并为未定年份的56条记录提供了候选年份。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多巴比伦天文日记泥板残缺、缺失日期公式，传统依赖专家逐条人工比对天象与历史信息进行断代，费时且难以规模化；本文旨在实现天文断代部分的自动化与可复现性。

Method: 构建规则解析器将观测文本转为结构化天文子句；用语言模型校验并补充遗漏观测；调用天文工具在701年时间窗口内遍历验证各年份与子句的吻合度，并按匹配度排序；全程盲测（不预先输入已知发表日期）；结果附带原始文本段落与对应计算位置。

Result: 在120块已有公认年代的泥板测试中：96%的案例中正确年份位列排名首位；84块（占86块单一年份顶级结果）完全匹配；119块的候选年份集合包含正确年份；对ADART IV中56条未定年份条目给出排名候选年份；提供交互式网页展示全流程与代码。

Conclusion: 该系统证明仅凭天文记录即可高精度约束巴比伦泥板年代，显著提升断代效率与透明度，为古代天文史料的数字化考证提供了可扩展方法论。

Abstract: The Babylonian Astronomical Diaries record the sky over nearly six centuries. Many tablets are broken and have lost their date formula. Experts dated many of them one tablet at a time in the standard edition, Astronomical Diaries and Related Texts from Babylonia (ADART). They used the recorded sky and other evidence, such as year formulas and royal names. Our system automates the astronomical part for all tablets with the same rules. A rule-based parser turns the recorded observations into clauses, short statements with fixed fields. A language model checks them against the text and adds observations that the parser missed. Astronomical tools test the clauses in all 701 years from 626 BCE to AD 75. The system ranks the years by how well they agree with the clauses. The system withholds published dates until its results are final, and the output lists the passages and computed positions behind each candidate year. On 120 dated tablets, the years tied at the top of the ranking include the published year in 96% of cases. For 86 of them, this top group is a single year, the published year in 84. In 119 of the 120, the reported candidate set contains it. For 56 undated entries of ADART IV, the system gives ranked candidate years. For each tablet, the results show how far astronomy alone narrows the year. An interactive page at https://tingyuansen.github.io/Babylonian_Dating/ follows each tablet and links the code, protocol and results.

</details>


### [155] [Reproducibility, Methodological Robustness, and Synthetic-Domain Selection Properties of AFINO for TESS Stellar-Flare QPP Analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34627)
*Marc Balboa Corominas*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文评估了AFINO方法在TESS数据中探测恒星耀斑准周期脉动（QPP）的可重复性、方法鲁棒性及在合成数据上的表现，发现其在真实观测中复现率低，而在合成数据中选择敏感度有限但特异性高，结果仅限于方法学层面，未达成观测验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: QPP探测结果易受方法选择影响，需明确区分可重复性、鲁棒性与已知真值下的性能，以支撑可信评估。

Method: 采用冻结版公开AFINO实现，在TESS背景下系统测试其可重复性、方法鲁棒性、数值行为及合成真值选择特性；开展观测初试（10个事件）、大规模稳健性实验（61个已发表QPP参考），并独立构建合成数据集评估AFINO 0.5规则在HELDOUT集上的选择与周期恢复性能。

Result: 冻结基线成功复现5个已发表TESS QPP检测；大规模稳健性实验中仅51/61个参考保持一致，故稳健性结论依赖于基线一致的子集；合成测试中AFINO 0.5规则检出152/1800阳性样本，0/1800阴性误报，获得156层选择曲面及周期恢复摘要。

Conclusion: 结果提供了对AFINO方法在特定冻结配置下稳健性与合成域选择行为的有界刻画，但不构成观测验证，亦不支持种群级校正或普适性结论。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic pulsation (QPP) searches in stellar flares depend on methodological choices that can affect both classification and the interpretation of recovered periods. A defensible assessment therefore requires explicit separation of reproducibility, robustness, and known-truth performance. We characterize the reproducibility, methodological robustness, numerical behavior, and synthetic-ground-truth selection properties of a frozen public AFINO implementation in the TESS context while keeping observational reference evidence separate from known synthetic truth. The frozen baseline reproduced five published TESS detections in the initial reproduction cohort. A ten-event observational pilot and a catalogue-scale robustness experiment then tested prospectively specified temporal-window and processing perturbations. At catalogue scale, 51 of 61 published-QPP references did not reproduce their frozen reference state under the F3A baseline, so robustness claims were conditioned on baseline-concordant references rather than interpreted as accuracy. In a separately developed synthetic programme, the untouched AFINO 0.5 rule was evaluated once on an independent HELDOUT set: 152 of 1800 synthetic positives were selected and no false selections were observed among 1800 synthetic nulls, with finite Wilson uncertainty. The resulting 156-stratum selection surface and period-recovery summaries remain conditional on the frozen synthetic domain and, for period recovery, on selection; they therefore provide a bounded characterization of methodological robustness and synthetic-domain selection behavior without establishing observational validation or a population-level correction.

</details>


### [156] [Data-driven acquisition offset correction for telescope networks using machine learning and sensitivity analysis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34719)
*Jackson Zariski,Kaitlin Kratter,Mark Bowman,Nandini Dharwadkar*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于机器学习的数据驱动流水线，利用历史观测日志自动预测和校正望远镜目标获取偏移，提升机器人望远镜网络的观测效率，并提供开源、容器化的实时服务工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有望远镜目标获取依赖静态指向模型和人工调整，难以适应望远镜配置、环境条件、仪器状态等时变因素，影响观测效率。

Method: 构建机器学习流水线，结合传统机器学习与深度学习回归模型，从Las Cumbres Observatory历史运行日志中学习获取偏移与多维观测/望远镜/仪器参数之间的关系；开发开源、Docker化、带Web界面的服务系统。

Result: 实现了对望远镜获取偏移的有效预测与校正，可补充现有指向模型，减少人工干预；部署了便携、实时可用的软件服务。

Conclusion: 该方法为望远镜目标获取提供了更自适应、自动化和数据驱动的实用解决方案，适用于多站点、多仪器的机器人望远镜网络。

Abstract: Reliable target acquisition is essential for maximizing observing efficiency, particularly for robotic telescope networks that operate across many sites, instruments, and environmental conditions. Although these facilities generate large volumes of operational and observational data, acquisition procedures often continue to rely on static pointing models and manual adjustments by observatory staff. These approaches can be effective, but they may not fully capture changing behavior associated with telescope configuration, observing conditions, instrument state, or other time-dependent effects. We present a data-driven machine-learning pipeline for automatically predicting and correcting telescope acquisition offsets, demonstrated using archival operational logs from the Las Cumbres Observatory network. Our approach applies both traditional machine-learning methods and deep-learning regression models to learn relationships between measured acquisition offsets and a range of observing, telescope, and instrumentation conditions. By using historical performance data, the pipeline is designed to complement existing pointing models and reduce the need for repeated manual correction. We also present an open-source, Dockerized software service that makes the trained models accessible through a browser-based graphical interface. The service is intended to support straightforward, real-time use by observatory staff while remaining portable across computing environments. Together, the prediction pipeline and deployment framework provide a practical path toward more adaptive, automated, and data-informed telescope acquisition.

</details>


### [157] [Experimental Validation of Double-Blind Fourier Holography in an HWO-Like Segmented Aperture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34760)
*Oren Pedatzur,Noah Schwartz,D. Scott Acton,Ben Buky*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次实验验证了双盲傅里叶全息术（DBFH）在分段式空间望远镜波前传感中的可行性，结果表明其精度与传统FDPR方法相当，并能处理FDPR难以应对的双星源场景，技术成熟度达3级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 解决未来分段式孔径空间望远镜（如宜居世界天文台HWO）的波前传感难题，克服传统方法在复杂场景（如双星源）下的局限性。

Method: 采用空间光调制器和类HWO EAC5分段孔径结构开展实验，利用精心设计的瞳面振幅掩模及其对应焦面强度测量，构建线性反演问题以重建复瞳面场，并与聚焦多样性相位恢复（FDPR）方法对比。

Result: DBFH成功重建了伪随机波前，精度与FDPR相当；首次实验证明其可在二元光源存在下进行波前传感，而FDPR无法自然处理该情形。

Conclusion: DBFH具备计算高效、稳定、适用场景更广等优势，实验验证了其作为分段主镜空间望远镜波前传感方案的可行性，技术就绪水平达到3级。

Abstract: Double-Blind Fourier Holography (DBFH) is a phase-retrieval method that has been shown numerically to address wavefront-sensing challenges for future segmented-aperture space telescopes, including the Habitable Worlds Observatory (HWO). DBFH uses a set of carefully selected pupil-plane amplitude masks and their corresponding focal-plane intensity measurements to construct an algebraically linear inverse problem, enabling computationally efficient and stable reconstruction of the complex pupil field. Here, we present the first experimental demonstration of DBFH using a spatial light modulator and an HWO EAC5-like segmented-aperture geometry. We reconstruct challenging pseudo-random wavefronts and compare DBFH with the established Focus-Diverse Phase Retrieval (FDPR) method. The two methods achieve comparable reconstruction precision, while exhibiting distinct strengths and limitations. We further demonstrate DBFH wavefront sensing in the presence of a binary source, a scenario that is not naturally accommodated by conventional FDPR. These results provide experimental proof of concept and establish technology readiness level 3 for DBFH as a wavefront-sensing method for space telescopes with segmented primary mirrors.

</details>


### [158] [GOWC: an open tool for observing-conditions prediction and observation planning, tested against site monitors and an exposure-time calculator](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.34940)
*Ahzam Ahmed*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: GOWC是一个开源网络应用，用于根据全球2642个观测站的实时气象数据（来自Open-Meteo）预测天文观测条件（如视宁度、天光亮度、信噪比等），并评估其预报精度；结果表明其信噪比计算高度准确，水汽预报偏差较大，视宁度预报在夜际排序上有一定技能但绝对标度不可靠，因此系统明确区分显示实测、校准和未验证的数据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为天文观测者提供一个统一、开放、基于实时气象数据的全球观测条件预报工具，支持从目标选择到曝光计划制定的全流程决策，并对各项预报量进行独立验证以建立可信度。

Method: 基于Open-Meteo提供的实时气象数据（含气压层廓线），计算视宁度（通过Tatarski C_n^2链积分并用ESO MASS校准）、天顶距、消光、可降水汽含量、天光亮度和信噪比；与多个独立实测数据集（如ING SIGNAL、Paranal LHATPRO、ESO DIMM、MASS）进行统计对比（相关系数ρ、中位偏差等）。

Result: SNR计算与ING SIGNAL工具中位偏差仅1.9%；PWV预报较LHATPRO高43%，相关性高（ρ=+0.95）；视宁度预报夜际排序能力弱但显著（ρ=+0.24@Paranal），自由大气项与MASS相关性达ρ=+0.35；表面风和持续性预报在排序上表现更好；但在未参与校准的站点上，视宁度绝对标度无改进。

Conclusion: GOWC是一个实用且透明的观测条件规划工具，但其视宁度预报仅宜作相对指示而非绝对定量依据；系统明确标注各输出项的数据来源与验证状态（实测/校准/未验证），强调结果的适用边界与不确定性。

Abstract: GOWC (Global Observing-conditions Weather Catalogue) is an open web application that takes the user from a target to an exposure plan across 2,642 catalogued observatory sites. It computes seeing, airmass, extinction, precipitable water vapour, sky brightness and signal-to-noise from live Open-Meteo meteorology, including pressure-level profiles, and tests most of these outputs against an independent reference. The photometric SNR calculator, run with GOWC's own catalogue entries, reproduces the ING SIGNAL exposure-time calculator to a median 1.9 per cent over a 16-point magnitude-airmass grid on two instruments. Precipitable water vapour tracks the Paranal LHATPRO radiometer closely ($ρ=+0.95$, 606 nights) with a +43 per cent wet bias. Seeing is computed from the Tatarski $C_n^2$ chain integrated over the pressure levels above the site and calibrated to the ESO MASS free-atmosphere median at Paranal; matching it requires an effective Tatarski coefficient of 5.7, nearly twice the upper end of the published range, which quantifies how much turbulence four coarse pressure levels fail to resolve. Against ~20 months of ESO DIMM data the seeing forecast has a weak but significant night-to-night ranking skill ($ρ=+0.24$, 575 nights, at Paranal; +0.16 at La Silla). Surface wind alone does as well or better (+0.27, +0.41) and persistence does better (+0.42, +0.47), but the free-atmosphere term tracks MASS ($ρ=+0.35$) and carries information that surface wind does not (partial $ρ=+0.28$, +0.40). At held-out sites the absolute seeing scale is no better than a constant. GOWC therefore treats forecast seeing as indicative, shows measured values wherever a public site monitor exists, and states which outputs are measured, calibrated or unvalidated.

</details>


### [159] [Comparison of Hybrid and Standard Reconstruction Methods on IXPE Astrophysical Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35087)
*Riccardo Ferrazzoli,Nicolò Cibrario Stefano Silvestri,Michela Negro,Paolo Soffitta,Sudip Chakraborty,Simone Pagliarella,Steven R. Ehlert*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文首次系统比较了混合重建算法与标准重建算法在IXPE天文数据上的性能，发现混合方法在Her X-1等源中显著提升探测信噪比（最高达18%），且不引入偏振测量偏差；虽在低能段显现CNN放大的网格伪影，但不影响关键偏振参数，验证了其在IXPE科学分析中的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Cibrario等人2023年提出的混合重建算法虽在实验室数据中验证有效，但其在真实天文观测（尤其是IXPE）中的系统性性能尚未评估，亟需量化其对偏振测量灵敏度的实际增益及潜在系统误差。

Method: 基于IXPE观测的两个典型点源——强偏振脉冲星Her X-1和非偏振低质量X射线双星XTE J1701-461，从两种重建流程生成的Level-2事件文件出发，对比归一化Stokes参数、偏振度（PD）、偏振角（PA）及探测显著性；并采用傅里叶分析检验偏振角分布中的周期性伪影。

Result: 混合重建在Her X-1中全能量段提升显著性，2.83–4.00 keV和5.66–8.00 keV波段信噪比增益分别达9.3σ和6.2σ（R=1.18±0.03）；XTE J1701-461中PD≈0且R≈1，验证无系统偏差；低能段出现m=3,6,12,15的六边形网格相关角度伪影（>7σ），但因正交于偏振模式，未影响Stokes参数。

Conclusion: 混合重建算法在IXPE真实天文数据中实现了统计显著的灵敏度提升（等效约20%有效面积增益），伪影可控且不影响核心偏振测量，支持其在IXPE后续科学分析中系统性应用。

Abstract: The hybrid reconstruction algorithm developed by Cibrario et al. 2023 combined a convolutional neural network (CNN) with standard moment analysis to improve track reconstruction in IXPE Gas Pixel Detectors. Although validated on laboratory data (Cibrario et al. 2025), the method's astrophysical performance had not been systematically characterized. We present the first comparison of hybrid and standard reconstruction on IXPE point sources, measuring sensitivity gains versus energy. We analyze Her X-1, a bright, polarized accreting pulsar as a benchmark, and soft-state XTE J1701-461, a bright neutron-star low-mass X-ray binary with no detected polarization, to assess residual systematics. From both pipelines' Level-2 event files, we compare normalized Stokes parameters, polarization degree (PD) and angle (PA), and detection significance. For Her X-1, hybrid reconstruction increases significance at all energies, significantly above ~2.83 keV. R = σ_hyb/σ_std differs from unity by 9.3σ in the 2.83-4.00 keV bin and reaches 1.18\pm0.03 at 5.66-8.00 keV (6.2σ). For XTE J1701-461, PD is consistent with zero and R with unity. Fourier analysis reveals excess power in hybrid angle distributions at low energies for modes m = 3, 6, 12 and 15, significant at >7σ relative to the standard method; at 5-8 keV, all modes are consistent with noise in both methods. These CNN-amplified hexagonal-grid artifacts do not affect Stokes parameters because they are orthogonal to polarization modes.The method delivers a statistically significant sensitivity gain for Her X-1, consistent with Cibrario et al. 2025 and equivalent to ~20% more effective area without extra observing time. These results support systematic use of hybrid reconstruction on IXPE astrophysical data.

</details>


### [160] [Space Electric and Magnetic Sensor (SEAMS) Phase I: A Pathfinder Mission for Low-Frequency Radio Astronomy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35104)
*Atharva Kulkarni,Samruddhi Rohokale,Niketan Pawar,Raghuttam Hombal,Nikhil Navale,Aditee Joshi,Damayanti Gharpure,Subramaniam Ananthakrishnan,Janardhan Padmanabhan,Abhirup Datta*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一项名为SEAMS的空间任务，旨在进行甚低频（<16 MHz）射电天文观测，分两阶段实施：第一阶段利用印度PSLV火箭第四级在近地轨道开展射频干扰（RFI）测绘；第二阶段计划将更先进载荷送至月球轨道并着陆。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 低频电磁波段受大量人工和自然射频干扰（RFI）影响，严重制约地面低频天文观测，亟需从空间获取RFI分布图以支持低频天文研究。

Method: 第一阶段：将正交有源单极子天线与基带采样接收机载荷搭载于ISRO的PSLV火箭第四级，在LEO运行数周，记录各天线功率谱并尝试识别强天文信号；第二阶段：发射更复杂载荷绕月飞行并实现月面着陆。

Result: 提出了SEAMS任务的整体架构与两阶段技术方案，明确了第一阶段LEO RFI测绘的可行性及载荷设计要点，并规划了第二阶段月球探测的路径。

Conclusion: SEAMS任务为解决低频射电天文中的RFI难题提供了创新性空间解决方案，其分阶段实施策略兼顾科学目标与工程可行性，有望显著提升低频宇宙观测能力。

Abstract: In this paper, the Space Electric and Magnetic Sensor (SEAMS), a space based mission dedicated to very low frequency (below 16 MHz) observations of the radio sky is proposed. The SEAMS mission is planned in two phases. The first phase is specifically designed for radio frequency interference (RFI) mapping from the low earth orbit (LEO). The low frequency region of the electromagnetic spectrum is filled with a variety of artificial as well as natural RFI. Previous observations have confirmed the effect of this terrestrial interference on astronomical observations. Thus, a map of terrestrial RFI as observed from space would give valuable information to groups working in the low-frequency regime. For the first phase, the payload will be mounted on to the fourth stage of the Indian Space Research Organisation (ISRO)'s third generation Polar Satellite Launch Vehicle (PSLV), which will stay in LEO for several weeks. The payload consists of a pair of orthogonal active monopoles, a simple baseband sampling receiver optimized for recording power spectra from each antenna. The design also aims to detect strong signals of astronomical origin despite RFI. In the second phase, we intend to send a more sophisticated space payload to orbit the moon and subsequently land on its surface.

</details>


### [161] [A Congestion-Aware RDMA Front-End for LST Advanced Camera Readout](https://arxiv.org/abs/2610.35171)
*F. Marini,M. Bellato,A. Bergnoli,A. Griggio,R. Isocrate,L. Modenese,F. Montecassiano,M. Toffano*

Main category: astro-ph.IM

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于DCQCN算法的FPGA实现方案，用于解决AdvCam升级中多路10GbE数据流汇聚导致的网络拥塞问题，通过仿真、FPGA固件设计与硬件测试验证了其公平性与动态带宽调节能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: AdvCam升级中存在多路10GbE链路汇聚至单条上行链路的incast场景，传统PFC协议存在不公平性和队头阻塞问题，亟需更优的拥塞控制机制。

Method: 在原有RoCEv2 FPGA设计基础上集成DCQCN拥塞控制算法；采用ns-3进行协议级仿真；在SURF MAC中嵌入FPGA速率限制器以响应CNP；使用cocotb+Python交换机仿真测试固件；并在支持DCQCN的商用交换机上开展三发送端incast硬件实验。

Result: 成功实现DCQCN状态机驱动的FPGA速率限制器；硬件测试复现了公平分配、响应式重分配和锯齿状吞吐变化等典型DCQCN行为；内部速率限制寄存器可逐周期跟踪实测RoCEv2吞吐量。

Conclusion: DCQCN可有效替代PFC用于AdvCam高密度incast场景，其FPGA实现具备实时性、可扩展性与与商用RDMA生态的兼容性，为未来大型天文观测系统高速数据传输提供了可靠拥塞控制方案。

Abstract: The Advanced Camera (AdvCam) upgrade of the Large-Sized Telescopes (LSTs) calls for a fully digital readout in which each front-end board streams data to the event-building servers over commercial 10 Gb/s Ethernet, using an FPGA implementation of Remote Direct Memory Access (RDMA) over Converged Ethernet v2 (RoCEv2). In a previous work, a customized RoCEv2 core integrated with the SLAC SURF UDP/MAC stack was shown to sustain near-line-rate data transfers from a Xilinx KCU105 to a server equipped with a Mellanox ConnectX-5 NIC. However, the AdvCam topology is an incast funnel: many 10 GbE links converge onto a single uplink, and bare Priority Flow Control (PFC) congestion management protocol is known to be unfair and to suffer from head-of-line blocking. This work extends the previous design with the Data Center Quantized Congestion Notification (DCQCN) algorithm. A protocol-level ns-3 simulation is used to validate the DCQCN dynamics and to explore the parameter space; an FPGA rate-limiter integrated into the SURF MAC reacts to incoming Congestion Notification Packets (CNPs) according to the DCQCN state machine; a cocotb testbench with an emulated Python switch verifies the firmware end-to-end against a Soft-RoCE receiver; and a three-sender incast hardware test on a DCQCN-enabled commercial switch reproduces the canonical fair-share, reactive-redistribution, and saw-tooth behaviour expected from a commercial RDMA NIC. The internal rate-limiter register is shown to track the measured RoCEv2 throughput cycle by cycle.
